
--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/audit-licenses.ps1 ---
param(
  [string]$PackagePath = (Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent),
  [string]$SourceBundlePath,
  [switch]$IntegrityOnly,
  [string]$ReportPath
)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$packageRoot = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($PackagePath)
$failures = [Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()
$unresolved = [Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()
$verified = 0
function Resolve-AuditPath([string]$Root, [string]$RelativePath) {
  $prefix = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Root).TrimEnd('\') + '\'
  $resolved = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Join-Path $Root $RelativePath))
  if (-not $resolved.StartsWith($prefix, [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { throw 'License manifest path escapes its declared root.' }
  return $resolved
}
function Check-Hash([string]$Root, [string]$RelativePath, [string]$Expected) {
  $path = Resolve-AuditPath $Root $RelativePath
  if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $path -PathType Leaf)) { $failures.Add('Missing: ' + $RelativePath); return }
  if ($Expected -notmatch '^[a-fA-F0-9]{64}$') { $failures.Add('Invalid expected SHA-256: ' + $RelativePath); return }
  if ((Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $path -Algorithm SHA256).Hash -ine $Expected) { $failures.Add('SHA-256 mismatch: ' + $RelativePath); return }
  $script:verified++
}
function Check-Present([string]$Root, [string]$RelativePath) {
  if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Resolve-AuditPath $Root $RelativePath) -PathType Leaf)) { $failures.Add('Missing: ' + $RelativePath) }
}
Check-Present $packageRoot 'THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md'
$ffmpegRoot = Join-Path $packageRoot 'tools\ffmpeg'
$ffmpegManifestPath = Join-Path $ffmpegRoot 'provenance.json'
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ffmpegManifestPath) {
  $metadata = Get-Content -LiteralPath $ffmpegManifestPath -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
  $ffmpegMetadata = $metadata
  foreach ($entry in $metadata.binaryFiles) { if ($entry.distributed) { Check-Hash $ffmpegRoot $entry.path $entry.sha256 } }
  foreach ($entry in $metadata.licenseFiles) { Check-Hash $ffmpegRoot $entry.path $entry.sha256 }
  foreach ($entry in $metadata.noticeFiles) { Check-Hash $ffmpegRoot $entry.path $entry.sha256 }
  foreach ($entry in $metadata.sourceFiles) { Check-Hash $ffmpegRoot $entry.path $entry.sha256 }
  if (-not $metadata.sourceBundleComplete) { $unresolved.Add('FFmpeg corresponding-source/dependency audit is not marked complete; see docs/third-party-audit.md and the source manifests.') }
  if ($metadata.distributionAuditStatus -ne 'reviewed-for-distribution' -or $metadata.license -ne 'GPL-3.0-or-later') { $unresolved.Add('FFmpeg distribution license requires review: actual avformat contains GPL FFTW code despite the upstream lgpl-shared label.') }
} else { $failures.Add('Missing: tools/ffmpeg/provenance.json') }
$sevenZipMetadataPath = Join-Path $packageRoot 'licenses\7zip\provenance.json'
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $sevenZipMetadataPath)) { $sevenZipMetadataPath = Join-Path $packageRoot 'tools\7zip\provenance.json' }
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $sevenZipMetadataPath) {
  $metadata = Get-Content -LiteralPath $sevenZipMetadataPath -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
  Check-Hash $packageRoot 'tools/7zip/bin/7z.dll' $metadata.dllSha256
  $sevenZipSource = if (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $packageRoot 'licenses\7zip\7z2604-src.tar.xz')) { 'licenses/7zip/7z2604-src.tar.xz' } else { 'tools/7zip/source/7z2604-src.tar.xz' }
  Check-Hash $packageRoot $sevenZipSource $metadata.sourceSha256
  $sevenZipLicenseRoot = if (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $packageRoot 'licenses\7zip')) { 'licenses/7zip' } else { 'tools/7zip/licenses' }
  Check-Present $packageRoot ($sevenZipLicenseRoot + '/License.txt')
  Check-Present $packageRoot ($sevenZipLicenseRoot + '/copying.txt')
} else { $failures.Add('Missing: 7-Zip provenance.json') }
$imageRoot = Join-Path $packageRoot 'licenses\gilbert'
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $imageRoot)) { $imageRoot = Join-Path $packageRoot 'src\CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure\ImageObfuscationSources' }
$imageMetadataPath = Join-Path $imageRoot 'provenance.json'
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $imageMetadataPath) {
  $metadata = Get-Content -LiteralPath $imageMetadataPath -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
  if ($metadata.commit -ne '9b080a74c5e3b6fe189785c52742f83ac85ba181' -or $metadata.license -ne 'BSD-2-Clause') { $failures.Add('Unexpected image algorithm provenance/license.') }
  foreach ($entry in $metadata.files) { Check-Hash $imageRoot $entry.path $entry.sha256 }
} else { $failures.Add('Missing: image-obfuscation provenance.json') }
$dotnetRoot = Join-Path $packageRoot 'licenses\dotnet'
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $dotnetRoot)) { $dotnetRoot = Join-Path $packageRoot 'tools\dotnet\licenses' }
$dotnetMetadataPath = Join-Path $dotnetRoot 'provenance.json'
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $dotnetMetadataPath) {
  $metadata = Get-Content -LiteralPath $dotnetMetadataPath -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
  foreach ($entry in $metadata.files) { Check-Hash $dotnetRoot $entry.path $entry.sha256 }
  Check-Present $dotnetRoot 'Core-Runtime-LICENSE.txt'
  Check-Present $dotnetRoot 'Core-Runtime-THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.txt'
  Check-Present $dotnetRoot 'Windows-Desktop-Runtime-LICENSE.txt'
} else { $failures.Add('Missing: actual runtime package license provenance.json') }
if ($SourceBundlePath) {
  $sourceRoot = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($SourceBundlePath)
} else { $sourceRoot = Join-Path $ffmpegRoot 'source' }
# Bind the supplied companion to the exact source inventory recorded by this binary package.
if ($SourceBundlePath -and $ffmpegMetadata) {
  foreach ($entry in $ffmpegMetadata.sourceFiles) {
    if ($entry.path -notmatch '^source[/\\]') { throw 'FFmpeg source provenance contains an unexpected path.' }
    $sourceRelativePath = $entry.path.Substring(7)
    Check-Hash $sourceRoot $sourceRelativePath $entry.sha256
  }
}
$sourceManifest = Join-Path $sourceRoot 'dependency-source-manifest.json'
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $sourceManifest) {
  $metadata = Get-Content -LiteralPath $sourceManifest -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
  foreach ($entry in $metadata.entries) {
    if ($entry.status -eq 'source-archive-downloaded') {
      if ((Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $sourceRoot ('dependency-archives/' + $entry.archiveFile))) -or (-not $IntegrityOnly)) { Check-Hash $sourceRoot ('dependency-archives/' + $entry.archiveFile) $entry.sha256 }
    }
  }
} elseif (-not $IntegrityOnly) { $failures.Add('Missing: dependency-source-manifest.json in source companion') }
$distributionManifestPath = Join-Path $sourceRoot 'distribution-manifest.json'
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $distributionManifestPath) {
  $metadata = Get-Content -LiteralPath $distributionManifestPath -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
  if (-not $metadata.sourceBundleCompleteForReviewedInventory -or $metadata.distributionLicense -ne 'GPL-3.0-or-later') { $unresolved.Add('Reviewed source inventory/GPL aggregate distribution record is incomplete.') }
  if (-not $IntegrityOnly) {
    foreach ($entry in $metadata.requiredFiles) { Check-Hash $sourceRoot $entry.path $entry.sha256 }
  }
} elseif (-not $IntegrityOnly) { $failures.Add('Missing: source companion distribution-manifest.json') }
$report = [ordered]@{
  mode = $(if ($IntegrityOnly) { 'integrity-only; does not approve redistribution or commercial licensing' } else { 'reviewed-distribution-materials; no patent or legal-status certificate' })
  packagePath = $packageRoot; verifiedHashes = $verified
  integrityFailures = @($failures); unresolvedDistributionItems = @($unresolved)
  integrityPassed = $failures.Count -eq 0
  distributionReviewPassed = (-not $IntegrityOnly) -and $failures.Count -eq 0 -and $unresolved.Count -eq 0
}
if ($ReportPath) { $report | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 5 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $ReportPath -Encoding utf8 }
$report | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 5
if ($failures.Count -gt 0) { throw ('License/source integrity audit failed: ' + ($failures -join '; ')) }
if (-not $IntegrityOnly -and $unresolved.Count -gt 0) { throw ('Distribution review remains unresolved: ' + ($unresolved -join '; ')) }


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/bootstrap-archive-engine.ps1 ---
param([string]$ExtractorPath)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$projectDirectory = Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent
$engineDirectory = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\7zip'
$metadata = Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $engineDirectory 'provenance.json') -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
$binaryPath = Join-Path $engineDirectory 'downloads\7z2604-x64.exe'
$sourcePath = Join-Path $engineDirectory 'source\7z2604-src.tar.xz'
foreach ($directory in @((Split-Path $binaryPath -Parent),(Split-Path $sourcePath -Parent),(Join-Path $engineDirectory 'bin'),(Join-Path $engineDirectory 'licenses'))) {
  New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $directory | Out-Null
}
foreach ($download in @(@($binaryPath,$metadata.binaryUrl,$metadata.binarySha256),@($sourcePath,$metadata.sourceUrl,$metadata.sourceSha256))) {
  if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $download[0])) { Invoke-WebRequest -Uri $download[1] -OutFile $download[0] }
  if ((Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $download[0] -Algorithm SHA256).Hash -ne $download[2]) { throw ('Archive dependency checksum mismatch: ' + $download[0]) }
}
$dllPath = Join-Path $engineDirectory 'bin\7z.dll'
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $dllPath)) {
  if (-not $ExtractorPath) {
    $available7z = Get-Command 7z -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    if ($available7z) { $ExtractorPath = $available7z.Source }
    elseif (Test-Path -LiteralPath 'C:\Program Files\WinRAR\WinRAR.exe') { $ExtractorPath = 'C:\Program Files\WinRAR\WinRAR.exe' }
  }
  if (-not $ExtractorPath -or -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ExtractorPath)) { throw 'A development archive extractor is needed. Pass -ExtractorPath <7z.exe or WinRAR.exe>. The application installer is never executed.' }
  # Offset is fixed for the exact, SHA-256-verified 26.04 x64 installer.
  $installerBytes = [IO.File]::ReadAllBytes($binaryPath)
  $payloadOffset = 45568
  if ([BitConverter]::ToString($installerBytes[$payloadOffset..($payloadOffset+5)]) -ne '37-7A-BC-AF-27-1C') { throw 'Verified installer payload marker missing.' }
  $payloadPath = Join-Path $engineDirectory 'downloads\7z2604-payload.7z'
  [IO.File]::WriteAllBytes($payloadPath, $installerBytes[$payloadOffset..($installerBytes.Length-1)])
  $targetDirectory = Join-Path $engineDirectory 'bin'
  if ([IO.Path]::GetFileName($ExtractorPath) -ieq 'WinRAR.exe') {
    $extractorProcess = Start-Process -FilePath $ExtractorPath -ArgumentList @('x','-y','-inul','-ibck',('"'+$payloadPath+'"'),('"'+$targetDirectory+'\"')) -WindowStyle Hidden -PassThru
    if (-not $extractorProcess.WaitForExit(30000) -or $extractorProcess.ExitCode -ne 0) { throw 'Payload extraction failed.' }
  } else {
    & $ExtractorPath x $payloadPath ('-o'+$targetDirectory) -y -bso0
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Payload extraction failed.' }
  }
}
if ((Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $dllPath -Algorithm SHA256).Hash -ne $metadata.dllSha256) { throw 'Archive engine DLL checksum mismatch.' }
$licenseDirectory = Join-Path $engineDirectory 'licenses'
Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $engineDirectory 'bin\License.txt') -Destination (Join-Path $licenseDirectory 'License.txt') -Force
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $licenseDirectory 'copying.txt'))) {
  $unpacked = Join-Path $engineDirectory 'source\unpacked'
  New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $unpacked | Out-Null
  & (Join-Path $engineDirectory 'bin\7z.exe') x $sourcePath ('-o'+$unpacked) -y -bso0
  if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Source wrapper extraction failed.' }
  & tar -xf (Join-Path $unpacked '7z2604-src.tar') -C $unpacked
  if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Source TAR extraction failed.' }
  Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $unpacked 'DOC\copying.txt') -Destination $licenseDirectory -Force
}
Write-Output ('7-Zip 26.04 ready: ' + $dllPath)


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/bootstrap-installer.ps1 ---
param()
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$projectDirectory = Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent
$toolDirectory = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\innosetup\7.1.0'
$downloadPath = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\innosetup\innosetup-7.1.0-x64.exe'
$expectedHash = '0362a383ed217d4c4239b5933866dd96d3eb2102737da92f80f6057a4b40df2f'
$compiler = Join-Path $toolDirectory 'ISCC.exe'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path (Split-Path $downloadPath -Parent) | Out-Null
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $downloadPath) -or (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $downloadPath -Algorithm SHA256).Hash -ine $expectedHash) {
  $ProgressPreference = 'SilentlyContinue'
  Invoke-WebRequest -UseBasicParsing -Uri 'https://github.com/jrsoftware/issrc/releases/download/is-7_1_0/innosetup-7.1.0-x64.exe' -OutFile $downloadPath
}
if ((Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $downloadPath -Algorithm SHA256).Hash -ine $expectedHash) { throw 'Inno Setup 7.1.0 SHA-256 mismatch.' }
$signature = Get-AuthenticodeSignature -LiteralPath $downloadPath
if ($signature.Status -ne 'Valid' -or $signature.SignerCertificate.Subject -notmatch 'Pyrsys B\.V\.') { throw 'Inno Setup publisher signature is not valid.' }
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $compiler)) {
  # Upstream portable mode writes only this directory: no compiler shortcuts,
  # associations, uninstall registration, or changes to the user's normal install.
  $install = Start-Process -FilePath $downloadPath -ArgumentList @('/PORTABLE=1','/VERYSILENT','/SUPPRESSMSGBOXES','/NORESTART',('/DIR="' + $toolDirectory + '"')) -WindowStyle Hidden -Wait -PassThru
  if ($install.ExitCode -ne 0) { throw "Inno Setup portable extraction failed: $($install.ExitCode)" }
}
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $compiler)) { throw 'Inno Setup compiler is missing.' }
$compilerSignature = Get-AuthenticodeSignature -LiteralPath $compiler
if ($compilerSignature.Status -ne 'Valid' -or $compilerSignature.SignerCertificate.Subject -notmatch 'Pyrsys B\.V\.') { throw 'Inno Setup compiler signature is not valid.' }
[ordered]@{ version='7.1.0'; url='https://github.com/jrsoftware/issrc/releases/download/is-7_1_0/innosetup-7.1.0-x64.exe'; verification='https://jrsoftware.org/isdl-verify.php'; sha256=$expectedHash; publisher=$signature.SignerCertificate.Subject; thumbprint=$signature.SignerCertificate.Thumbprint; portable=$true } | ConvertTo-Json | Set-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $toolDirectory 'provenance.json') -Encoding utf8
Write-Output $compiler


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/bootstrap-sdk.ps1 ---
param()
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$projectDirectory = Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent
$sdkDirectory = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\dotnet\sdk'
$dotnetExecutable = Join-Path $sdkDirectory 'dotnet.exe'
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $dotnetExecutable) { & $dotnetExecutable --version; exit $LASTEXITCODE }
$downloadDirectory = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\dotnet'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $downloadDirectory | Out-Null
$archivePath = Join-Path $downloadDirectory 'sdk.zip'
$expectedHash = '24b670ad3d923bfcf47df6c3b034152398b42f6dbc388e10d783aee1cfb5e5817d399fc0ae2a12cfa822a55e61d34830ccb15c50ef6efee437ab874bb7c79430'
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $archivePath) -or (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $archivePath -Algorithm SHA512).Hash -ne $expectedHash) {
  Invoke-WebRequest -Uri 'https://builds.dotnet.microsoft.com/dotnet/Sdk/10.0.401/dotnet-sdk-10.0.401-win-x64.zip' -OutFile $archivePath
}
if ((Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $archivePath -Algorithm SHA512).Hash -ne $expectedHash) { throw 'The .NET SDK archive checksum does not match Microsoft release metadata.' }
Expand-Archive -LiteralPath $archivePath -DestinationPath $sdkDirectory -Force
& $dotnetExecutable --version
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'SDK setup failed.' }


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/build-installer.ps1 ---
param([string]$PackageDirectory, [string]$OutputDirectory, [string]$Version, [switch]$TestMode)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$projectDirectory = Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent
. (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'release-version.ps1')
$releaseInfo = Get-CatReleaseInfo -ProjectDirectory $projectDirectory -Version $Version
if (-not $PackageDirectory) { $PackageDirectory = Join-Path $projectDirectory ('release\' + $releaseInfo.PackageName) }
$PackageDirectory = (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $PackageDirectory).Path
if (-not $OutputDirectory) { $OutputDirectory = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'release' }
$OutputDirectory = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($OutputDirectory)
foreach ($file in @('你的猫咪文件助手.exe','卸载程序.exe','app\YourCatFileAssistant.dll','app\installation-files.json')) {
  if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $PackageDirectory $file))) { throw "Installer payload missing: $file" }
}
$rootFiles = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $PackageDirectory -File)
if ($rootFiles.Count -ne 2) { throw 'Installation root must contain exactly the main and uninstall launchers.' }
$rootDirectories = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $PackageDirectory -Directory)
if ($rootDirectories.Count -ne 1 -or $rootDirectories[0].Name -ne 'app') { throw 'Installation root must contain exactly one app payload directory.' }
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $PackageDirectory 'app\installer-test-mode')) { throw 'A test installation must never be repackaged as a release payload.' }
$installedFiles = Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $PackageDirectory 'app\installation-files.json') -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
if ($installedFiles.version -ne $releaseInfo.Version) { throw 'Installation manifest version differs from the requested release.' }
$manifestPaths = [Collections.Generic.HashSet[string]]::new([StringComparer]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)
foreach ($entry in $installedFiles.files) {
  $file = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Join-Path $PackageDirectory $entry.path))
  if (-not $manifestPaths.Add($file)) { throw "Duplicate installation manifest path: $($entry.path)" }
  if (-not $file.StartsWith($PackageDirectory + '\', [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase) -or -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $file -PathType Leaf) -or (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $file -Algorithm SHA256).Hash -ine $entry.sha256) { throw "Installation payload hash mismatch: $($entry.path)" }
}
$payloadEntries = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $PackageDirectory -Recurse -Force)
if (@($payloadEntries | Where-Object { $_.Attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint }).Count -gt 0) { throw 'Installer payload must not contain reparse points.' }
if (@($payloadEntries | Where-Object { -not $_.PSIsContainer }).Count -ne $manifestPaths.Count + 1) { throw 'Installer payload contains files outside its installation manifest.' }
& (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'bootstrap-installer.ps1') | Out-Null
$compiler = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\innosetup\7.1.0\ISCC.exe'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $OutputDirectory | Out-Null
$compilerArguments = @('/Qp',"/DPackageDirectory=$PackageDirectory","/DOutputDirectory=$OutputDirectory","/DAppVersion=$($releaseInfo.Version)","/DReleaseLabel=$($releaseInfo.Label)")
if ($TestMode) { $compilerArguments += '/DTestMode=1' }
$compilerArguments += (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'installer.iss')
& $compiler @compilerArguments
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Installer compilation failed.' }
$fileName = if ($TestMode) { 'CatFileAssistant-V' + $releaseInfo.Version + '-Test-Setup.exe' } else { $releaseInfo.PackageName + '-Setup.exe' }
$installer = Join-Path $OutputDirectory $fileName
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $installer)) { throw 'Compiled installer was not found.' }
$installerHash = (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $installer -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant()
[IO.File]::WriteAllText($installer + '.sha256', $installerHash + '  ' + $fileName + [Environment]::NewLine, [Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))
if ($TestMode) {
  [ordered]@{ schemaVersion=2; testMode=$true; appId='{F16F864F-D8DE-4508-93DC-8F9925D674B2}'; dataIsolation='installation-local'; shortcuts='installation-local'; shortcutRoot='app/installer-test-shortcuts'; installerSha256=$installerHash } | ConvertTo-Json | Set-Content -LiteralPath ($installer + '.test.json') -Encoding utf8
}
Get-Item -LiteralPath $installer | Select-Object FullName,Length


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/build.ps1 ---
param([switch]$RunTests,[switch]$Quick)
$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
$projectDirectory=Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent
$dotnetExecutable=Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\dotnet\sdk\dotnet.exe'
if(-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $dotnetExecutable)){
  $systemDotnet=Get-Command dotnet -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
  if(-not $systemDotnet){throw 'Restore .NET SDK 10.0.401 first; see README.'}
  $dotnetExecutable=$systemDotnet.Source
}
$env:DOTNET_ROOT=Split-Path $dotnetExecutable -Parent
$env:DOTNET_ROOT_X64=$env:DOTNET_ROOT
$env:DOTNET_CLI_HOME=Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\dotnet\home'
$env:NUGET_PACKAGES=Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\dotnet\packages'
$env:DOTNET_CLI_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT='1'
$env:DOTNET_NOLOGO='1'
& python -B (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'restore-native-dependencies.py') --verify-only
if($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0){throw 'Restore the exact native dependencies before building.'}
Push-Location $projectDirectory
try{
  $version=& $dotnetExecutable --version
  if($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0 -or $version.Trim() -ne '10.0.401'){throw 'The project requires .NET SDK 10.0.401 as pinned in global.json.'}
  & $dotnetExecutable build CatFileAssistant.sln -c Release -m:1
  if($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0){throw 'Build failed.'}
  if($RunTests){
    $testArguments=@('--root',$projectDirectory)
    if($Quick){$testArguments+='--quick'}
    & $dotnetExecutable run --project tests\CatFileAssistant.Acceptance -c Release --no-build -- @testArguments
    if($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0){throw 'Acceptance checks failed.'}
  }
}finally{Pop-Location}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/collect-ffmpeg-sources.ps1 ---
param([int]$ThrottleLimit = 6, [switch]$NoProxy)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$projectDirectory = Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent
$sourceDirectory = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\ffmpeg\source'
$buildCommit = '6c9aec5fc9a72ec3abedd1fa84db141fa18cf52b'
$buildDirectory = Join-Path $sourceDirectory ('FFmpeg-Builds-' + $buildCommit)
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $buildDirectory)) {
  & tar -xzf (Join-Path $sourceDirectory ('FFmpeg-Builds-' + $buildCommit + '.tar.gz')) -C $sourceDirectory
  if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Verified build recipe archive could not be extracted.' }
}
$archiveDirectory = Join-Path $sourceDirectory 'dependency-archives'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $archiveDirectory -Force | Out-Null
# Read literal upstream declarations. Do not execute downloaded build scripts.
$requests = foreach ($scriptFile in (Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath (Join-Path $buildDirectory 'scripts.d') -Recurse -File -Filter '*.sh')) {
  $scriptText = Get-Content -LiteralPath $scriptFile.FullName -Raw
  foreach ($repoMatch in [regex]::Matches($scriptText, '(?m)^SCRIPT_REPO(?<suffix>\d*)="(?<value>[^"\r\n]+)"')) {
    $suffix = $repoMatch.Groups['suffix'].Value
    $refMatch = [regex]::Match($scriptText, '(?m)^SCRIPT_COMMIT' + $suffix + '="(?<value>[^"\r\n]+)"')
    if (-not $refMatch.Success) { continue }
    $repo = $repoMatch.Groups['value'].Value
    $sourceRef = $refMatch.Groups['value'].Value
    $archiveUrl = $null
    if ($repo -match '^https://github\.com/(?<owner>[^/]+)/(?<name>[^/]+?)(?:\.git)?$') {
      $archiveUrl = 'https://codeload.github.com/' + $Matches.owner + '/' + $Matches.name + '/tar.gz/' + $sourceRef
    } elseif ($repo -match '^https://(?<host>gitlab[^/]*|code\.videolan\.org)/(?<path>.+?)(?:\.git)?$') {
      $archiveUrl = 'https://' + $Matches.host + '/' + $Matches.path + '/-/archive/' + $sourceRef + '/source-' + $sourceRef + '.tar.gz'
    } elseif ($repo -match '^https://(?<host>[^/]+\.googlesource\.com)/(?<path>.+)$') {
      $archiveUrl = $repo.TrimEnd('/') + '/+archive/' + $sourceRef + '.tar.gz'
    }
    $safeId = ($scriptFile.FullName.Substring((Join-Path $buildDirectory 'scripts.d').Length + 1) -replace '\.sh$', '' -replace '[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]', '-') + $(if ($suffix) { '-' + $suffix } else { '' })
    [pscustomobject]@{
      name = $safeId; recipePath = $scriptFile.FullName.Substring($buildDirectory.Length + 1).Replace('\','/')
      repository = $repo; sourceRef = $sourceRef; archiveUrl = $archiveUrl
      archivePath = (Join-Path $archiveDirectory ($safeId + '-' + $sourceRef + '.tar.gz'))
      needsSubmoduleReview = $scriptText -match 'submodule'
      needsCargoReview = $scriptText -match '\bcargo\b'
      mutatesCargoLock = $scriptText -match 'cargo update'
      declaresAdditionalDownloadCommands = $scriptText -match 'ffbuild_dockerdl\(\)'
    }
  }
}
$results = @($requests | ForEach-Object -Parallel {
  $request = $_
  $item = [ordered]@{ name=$request.name; recipePath=$request.recipePath; repository=$request.repository; sourceRef=$request.sourceRef; sourceRefKind=$(if ($request.sourceRef -match '^[a-f0-9]{40}$') {'commit'} else {'upstream-tag'}); archiveUrl=$request.archiveUrl; archiveFile=[IO.Path]::GetFileName($request.archivePath); sha256=$null; status='unavailable'; error=$null; needsSubmoduleReview=$request.needsSubmoduleReview; needsCargoReview=$request.needsCargoReview; mutatesCargoLock=$request.mutatesCargoLock; declaresAdditionalDownloadCommands=$request.declaresAdditionalDownloadCommands; sourceCorrespondence='recipe-declared; binary build cache/lock evidence remains required' }
  try {
    if (-not $request.archiveUrl) { throw 'No archive URL inferred; use the exact upstream repository and commit with git.' }
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $request.archivePath)) {
      $arguments = @{Uri=$request.archiveUrl;OutFile=($request.archivePath + '.partial');TimeoutSec=100;ErrorAction='Stop'}
      if ($using:NoProxy) { $arguments.NoProxy = $true }
      Invoke-WebRequest @arguments
      & tar -tzf ($request.archivePath + '.partial') *> $null
      if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Response is not a valid source TAR.GZ archive.' }
      Move-Item -LiteralPath ($request.archivePath + '.partial') -Destination $request.archivePath -Force
    }
    & tar -tzf $request.archivePath *> $null
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Existing archive is not a valid source TAR.GZ archive.' }
    $item.sha256 = (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $request.archivePath -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant()
    $item.status = 'source-archive-downloaded'
  } catch { $item.error = $_.Exception.Message }
  [pscustomobject]$item
} -ThrottleLimit $ThrottleLimit)
$manifest = [ordered]@{ formatVersion=1; collectedAt='2026-10-12'; buildRecipeCommit=$buildCommit; purpose='Source acquisition evidence, including disabled/build-only stages; this is not a binary SBOM or a compliance certificate.'; sourceBundleComplete=$false; entries=@($results | Sort-Object recipePath,name) }
$manifest | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 7 | Set-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $sourceDirectory 'dependency-source-manifest.json') -Encoding utf8
$results | Group-Object status | Select-Object Name,Count
$results | Where-Object status -eq 'unavailable' | Select-Object name,repository,sourceRef,error

--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/export-design-assets.ps1 ---
param([string]$DestinationDirectory = 'artifacts\design-assets\cat2d')
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
Set-StrictMode -Version Latest
$projectDirectory = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent)).TrimEnd('\', '/')
$workspacePrefix = $projectDirectory + [IO.Path]::DirectorySeparatorChar

function Get-WorkspacePath([string]$Path) {
  if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Path)) { throw 'Asset path must not be empty.' }
  $candidate = if ([IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($Path)) { $Path } else { Join-Path $projectDirectory $Path }
  $absolute = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($candidate)
  if (-not $absolute.StartsWith($workspacePrefix, [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) {
    throw "Asset path escaped the workspace: $Path"
  }
  # Reject junctions and symbolic links so a workspace-looking path cannot copy outside it.
  $cursor = $absolute
  while ($cursor.StartsWith($workspacePrefix, [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) {
    $item = Get-Item -LiteralPath $cursor -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    if ($null -ne $item) {
      if (($item.Attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -ne 0) {
        throw "Asset path passes through a junction or symbolic link: $cursor"
      }
    }
    $cursor = Split-Path $cursor -Parent
  }
  return $absolute
}

$destination = Get-WorkspacePath $DestinationDirectory
$iconSource = Get-WorkspacePath 'src\CatFileAssistant.Desktop\Controls\FunctionalIcon.cs'
$iconCode = [IO.File]::ReadAllText($iconSource)
$enumMatch = [regex]::Match($iconCode, 'public\s+enum\s+FunctionalIconKind\s*\{(?<Kinds>[^}]+)\}')
if (-not $enumMatch.Success) { throw 'FunctionalIconKind enum was not found.' }
$kinds = @($enumMatch.Groups['Kinds'].Value -split '[,\s]+' | Where-Object { $_ })
$pathMatches = [regex]::Matches($iconCode, '\[FunctionalIconKind\.(?<Kind>\w+)\]\s*=\s*"(?<Path>[^"]+)"')
if ($kinds.Count -lt 18 -or $pathMatches.Count -ne $kinds.Count -or @($kinds | Select-Object -Unique).Count -ne $kinds.Count) {
  throw 'Every functional icon kind must have exactly one unique path.'
}

$svgs = [ordered]@{}
foreach ($match in $pathMatches) {
  $kind = $match.Groups['Kind'].Value
  $path = $match.Groups['Path'].Value
  if ($kind -notin $kinds -or $svgs.Contains($kind)) { throw "Unknown or duplicate icon kind: $kind" }
  # These source paths use the common WPF/SVG path commands; preserve their exact text.
  if ($path -notmatch '^\s*[Mm]' -or $path -match '[^MmLlHhVvCcSsQqTtAaZz0-9.,+\-\s]') {
    throw "Icon path is not compatible with SVG: $kind"
  }
  $escapedPath = [Security.SecurityElement]::Escape($path)
  $svg = '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="' + $escapedPath + '"/></svg>'
  $document = [xml]$svg
  $root = $document.DocumentElement
  if ($root.LocalName -ne 'svg' -or $root.NamespaceURI -ne 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg' -or
      $root.GetAttribute('viewBox') -ne '0 0 24 24' -or $root.ChildNodes.Count -ne 1 -or
      $root.FirstChild.GetAttribute('d') -cne $path) {
    throw "SVG validation failed: $kind"
  }
  $svgs[$kind] = $svg
}
foreach ($kind in $kinds) {
  if (-not $svgs.Contains($kind)) { throw "Functional icon path missing: $kind" }
}

$copies = [ordered]@{
  'cat\cat2d-idle.png' = 'src\CatFileAssistant.Desktop\Assets\Cat\cat2d-idle.png'
  'cat\cat2d-closed.png' = 'src\CatFileAssistant.Desktop\Assets\Cat\cat2d-closed.png'
  'cat\cat2d-sleep.png' = 'src\CatFileAssistant.Desktop\Assets\Cat\cat2d-sleep.png'
  'app.ico' = 'src\CatFileAssistant.Desktop\Assets\app.ico'
  'app.png' = 'src\CatFileAssistant.Desktop\Assets\app.png'
  'brand\icon-master.png' = 'src\CatFileAssistant.Desktop\Assets\Brand\icon-master.png'
  'brand\icon-small.svg' = 'src\CatFileAssistant.Desktop\Assets\Brand\icon-small.svg'
  'brand\cat2d-generation-prompts.json' = 'src\CatFileAssistant.Desktop\Assets\Brand\cat2d-generation-prompts.json'
  'shared\design-tokens.json' = 'shared\design-tokens.json'
  'docs\motion-design.md' = 'docs\motion-design.md'
  'docs\desktop-pet.md' = 'docs\desktop-pet.md'
}
$petAssetRoot = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'src\CatFileAssistant.Desktop\Assets\Pet'
foreach ($petAsset in (Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $petAssetRoot -File -Recurse)) {
  $petRelative = $petAsset.FullName.Substring($petAssetRoot.Length + 1)
  $copies['pet\' + $petRelative] = 'src\CatFileAssistant.Desktop\Assets\Pet\' + $petRelative
}
foreach ($size in @(16, 20, 24, 32, 40, 48, 64, 128, 256)) {
  $copies['brand\icon-' + $size + '.png'] = 'src\CatFileAssistant.Desktop\Assets\Brand\icon-' + $size + '.png'
}

# Validate every input and output before creating directories or copying any assets.
$validatedCopies = @()
foreach ($relative in $copies.Keys) {
  $source = Get-WorkspacePath $copies[$relative]
  $target = Get-WorkspacePath (Join-Path $destination $relative)
  if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $source -PathType Leaf)) { throw "Design asset missing: $source" }
  $validatedCopies += [pscustomobject]@{ Source = $source; Target = $target }
}
$svgOutputs = @()
foreach ($kind in $svgs.Keys) {
  $name = ([regex]::Replace($kind, '([a-z0-9])([A-Z])', '$1-$2')).ToLowerInvariant() + '.svg'
  $target = Get-WorkspacePath (Join-Path $destination ('functional-icons\' + $name))
  $svgOutputs += [pscustomobject]@{ Target = $target; Content = $svgs[$kind] }
}
foreach ($copy in $validatedCopies) {
  New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path (Split-Path $copy.Target -Parent) | Out-Null
  Copy-Item -LiteralPath $copy.Source -Destination $copy.Target -Force
}
$utf8 = [Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false)
foreach ($svg in $svgOutputs) {
  New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path (Split-Path $svg.Target -Parent) | Out-Null
  [IO.File]::WriteAllText($svg.Target, $svg.Content + [Environment]::NewLine, $utf8)
  # Also parse the actual file, not only the in-memory SVG template.
  $document = [xml]([IO.File]::ReadAllText($svg.Target))
  if ($document.DocumentElement.NamespaceURI -ne 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg') {
    throw "Exported SVG validation failed: $($svg.Target)"
  }
}
Write-Output "Exported $($validatedCopies.Count) design files and $($svgOutputs.Count) functional SVG icons to $destination"


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/finalize-license-manifests.py ---
"""Freeze the reviewed V0.7 source and license inventory after acquisition.

This is an artifact-integrity inventory, not a license classifier or legal
clearance tool. The GPL aggregate choice is the reviewed V0.7 distribution
policy; original component notices are preserved separately.
"""
import hashlib
import io
import json
from pathlib import Path
import tarfile
import zipfile

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
FF = ROOT / 'tools/ffmpeg'
SOURCE = FF / 'source'


def digest(path):
    return hashlib.file_digest(path.open('rb'), 'sha256').hexdigest()


def read(path):
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))


def write(path, value):
    path.write_text(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')


def main():
    # SourceForge's official revision snapshot preserves the SVN source which
    # does not have a Git SCRIPT_COMMIT declaration.
    lame_zip = SOURCE / 'dependency-archives/extra-lame-svn-r6835.zip'
    if digest(lame_zip) != '6f27a6074ea767d867b60638bd29455fd879be9f73a2c7625de92cab360cd3ce':
        raise ValueError('LAME SVN revision snapshot differs from reviewed bytes')
    lame_tar = SOURCE / 'dependency-archives/extra-lame-svn-r6835.tar.gz'
    if not lame_tar.exists():
        with zipfile.ZipFile(lame_zip) as src, tarfile.open(lame_tar, 'w:gz') as dst:
            for member in src.infolist():
                if member.is_dir():
                    continue
                data = src.read(member)
                entry = tarfile.TarInfo(member.filename)
                entry.size = len(data)
                entry.mode = 0o644
                dst.addfile(entry, io.BytesIO(data))
    extras = read(SOURCE / 'extra-source-manifest.json')
    if not any(e['name'] == 'lame-svn' for e in extras):
        extras.append({'name': 'lame-svn', 'parent': '50-libmp3lame', 'declaration': 'scripts.d/50-libmp3lame.sh SCRIPT_REV=6835', 'repository': 'https://svn.code.sf.net/p/lame/svn/trunk/lame', 'version': 'SVN revision 6835', 'archiveUrl': 'https://sourceforge.net/code-snapshots/svn/l/la/lame/svn/lame-svn-r6835-trunk-lame.zip', 'archiveFile': lame_tar.name, 'sha256': digest(lame_tar), 'originalArchiveFile': lame_zip.name, 'originalArchiveSha256': digest(lame_zip), 'status': 'source-archive-downloaded', 'conversion': 'TAR.GZ contains the unmodified file bytes from the official revision ZIP; original ZIP retained.'})
        write(SOURCE / 'extra-source-manifest.json', extras)
    deps = read(SOURCE / 'dependency-source-manifest.json')
    excluded = []
    for item in deps['entries']:
        if item['status'] == 'source-archive-downloaded':
            actual = digest(SOURCE / 'dependency-archives' / item['archiveFile'])
            if actual != item['sha256']:
                raise ValueError('Source archive hash mismatch: ' + item['name'])
            continue
        if item['name'].startswith('45-x11-') or item['name'] == '45-pulseaudio' or item['name'] in ('50-vaapi-30-libpciaccess', '50-vaapi-40-libdrm'):
            reason = 'Windows target disabled by historical ffbuild_enabled conditions; not distributed code'
        elif item['name'] == '10-xorg-macros':
            reason = 'Generally available build macro tool; not linked into the distributed Windows engine'
        else:
            raise ValueError('Unreviewed unavailable source stage: ' + item['name'])
        item['distributionSourceStatus'] = 'excluded'
        item['exclusionReason'] = reason
        excluded.append({'name': item['name'], 'reason': reason, 'recipePath': item['recipePath']})
    deps['sourceBundleCompleteForReviewedWindowsInventory'] = True
    deps['sourceBundleComplete'] = True
    write(SOURCE / 'dependency-source-manifest.json', deps)
    rust = read(SOURCE / 'rust-source-manifest.json')
    if rust['gitEntries'] or not rust['originalLockRegistrySourcesComplete']:
        raise ValueError('Rust original-lock sources are incomplete')
    for item in rust['registryEntries']:
        if digest(SOURCE / item['archiveFile']) != item['expectedSha256']:
            raise ValueError('Cargo.lock checksum mismatch: ' + item['name'])
    required = {}

    def include(path, expected=None):
        relative = path.relative_to(SOURCE).as_posix()
        actual = digest(path)
        if expected and actual != expected:
            raise ValueError('Inventory SHA-256 mismatch: ' + relative)
        required[relative] = {'path': relative, 'sha256': actual}

    for name in ('dependency-source-manifest.json', 'submodule-source-manifest.json', 'extra-source-manifest.json', 'rust-source-manifest.json'):
        data = read(SOURCE / name)
        entries = data['entries'] if name.startswith('dependency') else data['registryEntries'] if name.startswith('rust') else data
        for item in entries:
            if item.get('status') in ('source-archive-downloaded', 'checksum-verified-source'):
                path = SOURCE / (item['archiveFile'] if name.startswith('rust') else 'dependency-archives/' + item['archiveFile'])
                include(path, item['sha256'])
                if item.get('originalArchiveFile'):
                    include(SOURCE / 'dependency-archives' / item['originalArchiveFile'], item['originalArchiveSha256'])
    for path in SOURCE.iterdir():
        if path.is_file() and path.name != 'distribution-manifest.json':
            include(path)
    include(SOURCE / 'FFmpeg-ebafaee10ab45fce1964bd09efcd848e2da5b401.tar.gz', '4fb0f61392cb355459f0f9fec819b47d3c698774b89c1df7029a897101ccb502')
    include(SOURCE / 'FFmpeg-Builds-6c9aec5fc9a72ec3abedd1fa84db141fa18cf52b.tar.gz', '81da4f7c878dc0f60e6b8d0049a112e32178e20f025d468fe4e99e77b2dfe0ec')
    distribution = {'formatVersion': 1, 'reviewedAt': '2026-10-12', 'binaryVersion': 'n9.0.2-14-gebafaee10a-20260929', 'distributionLicense': 'GPL-3.0-or-later', 'sourceBundleCompleteForReviewedInventory': True, 'sourceCompanionFileName': '你的猫咪文件助手-V0.7-Windows-x64-Sources.zip', 'archiveRelativeRoot': 'tools/ffmpeg/source', 'description': 'Reviewed corresponding-source/materials inventory for the unchanged Windows engine. Preserves core, static dependencies, actual relevant submodules, extra source downloads and Cargo.lock registry sources; includes conservative optional/build-only coverage.', 'scopeLimits': ['Does not certify patent rights or commercial contracts.', 'General-purpose compilers/build tools and system libraries are treated according to applicable source exceptions.', 'The original cc package is a build-dependency; original locks and update command are retained, without claiming the build-tool version used at build time.', 'Source archive stages for other targets and disabled features may be conservatively included; this list does not claim exact linker object placement.'], 'excludedStages': excluded, 'requiredFiles': sorted(required.values(), key=lambda item: item['path'])}
    write(SOURCE / 'distribution-manifest.json', distribution)
    # License expressions are asserted only where actually established. Unknown
    # per-file expressions remain NOASSERTION; original complete notices and
    # corresponding source are always referenced rather than replaced by a
    # guessed SPDX label.
    binary_group = ['bin/' + p.name for p in sorted((FF / 'bin').iterdir()) if p.is_file() and p.name != 'ffplay.exe']
    components = [{'spdxId': 'SPDXRef-FFmpeg-Engine', 'name': 'FFmpeg independent native engine', 'version': distribution['binaryVersion'], 'licenseConcluded': 'GPL-3.0-or-later', 'coreLicense': 'LGPL-3.0-or-later', 'sourceRef': 'ebafaee10ab45fce1964bd09efcd848e2da5b401', 'sourceRepository': 'https://github.com/FFmpeg/FFmpeg', 'sourceArchive': 'source/FFmpeg-ebafaee10ab45fce1964bd09efcd848e2da5b401.tar.gz', 'binaryMapping': binary_group}]
    lines = ['FFmpeg V0.7 - original third-party license and copyright index', '', 'The upstream lgpl-shared label does NOT describe every linked component.', 'Actual avformat contains static GPL FFTW through Chromaprint. The independent', 'engine is distributed under GPL-3.0-or-later; original component terms remain.', 'Full GPL text: licenses/COPYING.GPLv3', 'Original LGPL core text: LICENSE.txt and licenses/COPYING.LGPLv3', 'Corresponding sources: same-version -Sources.zip; source/README.md.', 'Source/license integrity inventory: provenance.json and source/distribution-manifest.json.', '', 'Each referenced original license/copyright/notice file is included with the', 'binary distribution under licenses/. Source archives retain their originals.', 'NOASSERTION is used instead of guessing an unaudited per-file SPDX expression;', 'it does not replace original notices. Some listed source stages/crates are', 'optional, build-only, or for disabled features and are conservatively preserved.', '', 'This software is based in part on the work of the Independent JPEG Group.', 'The application has not modified the embedded IJG-derived FFmpeg source files.', 'Original IJG notice: licenses/IJG-jfdctfst-original-notice.txt', '', 'LCEVC BSD-3-Clause-Clear COPYING and LICENSE.md remain applicable, including', 'the explicit exclusion of patent grants. Codec/SDK patent rights are separate', 'from open-source copyright permissions; see docs/third-party-audit.md.', 'GCC runtime original GPL3 + Runtime Library Exception and actual Rust runtime', 'MIT/Apache/COPYRIGHT files: licenses/compiler-runtimes/.', '']
    for item in deps['entries']:
        if item['status'] != 'source-archive-downloaded':
            continue
        expression = 'GPL-2.0-or-later' if item['name'] == '25-fftw3' else 'NOASSERTION'
        components.append({'spdxId': 'SPDXRef-' + item['name'], 'name': item['name'], 'version': item['sourceRef'], 'licenseConcluded': expression, 'licenseEvidenceFiles': item.get('licenseFiles', []), 'sourceRef': item['sourceRef'], 'sourceRepository': item['repository'], 'sourceArchive': 'source/dependency-archives/' + item['archiveFile'], 'sourceSha256': item['sha256'], 'binaryMapping': 'FFmpeg engine group; optional/build-only stages included conservatively, exact linker-object placement not asserted'})
        lines.extend([item['name'] + ' | source ref ' + item['sourceRef'], '  Upstream: ' + item['repository'], '  Source archive: source/dependency-archives/' + item['archiveFile']])
        lines.extend('  Original notice: ' + f['path'] for f in item.get('licenseFiles', []))
        lines.append('')
    for name in ('submodule-source-manifest.json', 'extra-source-manifest.json'):
        for item in read(SOURCE / name):
            components.append({'spdxId': 'SPDXRef-' + item['name'], 'name': item['name'], 'version': item.get('commit', item.get('version', 'NOASSERTION')), 'licenseConcluded': 'NOASSERTION', 'licenseEvidenceFiles': item.get('licenseFiles', []), 'sourceRepository': item['repository'], 'sourceArchive': 'source/dependency-archives/' + item['archiveFile'], 'sourceSha256': item['sha256'], 'binaryMapping': 'Native source dependency of ' + item['parent']})
            lines.extend([item['name'], '  Source index: source/' + name])
            lines.extend('  Original notice: ' + f['path'] for f in item.get('licenseFiles', []))
    for item in rust['registryEntries']:
        components.append({'spdxId': 'SPDXRef-Rust-' + item['name'] + '-' + item['version'], 'name': item['name'], 'version': item['version'], 'licenseConcluded': 'NOASSERTION', 'licenseDeclaredBySource': item.get('licenseDeclaredBySource', 'NOASSERTION'), 'licenseEvidenceFiles': item.get('licenseFiles', []), 'sourceRepository': item['url'], 'sourceArchive': 'source/' + item['archiveFile'], 'sourceSha256': item['expectedSha256'], 'binaryMapping': 'Cargo.lock source coverage for ' + ', '.join(item['locks']) + '; includes optional/build-only crates'})
    lines.extend(['', 'Rust registry source and notice index (all 577 checksum-verified archives):', '  source/rust-source-manifest.json', '  licenses/dependencies/rust-*/', '', 'Original build-script MIT license: licenses/BtbN-build-scripts-LICENSE.txt'])
    (FF / 'THIRD-PARTY-LICENSES.txt').write_text('\n'.join(lines) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')
    write(FF / 'component-manifest.json', {'formatVersion': 1, 'inventoryKind': 'Reviewed source/notice coverage; not a linker-level object SBOM.', 'distributionLicense': 'GPL-3.0-or-later', 'components': components})
    provenance = read(FF / 'provenance.json')
    provenance['license'] = 'GPL-3.0-or-later'
    provenance['coreLicense'] = 'LGPL-3.0-or-later'
    provenance['sourceBundleComplete'] = True
    provenance['distributionAuditStatus'] = 'reviewed-for-distribution'
    provenance['distributionLicenseCorrection'] = 'Actual avformat DLL embeds GPL FFTW 3.3.11 through static Chromaprint; LGPL upstream package label only describes the core, not all linked code.'
    provenance['noticeFiles'] = [{'path': p.name, 'sha256': digest(p)} for p in (FF / 'THIRD-PARTY-LICENSES.txt', FF / 'component-manifest.json', FF / 'LICENSE.txt')]
    provenance['sourceFiles'] = [{'path': 'source/' + p.name, 'sha256': digest(p)} for p in sorted(SOURCE.iterdir()) if p.is_file()]
    provenance['licenseFiles'] = [{'path': p.relative_to(FF).as_posix(), 'sha256': digest(p)} for p in sorted((FF / 'licenses').rglob('*')) if p.is_file()]
    write(FF / 'provenance.json', provenance)
    print(json.dumps({'sourceArchives': len(required), 'sourceBytes': sum((SOURCE / p).stat().st_size for p in required), 'licenseFiles': len(provenance['licenseFiles']), 'excludedStages': len(excluded)}))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/inspect-ffmpeg-sources.py ---
"""Preserve source-license bytes and registry sources; never execute upstream code.

Run after collect-ffmpeg-sources.ps1. --download-rust accesses only sources pinned
by the original Cargo.lock checksum; it does not rewrite the lock or claim which
optional/build-only crates were actually linked into the upstream binaries.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import concurrent.futures
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
import re
import tarfile
import tomllib
import urllib.request

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
SOURCE = ROOT / 'tools/ffmpeg/source'
LICENSES = ROOT / 'tools/ffmpeg/licenses/dependencies'


def sha256(data: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


def save_json(path: Path, value: object) -> None:
    path.write_text(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')


def retain_licenses(archive: tarfile.TarFile, name: str) -> list[dict]:
    result = []
    destination = LICENSES / name
    destination.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for member in archive.getmembers():
        basename = PurePosixPath(member.name).name
        if not re.match(r'^(LICENSE|COPYING|COPYRIGHT|NOTICE|AUTHORS|PATENTS|UNLICENSE|README)([._-].*)?$', basename, re.I):
            continue
        if member.isdir():
            continue
        try:
            stream = archive.extractfile(member)
        except (KeyError, tarfile.TarError):
            continue
        if stream is None:
            continue
        data = stream.read()
        filename = sha256(member.name.encode())[:16] + '-' + basename
        output = destination / filename
        output.write_bytes(data)
        result.append({'archiveMember': member.name, 'path': output.relative_to(ROOT / 'tools/ffmpeg').as_posix(), 'sha256': sha256(data), 'preservation': 'original bytes; archive links resolved within the same archive'})
    return result


def inspect_sources() -> dict:
    path = SOURCE / 'dependency-source-manifest.json'
    manifest = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
    registry = {}
    git_sources = []
    locks = []
    for item in manifest['entries']:
        source = SOURCE / 'dependency-archives' / item['archiveFile']
        if not source.is_file():
            continue
        with tarfile.open(source, 'r:gz') as archive:
            # A separately acquired git archive is still checked against its
            # exact pinned git object by the collector's git-fetch record.
            item.update(status='source-archive-downloaded', sha256=sha256(source.read_bytes()), error=None)
            item['licenseFiles'] = retain_licenses(archive, item['name'])
            item['licenseConcluded'] = 'NOASSERTION'
            if item['name'] not in ('50-rav1e', '50-librsvg-99-librsvg'):
                continue
            lock_member = next((m for m in archive.getmembers() if len(PurePosixPath(m.name).parts) == 2 and m.name.endswith('/Cargo.lock')), None)
            if lock_member is None:
                continue
            data = archive.extractfile(lock_member).read()
            destination = SOURCE / (item['name'] + '-Cargo.lock')
            destination.write_bytes(data)
            lock = tomllib.loads(data.decode('utf-8'))
            locks.append({'component': item['name'], 'file': destination.name, 'sha256': sha256(data), 'sourceRef': item['sourceRef'], 'upstreamBuildMutatesLock': item['mutatesCargoLock']})
            for package in lock.get('package', []):
                source_id = package.get('source', '')
                if source_id.startswith('registry+'):
                    key = (package['name'], package['version'], package['checksum'])
                    registry.setdefault(key, {'name': key[0], 'version': key[1], 'expectedSha256': key[2], 'locks': []})['locks'].append(item['name'])
                elif source_id.startswith('git+'):
                    git_sources.append({'name': package['name'], 'version': package['version'], 'source': source_id, 'lock': item['name']})
    save_json(path, manifest)
    for extra_name in ('submodule-source-manifest.json', 'extra-source-manifest.json'):
        extra_path = SOURCE / extra_name
        if not extra_path.is_file():
            continue
        extras = json.loads(extra_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
        for item in extras:
            if item.get('status') != 'source-archive-downloaded':
                continue
            source = SOURCE / 'dependency-archives' / item['archiveFile']
            with tarfile.open(source, 'r:gz') as archive:
                item['licenseFiles'] = retain_licenses(archive, item['name'])
        save_json(extra_path, extras)
    return {'formatVersion': 1, 'purpose': 'Registry sources referenced by original pinned Cargo locks; includes optional and build-only packages. Exact link membership is not asserted.', 'locks': locks, 'registryEntries': list(registry.values()), 'gitEntries': git_sources, 'originalLockRegistrySourcesComplete': False}


def collect_crate(item: dict, no_proxy: bool) -> dict:
    target = SOURCE / 'rust-crates' / (item['name'] + '-' + item['version'] + '.crate')
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    url = 'https://static.crates.io/crates/{0}/{0}-{1}.crate'.format(item['name'], item['version'])
    item.update(url=url, archiveFile=target.relative_to(SOURCE).as_posix(), status='unavailable', sha256=None, licenseConcluded='NOASSERTION')
    try:
        if target.is_file():
            data = target.read_bytes()
        else:
            opener = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({} if no_proxy else None))
            with opener.open(url, timeout=60) as response:
                data = response.read()
        checksum = sha256(data)
        if checksum != item['expectedSha256']:
            raise ValueError('Downloaded bytes do not match the pinned Cargo.lock SHA-256')
        target.write_bytes(data)
        with tarfile.open(target, 'r:gz') as archive:
            item['licenseFiles'] = retain_licenses(archive, 'rust-' + item['name'] + '-' + item['version'])
            toml_member = next((m for m in archive.getmembers() if len(PurePosixPath(m.name).parts) == 2 and m.name.endswith('/Cargo.toml')), None)
            if toml_member:
                package = tomllib.loads(archive.extractfile(toml_member).read().decode('utf-8')).get('package', {})
                if isinstance(package.get('license'), str):
                    item['licenseDeclaredBySource'] = package['license']
        item.update(status='checksum-verified-source', sha256=checksum)
    except Exception as error:
        item['error'] = str(error)
    return item


if __name__ == '__main__':
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('--download-rust', action='store_true')
    parser.add_argument('--no-proxy', action='store_true')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    rust = inspect_sources()
    if args.download_rust:
        with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=16) as pool:
            rust['registryEntries'] = list(pool.map(lambda entry: collect_crate(entry, args.no_proxy), rust['registryEntries']))
        rust['originalLockRegistrySourcesComplete'] = all(e['status'] == 'checksum-verified-source' for e in rust['registryEntries'])
    save_json(SOURCE / 'rust-source-manifest.json', rust)
    print(json.dumps({'locks': len(rust['locks']), 'registryPackages': len(rust['registryEntries']), 'gitPackages': len(rust['gitEntries']), 'verifiedRegistryPackages': sum(e.get('status') == 'checksum-verified-source' for e in rust['registryEntries'])}))

[Code]
var
  KeepSaveChoice: Boolean;

function GetFileAttributes(const FileName: String): Integer;
  external 'GetFileAttributesW@kernel32.dll stdcall';

function ReparsePath(Path: String): Boolean;
var Attributes: Integer; Parent: String;
begin
  Result := False;
  while Length(Path) > 3 do begin
    Attributes := GetFileAttributes(Path);
    if (Attributes <> -1) and ((Attributes and $400) <> 0) then begin Result := True; Exit; end;
    Parent := ExtractFileDir(Path);
    if Parent = Path then Exit;
    Path := Parent;
  end;
end;

function ReparseTree(const Path: String): Boolean;
var FindRec: TFindRec; Child: String;
begin
  Result := ReparsePath(Path);
  if Result then Exit;
  if FindFirst(AddBackslash(Path) + '*', FindRec) then begin
    try
      repeat
        if (FindRec.Name <> '.') and (FindRec.Name <> '..') then begin
          Child := AddBackslash(Path) + FindRec.Name;
          if (FindRec.Attributes and $400) <> 0 then begin Result := True; Exit; end;
          if (FindRec.Attributes and FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY) <> 0 then
            if ReparseTree(Child) then begin Result := True; Exit; end;
        end;
      until not FindNext(FindRec);
    finally FindClose(FindRec); end;
  end;
end;

function LegacyRunning: Boolean;
var Identity, Session: String;
begin
#ifdef TestMode
  Result := False;
#else
  Identity := GetEnv('USERDOMAIN') + '\' + GetUserNameString;
  Session := Uppercase(Copy(GetSHA256OfString(UTF8Encode(Identity)), 1, 16));
  Result := CheckForMutexes('Local\CatFileAssistant.V08.' + Session);
#endif
end;

function StopApplication: Boolean;
var ExitCode: Integer; Executable: String;
begin
  Executable := ExpandConstant('{app}\你的猫咪文件助手.exe');
  Result := True;
  if FileExists(Executable) then
    Result := Exec(Executable, '--installer-exit', ExpandConstant('{app}'), SW_HIDE, ewWaitUntilTerminated, ExitCode) and (ExitCode = 0)
  else Result := not LegacyRunning;
end;

function SafeInstallDestination(const DirectoryName: String): Boolean;
var Path, UserProfilePath: String; FindRec: TFindRec;
begin
  Result := True;
  Path := RemoveBackslashUnlessRoot(DirectoryName);
  UserProfilePath := RemoveBackslashUnlessRoot(GetEnv('USERPROFILE'));
  if (Length(Path) <= 3) or (UserProfilePath = '') or ReparsePath(Path) or
     (CompareText(Path, UserProfilePath) = 0) or
     (CompareText(Path, ExpandConstant('{localappdata}')) = 0) or
     (CompareText(Path, ExpandConstant('{autodesktop}')) = 0) or
     (CompareText(Path, ExpandConstant('{userdocs}')) = 0) or
     (CompareText(Path, ExpandConstant('{win}')) = 0) then Result := False;
  if Result and DirExists(Path) and not FileExists(AddBackslash(Path) + 'app\YourCatFileAssistant.dll') then begin
    if FindFirst(AddBackslash(Path) + '*', FindRec) then begin
      try
        repeat
          if (FindRec.Name <> '.') and (FindRec.Name <> '..') then Result := False;
        until not FindNext(FindRec);
      finally FindClose(FindRec); end;
    end;
  end;
end;

function NextButtonClick(CurPageID: Integer): Boolean;
begin
  Result := True;
  if CurPageID = wpSelectDir then begin
    Result := SafeInstallDestination(WizardDirValue);
    if not Result then MsgBox(CustomMessage('UnsafePath'), mbError, MB_OK);
  end;
end;

function PrepareToInstall(var NeedsRestart: Boolean): String;
begin
  Result := '';
  if not SafeInstallDestination(ExpandConstant('{app}')) or ReparseTree(ExpandConstant('{app}')) then Result := CustomMessage('UnsafePath')
  else if not StopApplication then Result := CustomMessage('CloseFirst');
end;

begin
  if CurStep = ssPostInstall then begin
    if ActiveLanguage = 'en_US' then Language := 'en-US' else Language := 'zh-CN';
    Seed := '{"language":"' + Language + '"}' + #13#10;

#endif
  end;
end;

var Dialog: TSetupForm; KeepSave: TNewCheckBox; Explanation: TNewStaticText; ConfirmButton, CancelButton: TNewButton;
begin
  KeepSaveChoice := ExpandConstant('{param:KEEPSAVE|0}') = '1';

    KeepSave.SetBounds(ScaleX(20), ScaleY(105), ScaleX(440), ScaleY(24));

function InitializeUninstall: Boolean;
begin
  Result := not ReparseTree(ExpandConstant('{app}'));
  if not Result then begin
    Log(CustomMessage('UnsafePath'));
    if not UninstallSilent then MsgBox(CustomMessage('UnsafePath'), mbError, MB_OK);
  end;
  if not Result then Exit;
  Result := ChooseUninstallOptions;
  if not Result then Exit;
  Result := StopApplication;
  if not Result then begin
    Log(CustomMessage('CloseFirst'));
    if not UninstallSilent then MsgBox(CustomMessage('CloseFirst'), mbError, MB_OK);
  end;
end;

procedure CleanupError;
begin
  Log(CustomMessage('CleanupFailed'));
  if UninstallSilent then Abort else RaiseException(CustomMessage('CleanupFailed'));
end;

procedure CurUninstallStepChanged(CurUninstallStep: TUninstallStep);
var Parameters: String; ExitCode: Integer;
begin
  if CurUninstallStep = usUninstall then begin
    Parameters := '--installer-clean-data';
    if KeepSaveChoice then Parameters := Parameters + ' --keep-pet-save';
    if not Exec(ExpandConstant('{app}\你的猫咪文件助手.exe'), Parameters, ExpandConstant('{app}'), SW_HIDE, ewWaitUntilTerminated, ExitCode) or (ExitCode <> 0) then
      CleanupError;
    if FileExists(ExpandConstant('{app}\app\installed-language.json')) and
       not DeleteFile(ExpandConstant('{app}\app\installed-language.json')) then
      CleanupError;
#ifdef TestMode
    if not DeleteFile(ExpandConstant('{app}\app\installer-test-mode')) then
      CleanupError;
#endif
  end;
end;

if (args.FirstOrDefault() == "validate")

            if(literal.Token.ValueText.Any(c=>c is >= '\u4e00' and <= '\u9fff')) sources.Add(literal.Token.ValueText);

            foreach(var content in literal.Contents)

                else if(content is InterpolationSyntax part){format.Append('{').Append(index++);if(part.AlignmentClause is not null)format.Append(',').Append(part.AlignmentClause.Value.ToString());if(part.FormatClause is not null)format.Append(':').Append(part.FormatClause.FormatStringToken.ValueText);format.Append('}');}

    }

    var missing=sources.Where(source=>!catalog.ContainsKey(source)).Order().ToArray();
    Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(root,"artifacts/localization"));

    Console.WriteLine($"Catalog {catalog.Count}, sources {sources.Count}, missing {missing.Length}");
    Console.WriteLine(string.Join("\n",missing));return;
}
var files = Directory.GetFiles(Path.Combine(root, "src"), "*.cs", SearchOption.AllDirectories)
    .Where(path => !path.Contains("\\obj\\") && !path.Contains("\\bin\\"))
    .Where(path => path.Contains("CatFileAssistant.Core\\") || path.Contains("CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure\\") || path.Contains("CatFileAssistant.Desktop\\ViewModels\\"))
    .Where(path => !new[] { "Locale.cs", "PetStateStore.cs", "PetEconomyContracts.cs", "SettingsStore.cs", "OwnedArtifacts.cs", "MainViewModel.Language.cs", "MainViewModel.DesktopPet.cs" }.Contains(Path.GetFileName(path)));
var total = 0;
foreach (var file in files)
{
    var before = File.ReadAllText(file);
    var rewriter = new Localizer();
    var after = rewriter.Visit(CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(before).GetRoot())!.ToFullString();
    if (before != after) { File.WriteAllText(file, after, new UTF8Encoding(false)); Console.WriteLine($"{rewriter.Count}: {Path.GetRelativePath(root, file)}"); total += rewriter.Count; }
}
Console.WriteLine($"Localized {total} expressions.");
class Localizer : CSharpSyntaxRewriter
{
    public int Count;
    private static bool Chinese(string text) => text.Any(c => c is >= '\u4e00' and <= '\u9fff');
    private static bool Excluded(SyntaxNode node)
    {
        if (node.Ancestors().OfType<InvocationExpressionSyntax>().Any(value => value.Expression.ToString() is "Locale.T" or "Locale.F" or "Locale.TranslateText")) return true;
        if (node.Ancestors().Any(value => value is ConstantPatternSyntax or AttributeSyntax or CaseSwitchLabelSyntax)) return true;
        if (node.Ancestors().OfType<FieldDeclarationSyntax>().Any(field => field.Modifiers.Any(SyntaxKind.ConstKeyword))) return true;
        if (node.Ancestors().OfType<VariableDeclaratorSyntax>().Any(value => value.Identifier.ValueText is "archiveName")) return true;
        return false;
    }
    private SyntaxNode Wrap(ExpressionSyntax original, ExpressionSyntax changed, string method)
    {
        Count++;
        return SyntaxFactory.InvocationExpression(SyntaxFactory.ParseExpression("Locale." + method),
            SyntaxFactory.ArgumentList(SyntaxFactory.SingletonSeparatedList(SyntaxFactory.Argument(changed.WithoutTrivia())))).WithTriviaFrom(original);
    }
    public override SyntaxNode? VisitLiteralExpression(LiteralExpressionSyntax node)
    {
        if (!node.IsKind(SyntaxKind.StringLiteralExpression) || !Chinese(node.Token.ValueText) || Excluded(node)) return base.VisitLiteralExpression(node);
        if (node.Token.ValueText.StartsWith('_') || node.Token.ValueText is ".分卷") return node;
        return Wrap(node, node, "T");
    }
    public override SyntaxNode? VisitInterpolatedStringExpression(InterpolatedStringExpressionSyntax node)
    {
        var changed = (InterpolatedStringExpressionSyntax)base.VisitInterpolatedStringExpression(node)!;
        return !Excluded(node) && node.Contents.OfType<InterpolatedStringTextSyntax>().Any(value => Chinese(value.TextToken.ValueText)) ? Wrap(node, changed, "F") : changed;
    }
}
