--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/measure-motion.ps1 ---
param(
    [string]$ArtifactsDirectory,
    [string]$EngineDirectory
)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$projectDirectory = Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent
$dotnetExecutable = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\dotnet\sdk\dotnet.exe'
$acceptanceAssembly = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tests\CatFileAssistant.Acceptance\bin\Release\net10.0-windows\CatFileAssistant.Acceptance.dll'
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $dotnetExecutable)) { throw 'Local .NET SDK is missing. Run scripts/build.ps1 first.' }
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $acceptanceAssembly)) { throw 'Release acceptance runner is missing. Run scripts/build.ps1 first.' }
if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($ArtifactsDirectory)) {
    $runName = [DateTimeOffset]::UtcNow.ToString('yyyyMMdd-HHmmss') + '-' + [Guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N').Substring(0, 6)
    $ArtifactsDirectory = Join-Path $projectDirectory ('artifacts\motion-performance\' + $runName)
}
$ArtifactsDirectory = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($ArtifactsDirectory)
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $ArtifactsDirectory -Force | Out-Null
$hardware = [ordered]@{
    capturedUtc = [DateTimeOffset]::UtcNow.ToString('o')
    operatingSystem = [Environment]::OSVersion.VersionString
    logicalProcessorCount = [Environment]::ProcessorCount
    method = 'WPF render callback scheduling on the recorded desktop; physical GPU present times and mouse latency are not measured.'
}
try {
    $hardware.processor = @(Get-CimInstance Win32_Processor | Select-Object Name, NumberOfCores, NumberOfLogicalProcessors, MaxClockSpeed)
    $hardware.computer = @(Get-CimInstance Win32_ComputerSystem | Select-Object Manufacturer, Model, TotalPhysicalMemory)
    $hardware.videoController = @(Get-CimInstance Win32_VideoController | Select-Object Name, DriverVersion, CurrentHorizontalResolution, CurrentVerticalResolution, CurrentRefreshRate)
    $hardware.windows = @(Get-CimInstance Win32_OperatingSystem | Select-Object Caption, Version, BuildNumber, OSArchitecture)
} catch { $hardware.hardwareQueryError = $_.Exception.Message }
try {
    $hardware.RegistryCpuName = (Get-ItemProperty -LiteralPath 'HKLM:\HARDWARE\DESCRIPTION\System\CentralProcessor\0' -Name ProcessorNameString).ProcessorNameString.Trim()
} catch { $hardware.registryCpuQueryError = $_.Exception.Message }
try {
    if (-not ('CatFileAssistant.MotionEvidenceNative' -as [type])) {
        Add-Type -TypeDefinition @'
using System;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
namespace CatFileAssistant {
    public static class MotionEvidenceNative {
        [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
        public struct MemoryStatusEx {
            public uint Length;
            public uint MemoryLoad;
            public ulong TotalPhysical;
            public ulong AvailablePhysical;
            public ulong TotalPageFile;
            public ulong AvailablePageFile;
            public ulong TotalVirtual;
            public ulong AvailableVirtual;
            public ulong AvailableExtendedVirtual;
        }
        [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)]
        public struct DisplayDevice {
            public uint Size;
            [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.ByValTStr, SizeConst = 32)] public string DeviceName;
            [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.ByValTStr, SizeConst = 128)] public string DeviceString;
            public uint StateFlags;
            [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.ByValTStr, SizeConst = 128)] public string DeviceId;
            [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.ByValTStr, SizeConst = 128)] public string DeviceKey;
        }
        [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
        [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
        public static extern bool GlobalMemoryStatusEx(ref MemoryStatusEx status);
        [DllImport("user32.dll", EntryPoint = "EnumDisplayDevicesW", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode, SetLastError = true)]
        [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
        public static extern bool EnumDisplayDevices(string device, uint index, ref DisplayDevice display, uint flags);
    }
}
'@
    }
    $memoryStatus = New-Object CatFileAssistant.MotionEvidenceNative+MemoryStatusEx
    $memoryStatus.Length = [Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::SizeOf($memoryStatus)
    if ([CatFileAssistant.MotionEvidenceNative]::GlobalMemoryStatusEx([ref]$memoryStatus)) {
        $hardware.nativeMemory = [ordered]@{ totalPhysicalBytes = $memoryStatus.TotalPhysical; availablePhysicalBytes = $memoryStatus.AvailablePhysical; method = 'Read-only kernel32 GlobalMemoryStatusEx' }
    } else { $hardware.nativeMemoryQueryError = 'GlobalMemoryStatusEx failed; Win32 error ' + [Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::GetLastWin32Error() }
    $nativeDisplays = @()
    for ($displayIndex = 0; $displayIndex -lt 16; $displayIndex++) {
        $displayDevice = New-Object CatFileAssistant.MotionEvidenceNative+DisplayDevice
        $displayDevice.Size = [Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::SizeOf($displayDevice)
        if (-not [CatFileAssistant.MotionEvidenceNative]::EnumDisplayDevices($null, $displayIndex, [ref]$displayDevice, 0)) { break }
        $nativeDisplays += [ordered]@{ deviceName = $displayDevice.DeviceName; deviceDescription = $displayDevice.DeviceString; stateFlags = $displayDevice.StateFlags }
    }
    $hardware.nativeDisplayDevices = $nativeDisplays
    if ($nativeDisplays.Count -eq 0) { $hardware.nativeDisplayQueryNote = 'No display devices returned in this session; GPU and monitor descriptions are unavailable.' }
    $hardware.nativeQueryMethod = 'Read-only GlobalMemoryStatusEx and EnumDisplayDevices; no display or system settings changed.'
} catch { $hardware.nativeHardwareQueryError = $_.Exception.Message }
if (-not $hardware.videoController) {
    $hardware.unavailableInformation = @('CIM hardware details: see hardwareQueryError.', 'Display refresh rate and graphics driver version were not available from the fallback queries.')
}
$hardware | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6 | Set-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $ArtifactsDirectory 'hardware.json') -Encoding utf8
$env:DOTNET_ROOT = Split-Path $dotnetExecutable -Parent
$env:DOTNET_CLI_HOME = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\dotnet\home'
$env:DOTNET_CLI_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT = '1'
$env:DOTNET_NOLOGO = '1'
$testArguments = @($acceptanceAssembly, '--root', $projectDirectory, '--case', 'motion-performance', '--artifacts', $ArtifactsDirectory)
if (-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($EngineDirectory)) { $testArguments += @('--engine-directory', [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($EngineDirectory)) }
& $dotnetExecutable @testArguments
$runnerExitCode = $LASTEXITCODE
Write-Output ('Motion evidence: ' + $ArtifactsDirectory)
if ($runnerExitCode -ne 0) { throw ('Motion performance budget not validated (runner exit ' + $runnerExitCode + '). Read report.json for measurements and failures.') }


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/measure-v09-baseline.ps1 ---
param([string]$ArtifactsDirectory)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$projectDirectory = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent))
if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($ArtifactsDirectory)) { $ArtifactsDirectory = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'artifacts\v09-baseline-performance' }
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $ArtifactsDirectory | Out-Null
$results = @()
foreach ($label in @('V0.8.1', 'V0.9')) {
  $executable = Join-Path $projectDirectory ('release\你的猫咪文件助手-' + $label + '-Windows-x64\你的猫咪文件助手.exe')
  if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $executable)) { throw "Missing comparison executable: $executable" }
  $samples = @()
  foreach ($sampleIndex in 1..3) {
    $ownedProcess = $null
    try {
      $startup = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
      # This flag uses temporary settings and bypasses the ordinary user's tray/pet session.
      $ownedProcess = Start-Process -FilePath $executable -ArgumentList '--verify-package' -WorkingDirectory (Split-Path $executable -Parent) -WindowStyle Hidden -PassThru
      if (-not $ownedProcess.WaitForInputIdle(30000)) { throw 'GUI did not reach an input-idle state.' }
      $startup.Stop()
      Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 2000
      $ownedProcess.Refresh()
      if ($ownedProcess.HasExited) { throw 'Comparison application exited before idle measurement.' }
      $cpuStart = $ownedProcess.TotalProcessorTime.TotalMilliseconds
      $idleClock = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
      $memorySamples = @()
      foreach ($tick in 1..10) { Start-Sleep -Seconds 1; $ownedProcess.Refresh(); $memorySamples += $ownedProcess.PrivateMemorySize64 }
      $idleClock.Stop()
      $cpu = 100 * ($ownedProcess.TotalProcessorTime.TotalMilliseconds - $cpuStart) / ($idleClock.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds * [Environment]::ProcessorCount)
      $memory = ($memorySamples | Measure-Object -Average).Average
      $normalClose = $ownedProcess.CloseMainWindow() -and $ownedProcess.WaitForExit(10000)
      $samples += [ordered]@{ run=$sampleIndex; inputIdleMs=$startup.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds; idleCpuPercent=$cpu; privateMemoryBytes=$memory; normallyClosed=$normalClose }
      if (-not $normalClose) { throw 'Comparison application did not close normally.' }
    } finally { if ($ownedProcess -and -not $ownedProcess.HasExited) { $ownedProcess.Kill($true); $ownedProcess.WaitForExit(10000) | Out-Null }; if ($ownedProcess) { $ownedProcess.Dispose() } }
  }
  function Get-Median($Values) { $orderedValues = @($Values | Sort-Object); return $orderedValues[[int][Math]::Floor($orderedValues.Count / 2)] }
  $results += [ordered]@{ version=$label; samples=$samples; medianInputIdleMs=(Get-Median @($samples | ForEach-Object inputIdleMs)); medianIdleCpuPercent=(Get-Median @($samples | ForEach-Object idleCpuPercent)); medianPrivateMemoryBytes=(Get-Median @($samples | ForEach-Object privateMemoryBytes)) }
}
$report = [ordered]@{
  measuredUtc=[DateTimeOffset]::UtcNow.ToString('o'); operatingSystem=[Environment]::OSVersion.VersionString; logicalProcessors=[Environment]::ProcessorCount
  method='Three separate self-contained production processes per version, --verify-package isolated settings, input-idle startup and ten one-second private-memory/CPU samples after two-second warmup. Production first screens: V0.8.1 video conversion, V0.9 home. Desktop pet excluded and covered separately. Input-idle does not measure physical display or end-to-end visual startup.'
  versions=$results
  inputIdleChangePercent=100 * ($results[1].medianInputIdleMs / $results[0].medianInputIdleMs - 1)
  idleCpuChangePercentagePoints=$results[1].medianIdleCpuPercent - $results[0].medianIdleCpuPercent
  privateMemoryChangeMiB=($results[1].medianPrivateMemoryBytes - $results[0].medianPrivateMemoryBytes) / 1048576
}
$report | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 7 | Set-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $ArtifactsDirectory 'report.json') -Encoding utf8
$report | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 7


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/pack-cat-assets.ps1 ---
param([switch]$OnlyIcons)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$projectDirectory = Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent
$catDirectory = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'src\CatFileAssistant.Desktop\Assets\Cat'
$brandDirectory = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'src\CatFileAssistant.Desktop\Assets\Brand'
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Drawing
# OnlyIcons leaves the legacy animation atlas and all extracted parts untouched.
$partCount = 0
if (-not $OnlyIcons) {
 $atlas = [Drawing.Bitmap]::FromFile((Join-Path $catDirectory 'rig-atlas.png'))
 $rectangles = [ordered]@{
  'head'=@(13,59,389,301); 'ear-left'=@(402,103,165,191); 'ear-right'=@(570,104,177,190)
  'eyes-open'=@(757,172,326,121); 'eyes-closed'=@(1109,205,327,94)
  'body'=@(29,395,371,306); 'tail'=@(427,400,328,298)
  'paw-left'=@(809,442,218,214); 'paw-right'=@(1152,440,217,213)
  'folder'=@(22,755,392,293); 'brows-sad'=@(472,864,228,51)
  'nose'=@(816,874,118,72); 'sleep'=@(1000,706,448,371)
 }
 try {
  foreach ($name in $rectangles.Keys) {
   $r=$rectangles[$name]
   $part=$atlas.Clone([Drawing.Rectangle]::new($r[0],$r[1],$r[2],$r[3]),[Drawing.Imaging.PixelFormat]::Format32bppArgb)
   try { $part.Save((Join-Path $catDirectory ($name+'.png')),[Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat]::Png) } finally { $part.Dispose() }
  }
  $partCount = $rectangles.Count
 } finally { $atlas.Dispose() }
}
$master = [Drawing.Bitmap]::FromFile((Join-Path $brandDirectory 'icon-master.png'))
$sizes=@(16,20,24,32,40,48,64,128,256)
$frames=[Collections.Generic.List[byte[]]]::new()
try {
 foreach ($size in $sizes) {
  $bitmap=[Drawing.Bitmap]::new($size,$size,[Drawing.Imaging.PixelFormat]::Format32bppArgb)
  $graphics=[Drawing.Graphics]::FromImage($bitmap)
  try {
   $graphics.Clear([Drawing.Color]::Transparent)
   $graphics.SmoothingMode=[Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode]::AntiAlias
   $graphics.InterpolationMode=[Drawing.Drawing2D.InterpolationMode]::HighQualityBicubic
   $graphics.PixelOffsetMode=[Drawing.Drawing2D.PixelOffsetMode]::HighQuality
   # Every size uses the supplied artwork; do not substitute a geometric miniature.
   $graphics.DrawImage($master,[Drawing.Rectangle]::new(0,0,$size,$size))
   $stream=[IO.MemoryStream]::new()
   try {
    $bitmap.Save($stream,[Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat]::Png)
    $frames.Add($stream.ToArray())
    $bitmap.Save((Join-Path $brandDirectory ('icon-'+$size+'.png')),[Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat]::Png)
    if ($size -eq 256) { $bitmap.Save((Join-Path (Split-Path $catDirectory -Parent) 'app.png'),[Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat]::Png) }
   } finally { $stream.Dispose() }
  } finally { $graphics.Dispose();$bitmap.Dispose() }
 }
} finally { $master.Dispose() }
$iconPath=Join-Path (Split-Path $catDirectory -Parent) 'app.ico'
$output=[IO.File]::Create($iconPath)
$writer=[IO.BinaryWriter]::new($output)
try {
 $writer.Write([uint16]0);$writer.Write([uint16]1);$writer.Write([uint16]$sizes.Count)
 $offset=6+16*$sizes.Count
 for ($index=0;$index -lt $sizes.Count;$index++) {
  $dimension=if($sizes[$index] -eq 256){0}else{$sizes[$index]}
  $writer.Write([byte]$dimension);$writer.Write([byte]$dimension);$writer.Write([byte]0);$writer.Write([byte]0)
  $writer.Write([uint16]1);$writer.Write([uint16]32);$writer.Write([uint32]$frames[$index].Length);$writer.Write([uint32]$offset)
  $offset+=$frames[$index].Length
 }
 foreach($frame in $frames){$writer.Write($frame)}
} finally { $writer.Dispose();$output.Dispose() }
Write-Output "Packed $partCount transparent parts and $($sizes.Count) icon sizes."


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/pack-sources.ps1 ---
param([string]$Version, [string]$OutputDirectory, [string]$StagingDirectory)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$sourceWorkspace = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent)).TrimEnd('\')
. (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'release-version.ps1')
$releaseInfo = Get-CatReleaseInfo -ProjectDirectory $sourceWorkspace -Version $Version
$sourceRoot = Join-Path $sourceWorkspace 'tools\ffmpeg\source'
$manifest = Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $sourceRoot 'distribution-manifest.json') -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
if (-not $manifest.sourceBundleCompleteForReviewedInventory -or $manifest.distributionLicense -ne 'GPL-3.0-or-later') { throw 'The reviewed corresponding-source inventory is incomplete.' }
$packageName = $releaseInfo.PackageName + '-Sources'
if (-not $StagingDirectory) { $StagingDirectory = Join-Path $sourceWorkspace 'artifacts\source-package' }
if (-not $OutputDirectory) { $OutputDirectory = Join-Path $sourceWorkspace 'release' }
$packageRoot = Join-Path ([IO.Path]::GetFullPath($StagingDirectory)) ([Guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N') + '\' + $packageName)
$packageSource = Join-Path $packageRoot 'tools\ffmpeg\source'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $packageSource | Out-Null
function Safe-SourcePath([string]$Root, [string]$Relative) {
  $prefix = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Root).TrimEnd('\') + '\'
  $absolute = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Join-Path $Root $Relative))
  if (-not $absolute.StartsWith($prefix, [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { throw "Source path escapes its root: $Relative" }
  return $absolute
}
foreach ($entry in $manifest.requiredFiles) {
  $inputPath = Safe-SourcePath $sourceRoot $entry.path
  if ((Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $inputPath -Algorithm SHA256).Hash -ine $entry.sha256) { throw "Corresponding-source hash mismatch: $($entry.path)" }
  $outputPath = Safe-SourcePath $packageSource $entry.path
  New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path (Split-Path $outputPath -Parent) | Out-Null
  Copy-Item -LiteralPath $inputPath -Destination $outputPath
}
Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $sourceRoot 'distribution-manifest.json') -Destination $packageSource
Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $sourceWorkspace 'tools\ffmpeg\licenses') -Destination (Join-Path $packageRoot 'tools\ffmpeg') -Recurse
Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $sourceWorkspace 'tools\ffmpeg\provenance.json'),(Join-Path $sourceWorkspace 'tools\ffmpeg\component-manifest.json'),(Join-Path $sourceWorkspace 'tools\ffmpeg\THIRD-PARTY-LICENSES.txt'),(Join-Path $sourceWorkspace 'tools\ffmpeg\LICENSE.txt') -Destination (Join-Path $packageRoot 'tools\ffmpeg')
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path (Join-Path $packageRoot 'scripts'),(Join-Path $packageRoot 'docs') | Out-Null
foreach ($scriptName in @('collect-ffmpeg-sources.ps1','inspect-ffmpeg-sources.py','audit-licenses.ps1','pack-sources.ps1')) {
  Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot $scriptName) -Destination (Join-Path $packageRoot 'scripts')
}
Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $sourceWorkspace 'THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md') -Destination $packageRoot
Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $sourceWorkspace 'docs\third-party-audit.md') -Destination (Join-Path $packageRoot 'docs')
# Include the application, acceptance suite, assets and rebuild instructions with the matching engine sources.
foreach ($sourceFolder in @('src','shared','tests','scripts')) {
  $folderRoot = Join-Path $sourceWorkspace $sourceFolder
  foreach ($sourceItem in (Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $folderRoot -Recurse -File | Where-Object { $_.FullName -notmatch '\\(bin|obj|__pycache__)\\' })) {
    $relative = $sourceItem.FullName.Substring($sourceWorkspace.Length + 1)
    $target = Safe-SourcePath $packageRoot $relative
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path (Split-Path $target -Parent) | Out-Null
    Copy-Item -LiteralPath $sourceItem.FullName -Destination $target -Force
  }
}
foreach ($name in @('CatFileAssistant.sln','Directory.Build.props','global.json','NuGet.Config','README.md')) {
  Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $sourceWorkspace $name) -Destination $packageRoot -Force
}
Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath (Join-Path $sourceWorkspace 'docs') -Filter '*.md' -File | Copy-Item -Destination (Join-Path $packageRoot 'docs') -Force
# Public source packages exclude private execution reports, screenshots and recordings.
$publicEvidenceDirectory = Join-Path $packageRoot 'docs\validation-evidence'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $publicEvidenceDirectory | Out-Null
Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $sourceWorkspace 'docs\validation-evidence\README.md') -Destination $publicEvidenceDirectory -Force
Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $sourceWorkspace 'tools\7zip') -Destination (Join-Path $packageRoot 'tools') -Recurse -Force
Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $sourceWorkspace 'tools\ffmpeg\bin') -Destination (Join-Path $packageRoot 'tools\ffmpeg') -Recurse -Force
$applicationSourceFiles = @()
foreach ($applicationFile in (Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $packageRoot -Recurse -File | Where-Object { $_.FullName -notlike ($packageSource + '\*') })) {
  $applicationSourceFiles += [ordered]@{ path = $applicationFile.FullName.Substring($packageRoot.Length + 1).Replace('\','/'); sha256 = (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $applicationFile.FullName -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant() }
}
[IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $packageRoot 'application-source-manifest.json'), ([ordered]@{ applicationVersion = $releaseInfo.Version; files = $applicationSourceFiles } | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 5), [Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))
$sourceArchive = Join-Path ([IO.Path]::GetFullPath($OutputDirectory)) ($packageName + '.zip')
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path (Split-Path $sourceArchive -Parent) | Out-Null
Compress-Archive -LiteralPath $packageRoot -DestinationPath $sourceArchive -CompressionLevel Optimal -Force
[IO.File]::WriteAllText($sourceArchive + '.sha256', (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $sourceArchive -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant() + '  ' + [IO.Path]::GetFileName($sourceArchive) + [Environment]::NewLine, [Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))
[pscustomobject]@{ archive=$sourceArchive; sourceRoot=$packageSource; requiredFiles=$manifest.requiredFiles.Count; bytes=(Get-Item -LiteralPath $sourceArchive).Length } | ConvertTo-Json


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/package-source.py ---
"""Package the lightweight public source tree; do not include installed dependencies."""
import pathlib,sys,zipfile,hashlib,json,re
ROOT=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
ROOT_FILES={'LICENSE','LICENSE-SCOPE.md','README.md','BUILD-STATUS.md','THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md','.gitignore','CatFileAssistant.sln','Directory.Build.props','global.json','NuGet.Config'}
TEXT_SUFFIXES={'.md','.txt','.json','.ps1','.py','.cs','.xaml','.csproj','.props','.proj','.config','.sln','.iss','.xml','.manifest','.svg'}
def selected(rel):
 p=pathlib.PurePosixPath(rel)
 if any(x in {'bin','obj','__pycache__','.git','.vs','.idea','.codex','.agents'} for x in p.parts):return False
 if p.suffix.lower() in {'.exe','.dll','.pdb','.log','.binlog','.dmp','.pfx','.p12','.key'} or p.name.startswith('.env'):return False
 if len(p.parts)==1:return rel in ROOT_FILES
 if p.parts[0] in {'src','shared','scripts','tests','docs'}:
  return not rel.startswith('docs/validation-evidence/') or rel=='docs/validation-evidence/README.md'
 if rel.startswith('tools/ffmpeg/licenses/'):
  return not rel.startswith('tools/ffmpeg/licenses/dependencies/')
 if rel.startswith(('tools/7zip/licenses/','tools/dotnet/licenses/')):return True
 if rel in {'tools/ffmpeg/provenance.json','tools/ffmpeg/THIRD-PARTY-LICENSES.txt','tools/ffmpeg/LICENSE.txt','tools/7zip/provenance.json','tools/7zip/cli-sha256.json'}:return True
 if rel.startswith('tools/ffmpeg/source/') and len(p.parts)==4:
  return p.name=='distribution-manifest.json' or p.name.startswith('FFmpeg-Builds-') and p.name.endswith('.tar.gz')
 return False
def main():
 output=pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1]).resolve()
 if output.exists():raise ValueError('Refusing to overwrite existing archive')
 files=sorted((p for p in ROOT.rglob('*') if p.is_file() and selected(p.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix())),key=lambda p:p.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix())
 output.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);manifest=[]
 with zipfile.ZipFile(output,'x',compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED,compresslevel=9) as z:
  def put(rel,data):
   info=zipfile.ZipInfo('YourCatFileAssistant/'+rel,(1980,1,1,0,0,0));info.compress_type=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED;info.external_attr=0o100644<<16;z.writestr(info,data,compresslevel=9)
  for p in files:
   rel=p.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix();data=p.read_bytes()
   if len(data)>100_000_000:raise ValueError('Source file exceeds 100 MB: '+rel)
   if not rel.startswith('tools/') and p.suffix.lower() in TEXT_SUFFIXES:
    text=data.decode('utf-8-sig');decoded=json.dumps(json.loads(text),ensure_ascii=False) if p.suffix=='.json' else text
    # Detect local developer roots without embedding this maintainer's own path in the published scanner.
    if re.search(r'(?i)[A-Z]:[\\/]+Users[\\/]+|[A-Z]:[\\/]+WindowsApps[\\/]+',decoded):raise ValueError('Personal local path found: '+rel)
   manifest.append({'path':rel,'bytes':len(data),'sha256':hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()});put(rel,data)
  put('source-package-manifest.json',(json.dumps({'applicationVersion':'1.0.2','variant':'lightweight-public-source','files':manifest},ensure_ascii=False,separators=(',',':'))+'\n').encode('utf-8'))
 with zipfile.ZipFile(output) as check_zip:
  extracted_bytes=sum(e.file_size for e in check_zip.infolist())
 if extracted_bytes>25_000_000:raise ValueError('Extracted source files exceed the strict 25 MB target')
 if output.stat().st_size>25_000_000:raise ValueError('Archive exceeds the strict 25 MB target')
 with output.open('rb') as f:digest=hashlib.file_digest(f,'sha256').hexdigest()
 output.with_suffix(output.suffix+'.sha256').write_text(digest+'  '+output.name+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
 print(json.dumps({'archive':output.name,'files':len(manifest)+1,'sourceBytes':sum(x['bytes'] for x in manifest),'extractedBytesIncludingManifest':extracted_bytes,'zipBytes':output.stat().st_size,'sha256':digest},indent=2))
if __name__=='__main__':main()


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/PetAnimationPreview/GALLERY.md ---
# Actual WPF action gallery

`--mode gallery` records the real V1.0.1 `PetSpriteView` for all 17 existing action names, plus any additional actions in the supplied v3 manifest (including `turn` when present). It uses normal `SetAnimation` requests and live player clocks. It never calls `SetFrameForTesting`, reconstructs sprite arrays or creates a controller/economy account.

Build/publish the recorder against the intended V1.0.1 product assemblies, retaining the preserved V1.0 recorder/runtime separately:

```powershell
./tools/dotnet/sdk/dotnet.exe publish scripts/PetAnimationPreview/PetAnimationPreview.csproj -c Release -r win-x64 --self-contained true -p:NuGetAudit=false '-p:ProductDirectory=..\..\src\CatFileAssistant.Desktop\bin\Release\net10.0-windows' -o artifacts/preview-v101-tool
./artifacts/preview-v101-tool/PetAnimationPreview.exe --mode gallery --manifest path/to/final/animations.json --output artifacts/preview-v101-gallery --label 'V1.0.1 · actual control'
```

Equipment is on by default. Add `--no-equipment` for the same action sequence without the collar/bell/star. The supplied manifest is used unchanged and cannot be combined with V1.0 comparison normalization.

Each action records for at least 3 seconds, extended to include its complete enter segment and one loop. Walking uses a 20 DIP/s monotonic QA distance input after the actual walk enter segment; the loop budget also covers a complete stride. This is an in-place action demonstration. Use the separate `--mode walk` recording to inspect actual native window displacement, landings and direction changes.

The player then receives an idle request. The exit observation budget includes a complete loop's possible wait for a valid marker, the longest normal/marker-specific exit, and a 250 ms scheduling margin. Legacy actions without an exit get a short idle observation. Randomized idle holds are observed from the live player before its cycle budget is finalized. Clips are not shortened to fit a 90-second video; the safety timeout is at least 180 seconds or the total planned sequence plus 20 seconds.

`recording-report.json` contains exact product DLL, manifest and every atlas SHA-256, action/segment definitions and duration plans, per-frame timestamps and lateness, requested and actually playing action, segment, pose index, source page/rectangle, anchors, foot contacts, equipment and render DPI. Manifest and atlas hashes are checked again after recording. Each PNG has its own SHA-256. `incompleteExits` records actions that failed to reach idle within the derived budget; a completed gallery with such failures returns a nonzero exit code. A partial report and error file are preserved on failure.

All captures are under `gallery/frame-000000.png`, in one sequence suitable for encoding with the bundled FFmpeg:

```powershell
./tools/ffmpeg/bin/ffmpeg.exe -framerate 30 -i artifacts/preview-v101-gallery/gallery/frame-%06d.png -c:v libx264 -pix_fmt yuv420p -crf 18 artifacts/preview-v101-gallery/gallery.mp4
```

The recorder targets 30 capture samples per second and preserves actual timestamps. Encoding them at 30 FPS does not certify physical display timing, smoothness, or 30 distinct drawn poses per second. Static pose approval, normal-speed motion review and native walk review remain separate acceptance steps. Only this tool's own control is captured, with no desktop or other application imagery.

For a metadata-only check with no window/control instance or animation, add `--gallery-plan-only`; it writes `gallery-plan.json` and exits. This does not certify any artwork or playback.


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/PetAnimationPreview/PetAnimationGallery.cs ---
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Globalization;
using System.IO;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;

/// <summary>Records the real player and its normal enter/loop/exit requests, never reconstructed sprite arrays.</summary>
internal static class PetAnimationGallery
{
    private const int Fps = 30;
    private const double WalkDipsPerSecond = 20;
    private static readonly string[] RequiredActions = { "idle", "blink", "groom", "stretch", "sleep", "walk", "happy", "feed", "play", "work", "success", "error", "drag", "play-wand", "feed-canned", "held", "sit" };
    private static readonly JsonSerializerOptions Json = new() { WriteIndented = true };
    private static readonly Brush Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(244, 246, 251));
    private static readonly Brush Ink = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(41, 47, 63));
    private static readonly Brush Muted = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(109, 117, 136));
    private static readonly BindingFlags InstanceFlags = BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.NonPublic;

    internal static int Run(string[] args)
    {
        string Option(string name, string? fallback = null)
        {
            var index = Array.IndexOf(args, name);
            return index >= 0 && index + 1 < args.Length ? args[index + 1] : fallback ?? throw new ArgumentException("Missing " + name);
        }
        if (args.Contains("--normalize-v10")) throw new ArgumentException("Gallery records the supplied manifest unchanged; do not use --normalize-v10.");
        var manifestPath = Path.GetFullPath(Option("--manifest"));
        var output = Path.GetFullPath(Option("--output"));
        if (Directory.Exists(output) && Directory.EnumerateFileSystemEntries(output).Any()) throw new IOException("Gallery output must be new or empty.");
        var label = Option("--label", "V1.0.1 · actual WPF action gallery");
        var equipment = !args.Contains("--no-equipment");
        using var document = JsonDocument.Parse(File.ReadAllText(manifestPath));
        if (document.RootElement.GetProperty("version").GetInt32() != 3) throw new InvalidDataException("Gallery requires the v3 production player manifest.");
        var definitions = document.RootElement.GetProperty("animations");
        foreach (var name in RequiredActions) if (!definitions.TryGetProperty(name, out _)) throw new InvalidDataException("Required gallery action is missing: " + name);
        var actions = RequiredActions.Concat(definitions.EnumerateObject().Select(item => item.Name).Except(RequiredActions, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)).ToArray();
        var plans = actions.Select(action => Plan(action, definitions.GetProperty(action))).ToArray();
        var maximumCaptureSeconds = Math.Max(180, plans.Sum(plan => plan.ActionSeconds + plan.ExitSeconds) + 20);
        var manifestHash = Hash(manifestPath);
        var atlasHashes = AtlasHashes(document.RootElement, manifestPath);
        var segmentDefinitions = definitions.EnumerateObject().Select(item => new
        {
            action = item.Name,
            segments = item.Value.TryGetProperty("segments", out var segments) ? segments.EnumerateObject().Select(segment => segment.Name).ToArray() : new[] { "legacy-loop" }
        }).ToArray();
        Directory.CreateDirectory(output);
        if (args.Contains("--gallery-plan-only"))
        {
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(output, "gallery-plan.json"), JsonSerializer.Serialize(new
            {
                mode = "gallery-plan", actualWpfCaptured = false, manifestPath, manifestHash, atlasHashes,
                equipment, actions, plans, maximumCaptureSeconds,
                plannedSeconds = plans.Sum(plan => plan.ActionSeconds + plan.ExitSeconds),
                note = "Metadata planning only; no window, sprite instance, animation, anatomy or performance validation."
            }, Json));
            Console.WriteLine(Path.Combine(output, "gallery-plan.json"));
            return 0;
        }
        var frameDirectory = Path.Combine(output, "gallery"); Directory.CreateDirectory(frameDirectory);
        var samples = new List<object>(); var scenes = new List<object>(); var completions = new List<object>();
        var app = new Application { ShutdownMode = ShutdownMode.OnExplicitShutdown };
        var result = 0;
        app.Startup += async (_, _) =>
        {
            using var sprite = new PetSpriteView { Width = 224, Height = 224 };
            var host = new Grid { Width = 224, Height = 224 }; host.Children.Add(sprite);
            var window = new Window { Title = "Actual cat action gallery", Content = host, Width = 270, Height = 300, Topmost = true, ShowActivated = false, ShowInTaskbar = false };
            var watch = new Stopwatch(); var frameNumber = 0;
            var incompleteExits = new List<string>(); var finished = false;
            var updateWalk = typeof(PetSpriteView).GetMethod("UpdateWalkDistance", InstanceFlags);
            var segmentProperty = typeof(PetSpriteView).GetProperty("ActiveSegmentName", InstanceFlags);
            var poseProperty = typeof(PetSpriteView).GetProperty("CurrentFrameDiagnostics", InstanceFlags);
            var actionField = typeof(PetSpriteView).GetField("activeAction", InstanceFlags);
            try
            {
                if (updateWalk is null || segmentProperty is null || poseProperty is null || actionField is null)
                    throw new InvalidOperationException("Gallery must run with the actual V1.0.1 player and its segment/pose diagnostics.");
                sprite.LoadManifest(manifestPath); sprite.SetMotionMode(PetMotionMode.Gentle); sprite.SetAccessories(equipment, equipment);
                sprite.AnimationCompleted += () => completions.Add(new { elapsedMilliseconds = watch.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds, requestedAnimation = sprite.Animation, playingAction = PlayingAction(sprite), segment = Segment(sprite) });
                window.Show(); window.UpdateLayout(); await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.Render);
                if (!sprite.HasSpriteAtlas || sprite.AssetError is not null) throw new InvalidDataException("Gallery did not load the supplied artwork: " + sprite.AssetError);
                watch.Start();
                for (var sceneIndex = 0; sceneIndex < actions.Length; sceneIndex++)
                {
                    var action = actions[sceneIndex]; var plan = plans[sceneIndex]; var scene = Stopwatch.StartNew();
                    var actionSeconds = plan.ActionSeconds;
                    var firstFrame = frameNumber; var idleRequested = false; double? walkingStarted = null; var walkDistance = 0d;
                    sprite.SetAnimation(action);
                    if (action == "walk") updateWalk.Invoke(sprite, new object[] { 0d });
                    // Idle randomizes only its initial hold; observe that real runtime
                    // value rather than shortening the player's first complete cycle.
                    if (action == "idle" && typeof(PetSpriteView).GetField("idleHoldMilliseconds", InstanceFlags)?.GetValue(sprite) is double actualHold)
                        actionSeconds += Math.Max(0, actualHold - plan.FirstLoopFrameMilliseconds) / 1000;
                    var count = (int)Math.Ceiling((actionSeconds + plan.ExitSeconds) * Fps);
                    var observedSegments = new HashSet<string>();
                    for (var localFrame = 0; localFrame < count; localFrame++)
                    {
                        var due = localFrame * 1000d / Fps;
                        var delay = due - scene.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds;
                        if (delay > 0) await Task.Delay(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(delay));
                        if (watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds >= maximumCaptureSeconds) throw new TimeoutException("Gallery exceeded its metadata-derived capture timeout; the partial report is retained.");
                        var elapsed = scene.Elapsed.TotalSeconds;
                        if (!idleRequested && elapsed >= actionSeconds) { idleRequested = true; sprite.SetAnimation("idle"); }
                        if (action == "walk" && !idleRequested && Segment(sprite) == "loop" && PlayingAction(sprite) == "walk")
                        {
                            walkingStarted ??= elapsed;
                            walkDistance = Math.Max(0, elapsed - walkingStarted.Value) * WalkDipsPerSecond;
                            updateWalk.Invoke(sprite, new object[] { walkDistance });
                        }
                        await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.Render);
                        if (sprite.AssetError is not null) throw new InvalidDataException(sprite.AssetError);
                        var actualSegment = Segment(sprite); observedSegments.Add(PlayingAction(sprite) + ":" + actualSegment);
                        var timestamp = watch.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds; var captureSceneMilliseconds = scene.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds;
                        var pose = poseProperty.GetValue(sprite);
                        var file = Path.Combine(frameDirectory, $"frame-{frameNumber:D6}.png");
                        Save(Capture(host, label, action, sceneIndex, actions.Length, actualSegment, sprite.RenderedFrameIndex, timestamp, idleRequested), file);
                        samples.Add(new
                        {
                            frame = frameNumber, sceneIndex, requestedAction = action, elapsedMilliseconds = timestamp,
                            sceneElapsedMilliseconds = captureSceneMilliseconds, intendedSceneMilliseconds = due,
                            captureLatenessMilliseconds = Math.Max(0, captureSceneMilliseconds - due), saveCompletedSceneMilliseconds = scene.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds,
                            requestedAnimation = sprite.Animation, playingAction = PlayingAction(sprite), segment = actualSegment,
                            frameIndex = sprite.RenderedFrameIndex, frameTimer = sprite.ActiveFrameTimerCount,
                            transitionClocks = sprite.ActiveTransitionClockCount, ambientClocks = sprite.ActiveAmbientClockCount,
                            idleRequested, walkDistanceInputDips = action == "walk" ? walkDistance : (double?)null,
                            pose = DescribePose(pose), file = Path.GetRelativePath(output, file).Replace('\\', '/'), sha256 = Hash(file)
                        });
                        if (frameNumber == 0) File.Copy(file, Path.Combine(output, "gallery-first.png"));
                        frameNumber++;
                    }
                    var exited = PlayingAction(sprite) == "idle";
                    if (!exited) incompleteExits.Add(action);
                    scenes.Add(new { action, sceneIndex, firstFrame, lastFrame = frameNumber - 1, actionSeconds, exitSeconds = plan.ExitSeconds,
                        actualSceneMilliseconds = scene.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds, observedSegments = observedSegments.ToArray(), exitReachedIdle = exited });
                    SaveReport(false);
                    Console.WriteLine($"Gallery {sceneIndex + 1}/{actions.Length}: {action}; idle exit={exited}.");
                }
                if (Hash(manifestPath) != manifestHash || !atlasHashes.SequenceEqual(AtlasHashes(document.RootElement, manifestPath)))
                    throw new InvalidDataException("The manifest or atlas bytes changed during recording; do not mix evidence from different art versions.");
                finished = true;
                if (incompleteExits.Count != 0) result = 1;
                File.Copy(Path.Combine(frameDirectory, $"frame-{frameNumber - 1:D6}.png"), Path.Combine(output, "gallery-last.png"));
                SaveReport(result == 0);
                Console.WriteLine($"Gallery complete: {frameNumber} actual WPF captures in {watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds:F3}s; report {Path.Combine(output, "recording-report.json")}");
            }
            catch (Exception error)
            {
                result = 1; File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(output, "recording-error.txt"), error.ToString());
                SaveReport(false); Console.Error.WriteLine(error);
            }
            finally { window.Close(); app.Shutdown(result); }

            string Segment(PetSpriteView value) => segmentProperty?.GetValue(value)?.ToString() ?? "unavailable";
            string PlayingAction(PetSpriteView value)
            {
                var current = actionField?.GetValue(value);
                return current?.GetType().GetProperty("Name", InstanceFlags)?.GetValue(current)?.ToString() ?? "unavailable";
            }
            void SaveReport(bool success)
            {
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(output, "recording-report.json"), JsonSerializer.Serialize(new
                {
                    success, finished, mode = "gallery", capturedAtUtc = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow, label, equipment,
                    actualWpfControls = true, generatedAnimationFrames = false, fullDesktopCaptured = false,
                    sourceManifest = manifestPath, sourceManifestSha256 = manifestHash, atlasFiles = atlasHashes,
                    requestedFps = Fps, maximumCaptureSeconds, plans, walkDipsPerSecond = WalkDipsPerSecond,
                    elapsedMilliseconds = watch.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds, actions, segmentDefinitions, scenes, samples, completions, incompleteExits,
                    renderDpi = new { x = VisualTreeHelper.GetDpi(window).PixelsPerInchX, y = VisualTreeHelper.GetDpi(window).PixelsPerInchY },
                    productAssemblies = new[] { typeof(PetSpriteView).Assembly, typeof(PetMotionMode).Assembly, Assembly.Load("CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure") }
                        .Select(assembly => new { name = assembly.GetName().Name, version = assembly.GetName().Version?.ToString(), path = assembly.Location, sha256 = Hash(assembly.Location) }),
                    runtime = System.Runtime.InteropServices.RuntimeEnvironment.GetRuntimeDirectory(),
                    notes = new[] {
                        "Every PNG samples the live visible production PetSpriteView with its own elapsed-time clocks; SetFrameForTesting is never called.",
                        "Each action is requested normally, followed by an idle request that lets the real player choose its exit segment and transition marker. Missing exits are reported, not hidden.",
                        "Action budgets cover the metadata's enter segment and one complete loop, with at least3seconds per action. Exit budgets cover a full marker-wait loop plus the longest exit variant and250ms scheduling margin. No clip is shortened to fit an arbitrary video length.",
                        "The walk gallery uses a monotonic QA distance input at20DIP/s once its real enter segment finishes; this is an in-place action demonstration, not evidence of actual native window displacement. Use --mode walk for the native walking record.",
                        "Whole-body artwork, alpha, anchors and vector equipment are rendered by the product. No animation frames or intermediate anatomy are synthesized by this recorder.",
                        "Video may encode these PNGs at30FPS; actual timestamps/lateness are preserved. That does not claim30distinct drawn poses per second or certify perceived continuity.",
                        "Only this tool's own pet control is sampled; no desktop, other application, settings, wallet or economy controller is accessed." }
                }, Json));
            }
        };
        return app.Run();
    }

    private sealed record ScenePlan(string Action, double ActionSeconds, double ExitSeconds, double EnterMilliseconds,
        double LoopMilliseconds, double MaximumExitMilliseconds, double FirstLoopFrameMilliseconds, string BudgetMethod);
    private static ScenePlan Plan(string name, JsonElement definition)
    {
        double Number(JsonElement element, string property, double fallback) => element.TryGetProperty(property, out var value) ? value.GetDouble() : fallback;
        double[] FrameDurations(JsonElement segment)
        {
            var interval = Number(segment, "frameMs", Number(definition, "frameMs", 1000d / 30));
            return segment.GetProperty("frames").EnumerateArray().Select(frame => Math.Max(33, Number(frame, "ms", interval))).ToArray();
        }
        var enter = 0d; var maximumExit = 0d; double[] loopFrames;
        if (definition.TryGetProperty("segments", out var segments))
        {
            loopFrames = FrameDurations(segments.GetProperty("loop"));
            if (segments.TryGetProperty("enter", out var incoming)) enter = FrameDurations(incoming).Sum();
            if (segments.TryGetProperty("exit", out var outgoing)) maximumExit = FrameDurations(outgoing).Sum();
            if (segments.TryGetProperty("exitByMarker", out var variants))
                foreach (var variant in variants.EnumerateObject()) maximumExit = Math.Max(maximumExit, FrameDurations(variant.Value).Sum());
        }
        else
        {
            var frameCountValue = definition.GetProperty("frames");
            var count = frameCountValue.ValueKind == JsonValueKind.Array ? frameCountValue.GetArrayLength() : frameCountValue.GetInt32();
            loopFrames = definition.TryGetProperty("frameDurationsMs", out var durations)
                ? durations.EnumerateArray().Select(frame => Math.Max(33, frame.GetDouble())).ToArray()
                : Enumerable.Repeat(Math.Max(33, Number(definition, "frameMs", 160)), count).ToArray();
        }
        if (loopFrames.Length == 0) throw new InvalidDataException("No frames for action " + name);
        var loop = loopFrames.Sum();
        if (name == "walk") loop = Math.Max(loop, Number(definition, "strideLength", 48) / WalkDipsPerSecond * 1000);
        return new ScenePlan(name, Math.Max(3, (enter + loop) / 1000 + .25),
            maximumExit > 0 ? (loop + maximumExit) / 1000 + .25 : .5,
            enter, loop, maximumExit, loopFrames[0], "enter + full loop; exit wait <= full loop + longest exit variant; 250ms scheduling margin; idle randomized hold observed at runtime");
    }

    private sealed record AtlasFingerprint(string File, string Path, string Sha256);
    private static AtlasFingerprint[] AtlasHashes(JsonElement manifest, string path)
    {
        var root = Path.GetDirectoryName(path)!; var prefix = root.TrimEnd(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar) + Path.DirectorySeparatorChar;
        return manifest.GetProperty("atlases").EnumerateArray().Select(atlas =>
        {
            var relative = atlas.GetProperty("file").GetString() ?? throw new InvalidDataException("Missing atlas path.");
            var file = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(root, relative));
            if (Path.IsPathRooted(relative) || !file.StartsWith(prefix, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) throw new InvalidDataException("Atlas escaped its manifest directory.");
            return new AtlasFingerprint(relative, file, Hash(file));
        }).ToArray();
    }
    private static object? DescribePose(object? pose)
    {
        if (pose is null) return null;
        object? Property(string name) => pose.GetType().GetProperty(name, InstanceFlags)?.GetValue(pose);
        object? Rectangle(object? value) => value switch
        {
            Int32Rect rectangle => new { x = (double)rectangle.X, y = (double)rectangle.Y, width = (double)rectangle.Width, height = (double)rectangle.Height },
            Rect rectangle => new { x = rectangle.X, y = rectangle.Y, width = rectangle.Width, height = rectangle.Height },
            _ => null
        };
        object? Vector(object? value) => value is Point point ? new { x = point.X, y = point.Y } : null;
        var canvas = Property("CanvasSize") is Size size ? new { width = size.Width, height = size.Height } : null;
        return new { atlasFile = Property("AtlasFile"), sourceRect = Rectangle(Property("SourceRect")), canvas,
            placement = Rectangle(Property("Placement")), head = Vector(Property("Head")), neck = Vector(Property("Neck")),
            headAngle = Property("HeadAngle"), neckAngle = Property("NeckAngle"), accessoryScale = Property("AccessoryScale"),
            contact = Property("Contact"), feet = (Property("Feet") as IEnumerable<Point>)?.Select(point => new { point.X, point.Y }).ToArray() };
    }
    private static BitmapSource Capture(Visual control, string label, string action, int index, int count, string segment, int pose, double elapsed, bool exiting)
    {
        var visual = new DrawingVisual();
        using (var drawing = visual.RenderOpen())
        {
            drawing.DrawRectangle(Background, null, new Rect(0, 0, 480, 360));
            drawing.DrawText(Text(label, 17, Ink), new Point(18, 14));
            drawing.DrawText(Text($"{index + 1:D2}/{count:D2}  {action}  ·  {(exiting ? "stop → idle" : "action")}", 13, Ink), new Point(18, 42));
            drawing.DrawRectangle(new VisualBrush(control) { Stretch = Stretch.Fill }, null, new Rect(128, 77, 224, 224));
            drawing.DrawText(Text($"{segment} · pose {pose} · {elapsed / 1000:0.00} s", 12, Muted), new Point(18, 324));
        }
        var bitmap = new RenderTargetBitmap(480, 360, 96, 96, PixelFormats.Pbgra32); bitmap.Render(visual); bitmap.Freeze(); return bitmap;
    }
    private static FormattedText Text(string value, double size, Brush brush) => new(value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, FlowDirection.LeftToRight, new Typeface("Segoe UI"), size, brush, 1);
    private static void Save(BitmapSource image, string file)
    {
        var encoder = new PngBitmapEncoder(); encoder.Frames.Add(BitmapFrame.Create(image)); using var stream = File.Create(file); encoder.Save(stream);
    }
    private static string Hash(string path) => Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(File.ReadAllBytes(path))).ToLowerInvariant();
}

--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/PetAnimationPreview/Program.cs ---
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Globalization;
using System.IO;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.Json.Nodes;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;
using System.Windows.Threading;
using CatFileAssistant.Core;
using CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet;

// This recorder deliberately uses only APIs already public in the released V1.0
// product. The exact same recorder runs against isolated V1.0 and V1.0.1 binaries.
internal static class Program
{
    private const int Fps = 30;
    private static readonly Brush backdrop = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(244, 246, 251));
    private static readonly Brush ink = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(41, 47, 63));
    private static readonly Brush muted = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(109, 117, 136));
    private static readonly JsonSerializerOptions json = new() { WriteIndented = true };

    [STAThread]
    private static int Main(string[] args)
    {
        var requestedMode = Array.IndexOf(args, "--mode");
        if (requestedMode >= 0 && requestedMode + 1 < args.Length && args[requestedMode + 1] == "gallery") return PetAnimationGallery.Run(args);
        string Option(string name, string? fallback = null)
        {
            var index = Array.IndexOf(args, name);
            return index >= 0 && index + 1 < args.Length ? args[index + 1] : fallback ?? throw new ArgumentException("Missing " + name);
        }
        var sourceManifest = Path.GetFullPath(Option("--manifest"));
        var output = Path.GetFullPath(Option("--output"));
        if (Directory.Exists(output) && Directory.EnumerateFileSystemEntries(output).Any()) throw new IOException("Recording requires a new or empty output directory.");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(output);
        var label = Option("--label", "Animation sample");
        var mode = Option("--mode", "all");
        if (mode is not ("all" or "work" or "work-transition" or "walk")) throw new ArgumentException("Mode must be all, work, work-transition or walk.");
        var normalizeOld = args.Contains("--normalize-v10");
        var accessories = args.Contains("--equipment");
        var manifest = normalizeOld ? NormalizeOldManifest(sourceManifest, output) : sourceManifest;
        var app = new Application { ShutdownMode = ShutdownMode.OnExplicitShutdown };
        var exitCode = 0;
        app.Startup += async (_, _) =>
        {
            var captures = new List<object>();
            try
            {
                if (mode is "all" or "work") await RecordWorkAsync(manifest, output, label, accessories, captures);
                if (mode == "work-transition") await RecordWorkAsync(manifest, output, label, accessories, captures, true);
                if (mode is "all" or "walk") await RecordWalkAsync(manifest, output, label, accessories, captures);
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(output, "recording-report.json"), JsonSerializer.Serialize(new
                {
                    success = true, capturedAtUtc = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow, label, mode, requestedFps = Fps,
                    actualWpfControls = true, generatedAnimationFrames = false, fullDesktopCaptured = false,
                    sourceManifest, sourceManifestSha256 = Hash(sourceManifest), playbackManifest = manifest,
                    playbackManifestSha256 = Hash(manifest), v10CycleNormalization = normalizeOld, equipment = accessories,
                    productAssemblies = new[] { typeof(PetSpriteView).Assembly, typeof(PetMotionMode).Assembly, Assembly.Load("CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure") }
                        .Select(assembly => new { name = assembly.GetName().Name, version = assembly.GetName().Version?.ToString(), path = assembly.Location, sha256 = Hash(assembly.Location) }),
                    runtime = System.Runtime.InteropServices.RuntimeEnvironment.GetRuntimeDirectory(), captures,
                    notes = new[] {
                        "Every PNG samples the live production WPF control, with its real animation clocks running; no sprite frame arrays are reconstructed by this tool.",
                        "Only the pet window/control is sampled using a VisualBrush into a fixed QA viewport. Other applications and the desktop are never captured.",
                        "V1.0 artwork and DLLs are unchanged. The optional copied comparison manifest changes only work timing to 2000 ms per six poses and walk timing to 1000 ms per six poses.",
                        "Desktop native positions are observed on each capture; the window's own visual includes the actual child mirroring and accessory rendering.",
                        "Capture timestamps, frame indexes, source hashes and motion requests are retained. Video output uses 30 fps; inspect timestamps to assess capture scheduling.",
                        "This tool creates no MainWindow, PetController or economy account, and never reads or writes the user's settings or wallet." }
                }, json));
                Console.WriteLine("PASS live WPF sample: " + output);
            }
            catch (Exception error)
            {
                exitCode = 1; Console.Error.WriteLine(error);
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(output, "recording-error.txt"), error.ToString());
            }
            finally { app.Shutdown(exitCode); }
        };
        return app.Run();
    }

    private static async Task RecordWorkAsync(string manifest, string output, string label, bool equipment, List<object> captures, bool stop = false)
    {
        using var sprite = new PetSpriteView { Width = 200, Height = 200 };
        sprite.LoadManifest(manifest); sprite.SetMotionMode(PetMotionMode.Gentle); sprite.SetAccessories(equipment, equipment);
        var host = new Grid { Width = 200, Height = 200 }; host.Children.Add(sprite);
        var window = new Window { Title = "Cat animation QA", Content = host, Width = 250, Height = 260, Topmost = true, ShowActivated = false };
        window.Show();
        try
        {
            window.UpdateLayout(); await Task.Delay(200);
            Check(sprite.HasSpriteAtlas && sprite.AssetError is null, "Work atlas failed to load: " + sprite.AssetError);
            sprite.SetAnimation("work");
            var stopRequested = false;
            await RecordAsync(stop ? "work-transition" : "work", stop ? 300 : 180, output, async (index, elapsed) =>
            {
                if (stop && !stopRequested && elapsed >= 4000) { stopRequested = true; sprite.SetAnimation("idle"); }
                await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.Render);
                return (Capture(host, new Rect(140, 52, 200, 200), label, stop ? "WORK · entry, 2-second loop, stop and exit" : "WORK · 2-second loop", elapsed),
                    new { animation = sprite.Animation, frameIndex = sprite.RenderedFrameIndex, frameTimer = sprite.ActiveFrameTimerCount, transitionClocks = sprite.ActiveTransitionClockCount, stopRequested });
            }, captures);
            Check(sprite.Animation == (stop ? "idle" : "work"), "The work recording did not finish its requested animation.");
        }
        finally { window.Close(); }
    }

    private static async Task RecordWalkAsync(string manifest, string output, string label, bool equipment, List<object> captures)
    {
        using var pet = new DesktopPetWindow();
        pet.Configure(false); pet.LoadSprites(manifest); pet.SetMotionMode(PetMotionMode.Gentle); pet.SetSize(160);
        pet.SpriteView.SetAccessories(equipment, equipment);
        var primary = DesktopEnvironment.GetMonitors().First(monitor => monitor.Primary);
        pet.SetPosition(primary.WorkArea.Left + 80 * primary.DpiScaleX, primary.WorkArea.Top + 90 * primary.DpiScaleY);
        pet.SetEnvironmentVisible(true); pet.UpdateLayout(); await Task.Delay(250);
        Check(pet.SpriteView.HasSpriteAtlas && pet.SpriteView.AssetError is null, "Walk atlas failed to load: " + pet.SpriteView.AssetError);
        var origin = pet.Position;
        var requests = new List<object>();
        var rightStarted = false; var leftStarted = false;
        await RecordAsync("walk", 540, output, async (index, elapsed) =>
        {
            if (!rightStarted && index >= 21)
            {
                rightStarted = true; var accepted = pet.TryWalk(96); requests.Add(new { direction = "right", requestedDips = 96, elapsedMilliseconds = elapsed, accepted }); Check(accepted, "Right walk did not start.");
            }
            if (!leftStarted && elapsed >= 8000 && !pet.IsWalking)
            {
                leftStarted = true; var accepted = pet.TryWalk(-96); requests.Add(new { direction = "left", requestedDips = -96, elapsedMilliseconds = elapsed, accepted }); Check(accepted, "Left walk did not start after normal landing.");
            }
            await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.Render);
            var position = pet.Position;
            var x = 140 + (position.X - origin.X) / primary.DpiScaleX;
            var y = 85 + (position.Y - origin.Y) / primary.DpiScaleY;
            return (Capture(pet, new Rect(x, y, 160, 160), label, "WALK · native start, landing and turn", elapsed),
                new { animation = pet.SpriteView.Animation, frameIndex = pet.SpriteView.RenderedFrameIndex, position,
                    xDips = (position.X - origin.X) / primary.DpiScaleX, mirrored = pet.SpriteView.RenderTransform.Value.M11 < 0,
                    frameTimer = pet.SpriteView.ActiveFrameTimerCount, transitionClocks = pet.SpriteView.ActiveTransitionClockCount });
        }, captures, () => new { requests, origin, final = pet.Position, actualMonitorDpiScaleX = primary.DpiScaleX, actualMonitorDpiScaleY = primary.DpiScaleY });
        Check(leftStarted && !pet.IsWalking, "The walking recording did not complete both requested walks and normal landings.");
        pet.SetEnvironmentVisible(false);
    }

    private static async Task RecordAsync(string name, int count, string output, Func<int, double, Task<(BitmapSource Image, object State)>> capture, List<object> captures, Func<object>? summary = null)
    {
        var directory = Path.Combine(output, name); Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        var samples = new List<object>(); var watch = Stopwatch.StartNew(); var lateness = new List<double>();
        for (var index = 0; index < count; index++)
        {
            var due = index * 1000d / Fps; var wait = due - watch.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds;
            if (wait > 0) await Task.Delay(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(wait));
            var (image, state) = await capture(index, watch.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds);
            var elapsed = watch.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds; lateness.Add(Math.Max(0, elapsed - due));
            var path = Path.Combine(directory, $"frame-{index:D4}.png");
            var encoder = new PngBitmapEncoder(); encoder.Frames.Add(BitmapFrame.Create(image)); using (var file = File.Create(path)) encoder.Save(file);
            samples.Add(new { frame = index, elapsedMilliseconds = elapsed, intendedMilliseconds = due, state, image.PixelWidth, image.PixelHeight, sha256 = Hash(path) });
            if (index is 0 || index == count - 1) File.Copy(path, Path.Combine(output, name + (index == 0 ? "-first.png" : "-last.png")));
        }
        captures.Add(new { name, count, targetSeconds = count / (double)Fps, elapsedMilliseconds = watch.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds,
            maximumCaptureLatenessMilliseconds = lateness.Max(), medianCaptureLatenessMilliseconds = lateness.Order().ElementAt(lateness.Count / 2), samples, summary = summary?.Invoke() });
        Console.WriteLine($"Captured {name}: {count} actual WPF samples in {watch.Elapsed.TotalSeconds:F3}s.");
    }

    private static BitmapSource Capture(Visual liveVisual, Rect target, string label, string subtitle, double elapsed)
    {
        var visual = new DrawingVisual();
        using (var drawing = visual.RenderOpen())
        {
            drawing.DrawRectangle(backdrop, null, new Rect(0, 0, 480, 320));
            drawing.DrawText(Text(label, 18, ink), new Point(18, 14));
            drawing.DrawText(Text(subtitle, 12, muted), new Point(18, 39));
            drawing.DrawRectangle(new VisualBrush(liveVisual) { Stretch = Stretch.Fill }, null, target);
            drawing.DrawText(Text("Live WPF control · " + (elapsed / 1000).ToString("0.00", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) + " s", 11, muted), new Point(18, 290));
        }
        var bitmap = new RenderTargetBitmap(480, 320, 96, 96, PixelFormats.Pbgra32); bitmap.Render(visual); bitmap.Freeze(); return bitmap;
    }
    private static FormattedText Text(string value, double size, Brush brush) => new(value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, FlowDirection.LeftToRight, new Typeface("Segoe UI"), size, brush, 1);

    private static string NormalizeOldManifest(string source, string output)
    {
        var node = JsonNode.Parse(File.ReadAllText(source))?.AsObject() ?? throw new InvalidDataException("Missing old manifest.");
        if (node["version"]?.GetValue<int>() != 2) throw new InvalidDataException("Comparison normalization only supports the released V1.0 version2 manifest.");
        var directory = Path.Combine(output, "normalized-v10-manifest"); Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        foreach (var atlas in node["atlases"]!.AsArray())
        {
            var relative = atlas!["file"]!.GetValue<string>();
            if (Path.IsPathRooted(relative) || relative.Contains("..", StringComparison.Ordinal)) throw new InvalidDataException("Unexpected atlas path.");
            var destination = Path.Combine(directory, relative); Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(destination)!);
            File.Copy(Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(source)!, relative), destination);
        }
        var actions = node["animations"]!.AsObject();
        Check(actions["work"]!["frames"]!.GetValue<int>() == 6 && actions["walk"]!["frames"]!.GetValue<int>() == 6, "Old comparison no longer has six poses.");
        actions["work"]!["frameMs"] = 333; actions["work"]!["frameDurationsMs"] = new JsonArray(333, 333, 334, 333, 333, 334);
        actions["walk"]!["frameMs"] = 166; actions["walk"]!["frameDurationsMs"] = new JsonArray(166, 167, 167, 166, 167, 167);
        var path = Path.Combine(directory, "animations.json"); File.WriteAllText(path, node.ToJsonString(json)); return path;
    }
    private static string Hash(string path) => Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(File.ReadAllBytes(path))).ToLowerInvariant();
    private static void Check(bool passed, string message) { if (!passed) throw new InvalidOperationException(message); }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/PetAnimationPreview/README.md ---
# Live pet animation comparison recorder

This WPF tool records the actual `PetSpriteView` and `DesktopPetWindow`, using only APIs that are public in the released V1.0 product. It does not construct the application's account/controller or read the user's settings. Its fixed viewport samples only the pet control/window; it never captures the desktop or another application.

Build with the bundled SDK, then publish self-contained with runtime 10.0.12. `ProductDirectory` selects the product assemblies used for compilation. By default it points to the preserved V1.0 release; a source-only checkout can pass the directory produced by `scripts/build.ps1` instead.

```powershell
./tools/dotnet/sdk/dotnet.exe publish scripts/PetAnimationPreview/PetAnimationPreview.csproj -c Release -r win-x64 --self-contained true -p:NuGetAudit=false -o artifacts/preview-tool
./artifacts/preview-tool/PetAnimationPreview.exe --manifest release/你的猫咪文件助手-V1.0-Windows-x64/app/Assets/Pet/animations.json --output artifacts/preview-v10 --label 'V1.0 · same cycle' --normalize-v10 --equipment
```

For a newer product, copy the published recorder to a separate runtime directory, replace only `YourCatFileAssistant.dll`, `CatFileAssistant.Core.dll` and `CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure.dll` with the intended product's matching files, and pass its manifest. Keep the old runtime and release untouched. Do not pass `--normalize-v10` for a version3 manifest.

The optional V1.0 normalization copies its atlas and manifest into the output directory. It changes only work timing to six poses totalling 2000 ms and walk timing to six poses totalling 1000 ms. It never alters released art or binaries. Work is recorded for six seconds. Walking is recorded for twelve seconds, including actual native 96-DIP walks in both directions, normal landings and the available turn animation. At 160-DIP pet size, movement itself takes about 2.4 seconds per direction; entry/turn/exit can add time.

`--mode work` or `--mode walk` selects a single scene. The default is both. `--mode work-transition` records ten seconds: the real work entry and two-second work loop, an idle request at four seconds, the marker-matched exit, and the destination idle pose. The original six-second `work` scene remains unchanged for comparisons. `--equipment` displays the production collar/bell and star clip. Output must be new or empty, so previous recordings cannot be overwritten accidentally.

Walking now records eighteen seconds to include the gentler V1.0.1 movement pace. The first 96-DIP request starts at about 0.7 seconds. The opposite request is made only after eight seconds and after the previous native walk has finished; both walks and normal landings must finish before recording succeeds. Use the same recorder for the preserved V1.0 baseline and the new product. Earlier twelve-second historical captures remain unchanged.

Every frame has its actual capture timestamp, frame index, animation name, native position where relevant, and SHA-256 in `recording-report.json`. The report also records exact loaded product DLL hashes, manifest hashes, runtime location and capture lateness. PNGs come from independently sampled live WPF rendering, rather than reconstructed sprite arrays. Video encoders can play these captured PNGs at 30 FPS; timestamps preserve the limits of the actual capture schedule.

Twelve or twenty-four drawn key poses remain twelve or twenty-four source drawings. A 30-FPS capture does not establish 30 distinct drawn poses per second. Character consistency, prop stability, loop seams and accessory placement require visual review in addition to these recordings.

--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/PetAtlasPackager/PetFrameProof.cs ---
using System.Collections;
using System.Globalization;
using System.IO;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Runtime.Loader;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;
using System.Windows.Threading;

namespace PetAtlasPackager;

/// <summary>Captures the real product control, including its vector accessories, on an opaque background.</summary>
internal static class PetFrameProof
{
    internal static int Run(IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> options)
    {
        string Required(string key) => options.TryGetValue(key, out var value) ? value : throw new ArgumentException("Missing --" + key);
        var product = Path.GetFullPath(Required("wpf-proof-product")); var manifest = Path.GetFullPath(Required("manifest"));
        var output = Path.GetFullPath(Required("output")); var action = Required("action");
        if (Directory.Exists(output) && Directory.EnumerateFileSystemEntries(output).Any()) throw new IOException("Proof output must be new or empty.");
        var directory = Path.GetDirectoryName(product)!;
        Assembly? Resolve(AssemblyLoadContext context, AssemblyName name)
        {
            var candidate = Path.Combine(directory, name.Name + ".dll"); return File.Exists(candidate) ? context.LoadFromAssemblyPath(candidate) : null;
        }
        AssemblyLoadContext.Default.Resolving += Resolve;
        var assembly = AssemblyLoadContext.Default.LoadFromAssemblyPath(product);
        var type = assembly.GetType("CatFileAssistant.Desktop.Pet.PetSpriteView", true)!;
        var application = new Application { ShutdownMode = ShutdownMode.OnExplicitShutdown };
        var exit = 0;
        application.Startup += async (_, _) =>
        {
            FrameworkElement? sprite = null; Window? window = null;
            try
            {
                Directory.CreateDirectory(output);
                sprite = (FrameworkElement)Activator.CreateInstance(type)!; sprite.Width = sprite.Height = 512;
                type.GetMethod("LoadManifest")!.Invoke(sprite, new object[] { manifest });
                if (type.GetProperty("AssetError")!.GetValue(sprite) is string loadError) throw new InvalidDataException(loadError);
                var motion = type.GetMethod("SetMotionMode")!; motion.Invoke(sprite, new[] { Enum.Parse(motion.GetParameters()[0].ParameterType, "Reduced") });
                var setFrame = type.GetMethod("SetFrameForTesting", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic)!;
                var diagnostics = (IEnumerable)type.GetMethod("GetFrameDiagnostics", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic)!.Invoke(sprite, new object[] { action })!;
                var count = diagnostics.Cast<object>().Count(); if (count == 0) throw new InvalidDataException("The requested action has no supplied frames.");
                var host = new Grid { Width = 512, Height = 512, Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(244, 246, 251)) }; host.Children.Add(sprite);
                window = new Window { Title = "Cat frame anatomy and accessory proof", Content = host, SizeToContent = SizeToContent.WidthAndHeight, ShowActivated = false, ShowInTaskbar = false };
                window.Show(); window.UpdateLayout(); await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.Render);
                if (type.GetProperty("AssetError")!.GetValue(sprite) is string error) throw new InvalidDataException(error);
                var baseFrames = new List<BitmapSource>(); var equippedFrames = new List<BitmapSource>(); var hashes = new List<object>();
                for (var index = 0; index < count; index++)
                {
                    type.GetMethod("SetAccessories")!.Invoke(sprite, new object[] { false, false });
                    setFrame.Invoke(sprite, new object[] { action, index }); await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.Render);
                    var plain = Capture(host); Save(plain, Path.Combine(output, $"base-{index:D2}.png")); baseFrames.Add(plain);
                    type.GetMethod("SetAccessories")!.Invoke(sprite, new object[] { true, true }); await Dispatcher.Yield(DispatcherPriority.Render);
                    var equipped = Capture(host); Save(equipped, Path.Combine(output, $"equipment-{index:D2}.png")); equippedFrames.Add(equipped);
                    if (HashPixels(plain) == HashPixels(equipped)) throw new InvalidDataException("The accessory proof did not render its actual equipment.");
                    hashes.Add(new { frame = index, basePixelSha256 = HashPixels(plain), equipmentPixelSha256 = HashPixels(equipped) });
                }
                Save(ContactSheet(baseFrames), Path.Combine(output, "base-contact-sheet.png"));
                Save(ContactSheet(equippedFrames), Path.Combine(output, "equipment-contact-sheet.png"));
                File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(output, "proof-report.json"), JsonSerializer.Serialize(new
                {
                    action, frames = count, actualWpfControl = true, opaqueBackground = true, frameSize = 512,
                    sourceManifest = manifest, manifestSha256 = HashFile(manifest), productAssembly = product, productSha256 = HashFile(product),
                    bodyImagesGeneratedByThisTool = false, anatomyApprovedByThisTool = false, accessoryPositionApprovedByThisTool = false,
                    capture = "Actual production PetSpriteView with SetFrameForTesting; its own decoded pages, placement and vector accessory rendering. No artwork reconstructed.",
                    clocksStopped = !(bool)type.GetProperty("HasActiveAnimation")!.GetValue(sprite)!, hashes
                }, new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented = true }));
                Console.WriteLine(JsonSerializer.Serialize(new { output, action, frames = count, actualWpfControl = true }));
            }
            catch (Exception error) { exit = 1; Console.Error.WriteLine(error); if (Directory.Exists(output)) File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(output, "proof-error.txt"), error.ToString()); }
            finally { window?.Close(); (sprite as IDisposable)?.Dispose(); application.Shutdown(exit); }
        };
        try { return application.Run(); }
        finally { AssemblyLoadContext.Default.Resolving -= Resolve; }
    }
    private static BitmapSource Capture(Visual visual) { var bitmap = new RenderTargetBitmap(512, 512, 96, 96, PixelFormats.Pbgra32); bitmap.Render(visual); bitmap.Freeze(); return bitmap; }
    private static BitmapSource ContactSheet(IReadOnlyList<BitmapSource> images)
    {
        const int columns = 4; var rows = (images.Count + columns - 1) / columns; var visual = new DrawingVisual();
        using (var drawing = visual.RenderOpen())
            for (var index = 0; index < images.Count; index++)
            {
                var x = index % columns * 512; var y = index / columns * 512;
                drawing.DrawImage(images[index], new Rect(x, y, 512, 512));
                drawing.DrawText(new FormattedText($"Frame {index + 1:D2}", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, FlowDirection.LeftToRight, new Typeface("Segoe UI"), 16, Brushes.DimGray, 1), new Point(x + 12, y + 10));
            }
        var bitmap = new RenderTargetBitmap(columns * 512, rows * 512, 96, 96, PixelFormats.Pbgra32); bitmap.Render(visual); bitmap.Freeze(); return bitmap;
    }
    private static void Save(BitmapSource bitmap, string path) { var encoder = new PngBitmapEncoder(); encoder.Frames.Add(BitmapFrame.Create(bitmap)); using var file = File.Create(path); encoder.Save(file); }
    private static string HashPixels(BitmapSource image) { var bytes = new byte[image.PixelWidth * image.PixelHeight * 4]; image.CopyPixels(bytes, image.PixelWidth * 4, 0); return Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(bytes)); }
    private static string HashFile(string path) => Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(File.ReadAllBytes(path)));
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/PetAtlasPackager/PetManifestComposer.cs ---
using System.IO;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.Json.Nodes;

namespace PetAtlasPackager;

/// <summary>Combines released legacy clips with explicitly reviewed whole-cat replacements.</summary>
internal static class PetManifestComposer
{
    private sealed record Replacement(string Action, string ManifestPath, JsonObject Manifest, string QaPath, JsonObject Qa, string QcPath, JsonObject Qc);
    private sealed record Copy(string Source, string Relative, string Sha256);

    internal static int Run(IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> options)
    {
        string Required(string key) => options.TryGetValue(key, out var value) ? value : throw new ArgumentException("Missing --" + key);
        var basePath = Path.GetFullPath(Required("merge-base"));
        var output = Path.GetFullPath(Required("output"));
        var baseManifest = JsonNode.Parse(File.ReadAllText(basePath))!.AsObject();
        var fragments = Required("fragments").Split(';', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries).Select(Path.GetFullPath).ToArray();
        var approvals = Required("approvals").Split(';', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries).Select(Path.GetFullPath).ToArray();
        if (fragments.Length == 0 || approvals.Length != fragments.Length) throw new InvalidDataException("Supply one reviewed QC file per explicit fragment.");
        var replacements = new List<Replacement>();
        for (var i = 0; i < fragments.Length; i++)
        {
            var fragment = JsonNode.Parse(File.ReadAllText(fragments[i]))!.AsObject();
            if (fragment["version"]?.GetValue<int>() != 3 || fragment["animations"]?.AsObject().Count != 1) throw new InvalidDataException("A replacement must be a v3 fragment for exactly one action.");
            var action = fragment["animations"]!.AsObject().Single().Key;
            if (action is "idle" or "blink" or "sit" or "sleep") throw new InvalidDataException("This integration preserves the released idle/blink/sit/sleep actions.");
            if (replacements.Any(x => x.Action == action)) throw new InvalidDataException("An action was supplied twice: " + action);
            var qaPath = Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(fragments[i])!, action + "-loop-qa.json");
            var qa = JsonNode.Parse(File.ReadAllText(qaPath))!.AsObject();
            var qc = JsonNode.Parse(File.ReadAllText(approvals[i]))!.AsObject();
            if (qc["action"]?.GetValue<string>() != action || qc["schema"]?.GetValue<int>() != 1) throw new InvalidDataException("QC action/schema does not match its fragment.");
            if (qc["artMethod"]?.GetValue<string>() != "imagegen-whole-cat") throw new InvalidDataException("Only reviewed whole-cat imagegen frames may replace legacy artwork.");
