            var sourceSheet = Path.GetFullPath(qa["sourceSheet"]!.GetValue<string>());
            if (sourceSheet.Split(new[] { Path.DirectorySeparatorChar, Path.AltDirectorySeparatorChar }).Any(part => part.Equals("PetAnimationBaker", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) || System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.IsMatch(part, @"(^|[-_])rig([-_.]|$)", System.Text.RegularExpressions.RegexOptions.IgnoreCase)))
                throw new InvalidDataException("Rejected rig artwork cannot enter the integration manifest.");
            var sourceHash = Hash(sourceSheet);
            if (!Equal(sourceHash, qa["sourceSha256"]?.GetValue<string>()) || !Equal(sourceHash, qc["sourceSha256"]?.GetValue<string>())) throw new InvalidDataException("QC is not bound to the current original sheet hash.");
            if (!Equal(Hash(qaPath), qc["qaSha256"]?.GetValue<string>())) throw new InvalidDataException("QC is not bound to the current packing/anchor annotations.");
            if (!Equal(Hash(fragments[i]), qa["manifestSha256"]?.GetValue<string>()) || !Equal(Hash(fragments[i]), qc["manifestSha256"]?.GetValue<string>())) throw new InvalidDataException("Frame metadata changed after its packing/review (including accessory occlusion). Repack and review the updated metadata.");
            if (qa["sourceFiles"] is JsonArray suppliedSources)
                foreach (var supplied in suppliedSources)
                    if (!Equal(Hash(Path.GetFullPath(supplied!["file"]!.GetValue<string>())), supplied["sha256"]?.GetValue<string>())) throw new InvalidDataException("A complete individually authored source changed after registration.");
            if (qa["manualAnnotationFile"]?.GetValue<string>() is { } manualAnnotation && !Equal(Hash(manualAnnotation), qa["manualAnnotationSha256"]?.GetValue<string>())) throw new InvalidDataException("A manual annotation source changed after packing.");
            var reviewedPages = qa["pages"]?.AsArray() ?? throw new InvalidDataException("Packing QA lacks encoded-page hashes; repack the approved source with the current tool.");
            foreach (var atlas in fragment["atlases"]!.AsArray())
            {
                var pagePath = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(fragments[i])!, atlas!["file"]!.GetValue<string>()));
                var pageRecord = reviewedPages.SingleOrDefault(page => page?["file"]?.GetValue<string>() == Path.GetFileName(pagePath));
                if (pageRecord is null || !Equal(Hash(pagePath), pageRecord["sha256"]?.GetValue<string>())) throw new InvalidDataException("An encoded sprite page changed after its packing QA.");
            }
            var reviewer = qc["reviewer"]?.GetValue<string>();
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(reviewer)) throw new InvalidDataException("QC must identify its visual reviewer.");
            var packedFrames = qa["frames"]!.AsArray(); var reviewedFrames = qc["frames"]!.AsArray();
            if (packedFrames.Count != reviewedFrames.Count) throw new InvalidDataException("Every supplied frame needs its own anatomy review.");
            var uniqueCells = new HashSet<int>();
            foreach (var packed in packedFrames)
            {
                var cell = packed!["Cell"]!.GetValue<int>();
                var matches = reviewedFrames.Where(frame => frame?["cell"]?.GetValue<int>() == cell).ToArray();
                if (!uniqueCells.Add(cell) || matches.Length != 1 || matches[0]?["anatomyApproved"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true)
                    throw new InvalidDataException($"Frame {action}/{cell} lacks an explicit positive anatomy review.");
                if (matches[0]?["hipConnectionApproved"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true || matches[0]?["plantedFootContinuityApproved"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true)
                    throw new InvalidDataException($"Frame {action}/{cell} lacks an explicit positive hip/foot-placement review.");
                if (!Equal(packed["PixelSha256"]!.GetValue<string>(), matches[0]?["pixelSha256"]?.GetValue<string>())) throw new InvalidDataException($"Frame {action}/{cell} changed after its visual review.");
                if (packed["OverrideSourceSha256"]?.GetValue<string>() is { } correctedHash)
                {
                    var correctedFile = Path.GetFullPath(packed["OverrideSourceFile"]!.GetValue<string>());
                    if (!Equal(Hash(correctedFile), correctedHash) || !Equal(correctedHash, matches[0]?["overrideSourceSha256"]?.GetValue<string>()) || matches[0]?["originalSourceRejected"]?.GetValue<bool>() != true)
                        throw new InvalidDataException($"Corrected frame {action}/{cell} lost its original-rejection or corrected-source provenance.");
                }
            }
            var frameDefinitions = SegmentFrames(fragment["animations"]![action]!["segments"]!).ToArray();
            var reviewedDefinitions = frameDefinitions.GroupBy(frame => frame["reviewCell"]?.GetValue<int>() is { } cell ? "review:" + cell : "atlas:" + frame["atlas"] + "/" + frame["cell"]).ToArray();
            foreach (var group in reviewedDefinitions)
            {
                var identities = group.Select(frame => { var copy = frame.DeepClone().AsObject(); copy.Remove("ms"); return copy.ToJsonString(); }).Distinct().Count();
                if (identities != 1) throw new InvalidDataException("A reused review cell refers to different pixels or attachment metadata.");
                if (group.First()["reviewCell"]?.GetValue<int>() is { } cell && !uniqueCells.Contains(cell)) throw new InvalidDataException("A segment refers to an unreviewed pose.");
            }
            if (reviewedDefinitions.Length != packedFrames.Count) throw new InvalidDataException("QA must cover every unique supplied pose in all segments. Reused exit poses are reviewed once and must keep identical pixels and anchors.");
            replacements.Add(new Replacement(action, fragments[i], fragment, qaPath, qa, approvals[i], qc));
        }
        // All approval checks above happen before any output is created.
        var composed = baseManifest.DeepClone().AsObject(); composed["version"] = 3;
        var atlases = composed["atlases"]!.AsArray(); var actions = composed["animations"]!.AsObject();
        foreach (var required in new[] { "idle", "blink", "sit", "sleep" })
            if (!actions.ContainsKey(required)) throw new InvalidDataException("The released base is missing preserved action: " + required);
        var copies = new List<Copy>(); var provenance = new JsonArray();
        for (var i = 0; i < atlases.Count; i++)
        {
            var atlas = atlases[i]!.AsObject(); var source = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(basePath)!, atlas["file"]!.GetValue<string>()));
            var relative = "legacy/" + i.ToString("D2") + "-" + Path.GetFileName(source);
            copies.Add(new Copy(source, relative, Hash(source))); atlas["file"] = relative;
        }
        foreach (var replacement in replacements)
        {
            var atlasStart = atlases.Count;
            foreach (var atlasNode in replacement.Manifest["atlases"]!.AsArray())
            {
                var atlas = atlasNode!.DeepClone().AsObject();
                var source = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(replacement.ManifestPath)!, atlas["file"]!.GetValue<string>()));
                var relative = "drawn/" + replacement.Action + "/" + Path.GetFileName(source);
                copies.Add(new Copy(source, relative, Hash(source))); atlas["file"] = relative; atlases.Add(atlas);
            }
            var sourceManifestRelative = "drawn/" + replacement.Action + "/source-fragment.json";
            copies.Add(new Copy(replacement.ManifestPath, sourceManifestRelative, Hash(replacement.ManifestPath)));
            var action = replacement.Manifest["animations"]![replacement.Action]!.DeepClone().AsObject();
            foreach (var frame in SegmentFrames(action["segments"]!)) frame["atlas"] = frame["atlas"]!.GetValue<int>() + atlasStart;
            actions[replacement.Action] = action;
            var provenanceRoot = "provenance/" + replacement.Action + "/";
            foreach (var path in new[] { replacement.QaPath, replacement.QcPath, replacement.Qa["sourceSheet"]!.GetValue<string>() })
                copies.Add(new Copy(Path.GetFullPath(path), provenanceRoot + Path.GetFileName(path), Hash(Path.GetFullPath(path))));
            if (replacement.Qa["manualAnnotationFile"]?.GetValue<string>() is { } annotationFile)
                copies.Add(new Copy(annotationFile, provenanceRoot + Path.GetFileName(annotationFile), Hash(annotationFile)));
            if (replacement.Qa["sourceFiles"] is JsonArray sourceFiles)
                for (var sourceIndex = 0; sourceIndex < sourceFiles.Count; sourceIndex++)
                {
                    var sourceFile = Path.GetFullPath(sourceFiles[sourceIndex]!["file"]!.GetValue<string>());
                    copies.Add(new Copy(sourceFile, provenanceRoot + "authored/" + sourceIndex.ToString("D3") + "-" + Path.GetFileName(sourceFile), Hash(sourceFile)));
                }
            var overrides = new JsonArray();
            foreach (var frame in replacement.Qa["frames"]!.AsArray())
                if (frame?["OverrideSourceFile"]?.GetValue<string>() is { } correctedFile)
                {
                    var cell = frame["Cell"]!.GetValue<int>(); var relative = provenanceRoot + "corrected/" + cell.ToString("D2") + "-" + Path.GetFileName(correctedFile);
                    copies.Add(new Copy(correctedFile, relative, Hash(correctedFile)));
                    overrides.Add(new JsonObject { ["cell"] = cell, ["sourceFile"] = relative, ["sourceSha256"] = frame["OverrideSourceSha256"]!.DeepClone(), ["originalSourceRejected"] = true });
                }
            provenance.Add(new JsonObject { ["action"] = replacement.Action, ["artMethod"] = "imagegen-whole-cat", ["sourceSha256"] = replacement.Qa["sourceSha256"]!.DeepClone(),
                ["reviewer"] = replacement.Qc["reviewer"]!.DeepClone(), ["reviewedFrames"] = replacement.Qc["frames"]!.DeepClone(),
                ["sourceFile"] = provenanceRoot + Path.GetFileName(replacement.Qa["sourceSheet"]!.GetValue<string>()),
                ["sourceManifestFile"] = sourceManifestRelative, ["sourceManifestSha256"] = Hash(replacement.ManifestPath),
                ["frameOverrides"] = overrides,
                ["qcFile"] = provenanceRoot + Path.GetFileName(replacement.QcPath), ["qaFile"] = provenanceRoot + Path.GetFileName(replacement.QaPath) });
        }
        composed["sourceProvenance"] = provenance;
        foreach (var item in copies)
        {
            var target = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(output, item.Relative));
            if (!target.StartsWith(output.TrimEnd(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar) + Path.DirectorySeparatorChar, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) throw new InvalidDataException("An output path escaped its integration directory.");
            if (File.Exists(target) && !Equal(Hash(target), item.Sha256)) throw new IOException("Use a fresh integration directory; an existing file differs: " + target);
        }
        Directory.CreateDirectory(output);
        foreach (var item in copies)
        {
            var target = Path.Combine(output, item.Relative); Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(target)!);
            if (!File.Exists(target)) File.Copy(item.Source, target);
        }
        var serializer = new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented = true };
        var manifestPath = Path.Combine(output, "animations.json"); File.WriteAllText(manifestPath + ".pending", composed.ToJsonString(serializer)); File.Move(manifestPath + ".pending", manifestPath, true);
        var audit = new { schema = 1, manifest = manifestPath, baseManifest = basePath, baseSha256 = Hash(basePath), replacedActions = replacements.Select(x => x.Action).ToArray(),
            preservedReleasedIdleSitSleepBlink = true, anatomyApprovalWasExplicit = true, automatedAnatomyJudgment = false, files = copies };
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(output, "integration-audit.json"), JsonSerializer.Serialize(audit, serializer));
        Console.WriteLine(JsonSerializer.Serialize(new { manifest = manifestPath, replacedActions = replacements.Select(x => x.Action), reviewedFrameCounts = replacements.Select(x => x.Qc["frames"]!.AsArray().Count) })); return 0;
    }
    internal static int WriteReviewTemplate(IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> options)
    {
        var qaPath = Path.GetFullPath(options["qc-template"]);
        var outputPath = Path.GetFullPath(options.TryGetValue("output", out var output) ? output : throw new ArgumentException("Missing --output QC.json"));
        if (File.Exists(outputPath)) throw new IOException("Do not overwrite an existing review: " + outputPath);
        var qa = JsonNode.Parse(File.ReadAllText(qaPath))!.AsObject();
        var template = new JsonObject { ["schema"] = 1, ["action"] = qa["action"]!.DeepClone(), ["artMethod"] = "imagegen-whole-cat", ["sourceSha256"] = qa["sourceSha256"]!.DeepClone(),
            ["qaSha256"] = Hash(qaPath), ["manifestSha256"] = qa["manifestSha256"]?.DeepClone(), ["reviewer"] = "", ["reviewNotes"] = "Inspect every frame at normal size and enlarged. Verify four limbs only, no extra paws, stable face/fur/scarf/props, complete tail, and accessories attached to fur. Leave rejected/unreviewed frames false.",
            ["frames"] = new JsonArray(qa["frames"]!.AsArray().Select(frame => (JsonNode)new JsonObject { ["cell"] = frame!["Cell"]!.DeepClone(), ["pixelSha256"] = frame["PixelSha256"]!.DeepClone(),
                ["overrideSourceSha256"] = frame["OverrideSourceSha256"]?.DeepClone(), ["originalSourceRejected"] = frame["OverrideSourceSha256"] is not null,
                ["anatomyApproved"] = false, ["hipConnectionApproved"] = false, ["plantedFootContinuityApproved"] = false, ["notes"] = "" }).ToArray()) };
        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(outputPath)!); File.WriteAllText(outputPath, template.ToJsonString(new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented = true }));
        Console.WriteLine(JsonSerializer.Serialize(new { qc = outputPath, anatomyApproved = false, requiresIndividualVisualReview = true })); return 0;
    }
    private static IEnumerable<JsonObject> SegmentFrames(JsonNode node)
    {
        if (node is not JsonObject value) yield break;
        if (value["frames"] is JsonArray frames) { foreach (var frame in frames) yield return frame!.AsObject(); yield break; }
        foreach (var child in value) if (child.Value is not null) foreach (var frame in SegmentFrames(child.Value)) yield return frame;
    }
    private static bool Equal(string? left, string? right) => left is not null && right is not null && string.Equals(left, right, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
    private static string Hash(string path) => Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(File.ReadAllBytes(path)));
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/PetAtlasPackager/PetPropOcclusion.cs ---
using System.IO;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;

namespace PetAtlasPackager;

/// <summary>Suggests a foreground folder mask from an actual opaque WPF proof; never edits artwork.</summary>
internal static class PetPropOcclusion
{
    internal static int Run(IReadOnlyDictionary<string,string> options)
    {
        var directory=Path.GetFullPath(options["blue-prop-proof"]);var output=Path.GetFullPath(options["output"]);
        if(File.Exists(output))throw new IOException("Use a fresh annotation suggestion file.");
        var result=new Dictionary<string,object>();
        for(var frame=2;frame<12;frame++)
        {
            var file=Path.Combine(directory,$"base-{frame:D2}.png");var image=new BitmapImage();image.BeginInit();image.CacheOption=BitmapCacheOption.OnLoad;image.UriSource=new Uri(file);image.EndInit();image.Freeze();
            var converted=new FormatConvertedBitmap(image,PixelFormats.Bgra32,null,0);var width=converted.PixelWidth;var height=converted.PixelHeight;var pixels=new byte[width*height*4];converted.CopyPixels(pixels,width*4,0);
            var selected=new bool[width*height];var seen=new bool[width*height];var largest=new List<int>();
            for(var y=(int)(height*.52);y<height;y++)for(var x=(int)(width*.43);x<width;x++)
            {
                var i=(y*width+x)*4;var b=pixels[i];var g=pixels[i+1];var r=pixels[i+2];
                selected[y*width+x]=r<150&&g>105&&b>180&&b>r+60&&b>g+40;
            }
            for(var i=0;i<selected.Length;i++)
            {
                if(!selected[i]||seen[i])continue;var component=new List<int>();var queue=new Queue<int>();queue.Enqueue(i);seen[i]=true;
                while(queue.Count>0){var p=queue.Dequeue();component.Add(p);var x=p%width;var y=p/width;foreach(var n in new[]{x>0?p-1:-1,x+1<width?p+1:-1,y>0?p-width:-1,y+1<height?p+width:-1})if(n>=0&&selected[n]&&!seen[n]){seen[n]=true;queue.Enqueue(n);}}
                if(component.Count>largest.Count)largest=component;
            }
            if(largest.Count<500)throw new InvalidDataException("No large isolated blue folder component in "+file);
            var hull=Hull(largest.Select(i=>new Point(i%width,i/width)).ToArray());
            var centre=new Point(hull.Average(p=>p.X),hull.Average(p=>p.Y));
            var polygon=hull.Select(p=>{var dx=p.X-centre.X;var dy=p.Y-centre.Y;var length=Math.Sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy);return new[]{(p.X+dx/length*1.5)/width,(p.Y+dy/length*1.5)/height};}).ToArray();
            result[frame.ToString()]=new{accessoryOcclusion=new[]{polygon},sourceProof=file,blueComponentPixels=largest.Count,manualReviewRequired=true};
        }
        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(output)!);File.WriteAllText(output,JsonSerializer.Serialize(result,new JsonSerializerOptions{WriteIndented=true}));Console.WriteLine(output);return 0;
    }
    private static Point[] Hull(Point[] points)
    {
        var sorted=points.Distinct().OrderBy(p=>p.X).ThenBy(p=>p.Y).ToArray();var lower=new List<Point>();var upper=new List<Point>();
        double Cross(Point a,Point b,Point c)=>(b.X-a.X)*(c.Y-a.Y)-(b.Y-a.Y)*(c.X-a.X);
        foreach(var p in sorted){while(lower.Count>=2&&Cross(lower[^2],lower[^1],p)<=0)lower.RemoveAt(lower.Count-1);lower.Add(p);}
        foreach(var p in sorted.Reverse()){while(upper.Count>=2&&Cross(upper[^2],upper[^1],p)<=0)upper.RemoveAt(upper.Count-1);upper.Add(p);}
        return lower.Take(lower.Count-1).Concat(upper.Take(upper.Count-1)).ToArray();
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/PetAtlasPackager/PetStandalonePackager.cs ---
using System.IO;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.Json.Nodes;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;

namespace PetAtlasPackager;

/// <summary>Registers complete individually authored drawings without changing their anatomy.</summary>
internal static class PetStandalonePackager
{
    internal static int Run(IReadOnlyDictionary<string,string> options)
    {
        var specPath = Path.GetFullPath(options["standalone-spec"]);
        var output = Path.GetFullPath(options.TryGetValue("output", out var target) ? target : throw new ArgumentException("Missing --output"));
        if (Directory.Exists(output) && Directory.EnumerateFileSystemEntries(output).Any()) throw new IOException("Use a fresh standalone output directory.");
        var spec = JsonNode.Parse(File.ReadAllText(specPath))!.AsObject();
        var action = spec["action"]!.GetValue<string>(); var segmentName = spec["segment"]?.GetValue<string>() ?? "enter";
        if (action.Any(c => !char.IsAsciiLetterOrDigit(c) && c is not '-' and not '_')) throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid action name.");
        var inputs = spec["frames"]!.AsArray(); if (inputs.Count is < 1 or > 128) throw new InvalidDataException("Supply 1–128 complete authored drawings.");
        var images = new List<BitmapSource>(); var frames = new JsonArray(); var qaFrames = new JsonArray(); var sources = new JsonArray();
        var sourceHashes = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase); var pixelHashes = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        foreach(var item in inputs)
        {
            var input = item!.AsObject(); var path = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(specPath)!, input["file"]!.GetValue<string>()));
            var hash = HashFile(path);
            var sourceIdentity = hash + "|" + (input["sourceRect"]?.ToJsonString() ?? "whole");
            if (!sourceHashes.Add(sourceIdentity)) throw new InvalidDataException("A supplied drawing or source cell is repeated. Use metadata references for deliberate source reuse.");
            BitmapSource image = Load(path);
            if (input["sourceRect"] is JsonArray crop)
            {
                var sourceRect = new Int32Rect(crop[0]!.GetValue<int>(),crop[1]!.GetValue<int>(),crop[2]!.GetValue<int>(),crop[3]!.GetValue<int>());
                image = new CroppedBitmap(image,sourceRect); image.Freeze();
            }
            var raw = Pixels(image); var sourceBounds = Bounds(raw, image.PixelWidth, image.PixelHeight);
            if (sourceBounds.IsEmpty) throw new InvalidDataException("An authored drawing is empty.");
            if (sourceBounds.X == 0 || sourceBounds.Y == 0 || sourceBounds.X+sourceBounds.Width >= image.PixelWidth || sourceBounds.Y+sourceBounds.Height >= image.PixelHeight)
                throw new InvalidDataException("An authored drawing touches its source boundary: "+path);
            var rect = input["draw"]!.AsArray(); var draw = new Rect(rect[0]!.GetValue<double>(),rect[1]!.GetValue<double>(),rect[2]!.GetValue<double>(),rect[3]!.GetValue<double>());
            if (draw.Width <= 0 || draw.Height <= 0 || Math.Abs(draw.Width/image.PixelWidth-draw.Height/image.PixelHeight)>1e-6) throw new InvalidDataException("Registration must use one uniform scale for the entire cat.");
            var visual = new DrawingVisual(); RenderOptions.SetBitmapScalingMode(visual, BitmapScalingMode.HighQuality);
            using(var context=visual.RenderOpen()) context.DrawImage(image,draw);
            var packed = new RenderTargetBitmap(256,256,96,96,PixelFormats.Pbgra32);packed.Render(visual);packed.Freeze();
            var packedPixels=Pixels(packed);var bounds=Bounds(packedPixels,256,256);
            if(bounds.X<2||bounds.Y<2||bounds.X+bounds.Width>254||bounds.Y+bounds.Height>254)throw new InvalidDataException("Registration clips the final canvas: "+path);
            var pixelHash=Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(packedPixels));if(!pixelHashes.Add(pixelHash))throw new InvalidDataException("Registration produced a duplicate pose.");
            var index=images.Count;images.Add(packed);
            var frame = new JsonObject { ["atlas"]=index/16,["cell"]=index%16,["reviewCell"]=index,["ms"]=input["ms"]?.DeepClone()??JsonValue.Create(90),
                ["head"]=input["head"]!.DeepClone(),["neck"]=input["neck"]!.DeepClone(),["feet"]=input["feet"]?.DeepClone()??new JsonArray(),
                ["headAngle"]=input["headAngle"]?.DeepClone()??JsonValue.Create(0),["neckAngle"]=input["neckAngle"]?.DeepClone()??JsonValue.Create(0),
                ["accessoryScale"]=input["accessoryScale"]?.DeepClone()??JsonValue.Create(1),["accessoryOcclusion"]=input["accessoryOcclusion"]?.DeepClone(),
                ["contact"]=input["contact"]?.DeepClone()??JsonValue.Create("none")};frames.Add(frame);
            qaFrames.Add(new JsonObject { ["Cell"]=index,["PixelSha256"]=pixelHash,["SourceFile"]=path,["SourceSha256"]=hash,
                ["SourceBounds"]=JsonSerializer.SerializeToNode(sourceBounds),["OutputBounds"]=JsonSerializer.SerializeToNode(bounds),["UniformScale"]=draw.Width/image.PixelWidth,
                ["Draw"]=input["draw"]!.DeepClone(),["SourceRect"]=input["sourceRect"]?.DeepClone(),["AuthoredPose"]=input["authoredPose"]?.DeepClone(),["ExactReuseOfApprovedPose"]=input["exactReuseOfApprovedPose"]?.DeepClone() });
            sources.Add(new JsonObject { ["file"]=path,["sha256"]=hash,["kind"]="complete-authored-drawing" });
        }
        var atlases = new JsonArray();var pageRecords=new JsonArray();var stem=action+"-"+segmentName.Replace('@','-');
        Directory.CreateDirectory(output);
        for(var page=0;page<(images.Count+15)/16;page++)
        {
            var name=stem+"-page-"+page.ToString("D2")+".png";var visual=new DrawingVisual();
            using(var context=visual.RenderOpen())for(var cell=0;cell<16&&page*16+cell<images.Count;cell++)context.DrawImage(images[page*16+cell],new Rect(cell%4*256,cell/4*256,256,256));
            var packed=new RenderTargetBitmap(1024,1024,96,96,PixelFormats.Pbgra32);packed.Render(visual);var encoder=new PngBitmapEncoder();encoder.Frames.Add(BitmapFrame.Create(packed));
            using(var file=File.Create(Path.Combine(output,name)))encoder.Save(file);
            atlases.Add(new JsonObject{["file"]=name,["columns"]=4,["rows"]=4,["sourceSize"]=new JsonArray(1024,1024)});
            pageRecords.Add(new JsonObject{["file"]=name,["sha256"]=HashFile(Path.Combine(output,name))});
        }
        var manifest=new JsonObject{["version"]=3,["atlases"]=atlases,["animations"]=new JsonObject{[action]=new JsonObject{["canvasSize"]=new JsonArray(256,256),["baseline"]=236d/256,["segments"]=new JsonObject{[segmentName]=new JsonObject{["frames"]=frames,["loop"]=spec["loop"]?.DeepClone()??JsonValue.Create(segmentName=="loop")}}}}};
        var jsonOptions=new JsonSerializerOptions{WriteIndented=true};var manifestPath=Path.Combine(output,"animations.json");File.WriteAllText(manifestPath,manifest.ToJsonString(jsonOptions));
        var qa=new JsonObject{["sourceSheet"]=specPath,["sourceSha256"]=HashFile(specPath),["sourceKind"]="individual-complete-drawing-registration-spec",["sourceFiles"]=sources,
            ["action"]=action,["segment"]=segmentName,["frameCount"]=images.Count,["frames"]=qaFrames,["pages"]=pageRecords,["manifestSha256"]=HashFile(manifestPath),
            ["noSynthesizedInbetweens"]=true,["noOpacityMixing"]=true,["noDuplicateFrames"]=true,["uniformWholeDrawingRegistration"]=true,
            ["decodedBytesPerPage"]=4194304,["hipConnectionApprovedByTool"]=false,["motionContinuityApprovedByTool"]=false};
        var qaPath=Path.Combine(output,stem+"-qa.json");File.WriteAllText(qaPath,qa.ToJsonString(jsonOptions));
        Console.WriteLine(JsonSerializer.Serialize(new{manifest=manifestPath,qa=qaPath,frames=images.Count}));return 0;
    }
    private static BitmapSource Load(string path){var image=new BitmapImage();image.BeginInit();image.CacheOption=BitmapCacheOption.OnLoad;image.CreateOptions=BitmapCreateOptions.IgnoreImageCache;image.UriSource=new Uri(path);image.EndInit();image.Freeze();var converted=new FormatConvertedBitmap(image,PixelFormats.Pbgra32,null,0);converted.Freeze();return converted;}
    private static byte[] Pixels(BitmapSource image){var bytes=new byte[image.PixelWidth*image.PixelHeight*4];image.CopyPixels(bytes,image.PixelWidth*4,0);return bytes;}
    private static Int32Rect Bounds(byte[] pixels,int width,int height){var l=width;var t=height;var r=-1;var b=-1;for(var y=0;y<height;y++)for(var x=0;x<width;x++)if(pixels[(y*width+x)*4+3]>16){l=Math.Min(l,x);t=Math.Min(t,y);r=Math.Max(r,x);b=Math.Max(b,y);}return r<l?Int32Rect.Empty:new Int32Rect(l,t,r-l+1,b-t+1);}
    private static string HashFile(string path)=>Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(File.ReadAllBytes(path)));
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/PetAtlasPackager/Program.cs ---
using System.Globalization;
using System.IO;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.Json.Nodes;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;

namespace PetAtlasPackager;

/// <summary>Production crop/registration/paging only. It never synthesizes or interpolates poses.</summary>
internal static class Program
{
    private sealed record Component(int Pixels, Int32Rect Bounds);
    private sealed record SourceFrame(int Cell, Int32Rect CellRect, Int32Rect OriginalBounds, Int32Rect Bounds, Component[] Components, string Sha256, BitmapSource Image, byte[] EffectivePixels, string EffectiveSha256, string? OverrideFile, string? OverrideSha256, Int32Rect? OverrideBounds, double? OverrideScale);
    private sealed record PackedFrame(int Cell, Int32Rect SourceBounds, Int32Rect OutputBounds, string PixelSha256, Point Head, Point Neck, Point[] Feet, double HeadAngle, double NeckAngle, double AccessoryScale, double GroundDelta, string? OverrideSourceFile, string? OverrideSourceSha256, double GroundSnapPixels);
    private static readonly CultureInfo invariant = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;

    [STAThread]
    private static int Main(string[] args)
    {
        try { return Run(Parse(args)); }
        catch (Exception error) { Console.Error.WriteLine(error.Message); return 1; }
    }

    private static int Run(Dictionary<string, string> options)
    {
        if (options.ContainsKey("merge-base")) return PetManifestComposer.Run(options);
        if (options.ContainsKey("standalone-spec")) return PetStandalonePackager.Run(options);
        if (options.ContainsKey("blue-prop-proof")) return PetPropOcclusion.Run(options);
        if (options.ContainsKey("wpf-proof-product")) return PetFrameProof.Run(options);
        if (options.ContainsKey("qc-template")) return PetManifestComposer.WriteReviewTemplate(options);
        if (options.TryGetValue("inspect", out var inspectPath))
        {
            var image = Load(Full(inspectPath));
            var components = Components(Pixels(image), image.PixelWidth, image.PixelHeight, Integer(options, "alpha", 16, 1, 254)).Where(x => x.Pixels >= Integer(options, "min-component", 8, 1, int.MaxValue)).OrderBy(x => x.Bounds.Y).ThenBy(x => x.Bounds.X).ToArray();
            Console.WriteLine(JsonSerializer.Serialize(new { width = image.PixelWidth, height = image.PixelHeight, components }, new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented = true })); return 0;
        }
        if (options.TryGetValue("self-test", out var selfTestDirectory)) return SelfTest(Full(selfTestDirectory));
        if (options.ContainsKey("help"))
        {
            Console.WriteLine("--sheet PNG --columns N --rows N --frames 12|24 --action NAME --output DIR --head x,y --neck x,y [--feet x,y;x,y] [--cells 0,1,...] [--segment loop|enter|exit|exit@MARKER] [--duration-ms 2000] [--loop true] [--manifest FILE] [--annotations FILE] [--padding 16] [--ground 236] [--anchor-space silhouette|cell|canvas] [--validate-only true]");
            return 0;
        }
        var sheet = Full(Required(options, "sheet"));
        var output = Full(Required(options, "output"));
        var action = Required(options, "action");
        var segmentName = options.GetValueOrDefault("segment", "loop");
        if (action.Any(c => !char.IsAsciiLetterOrDigit(c) && c is not '-' and not '_')) throw new ArgumentException("Action names may contain ASCII letters, digits, '-' and '_'.");
        if (segmentName is not ("loop" or "enter" or "exit") && !(segmentName.StartsWith("exit@", StringComparison.Ordinal) && int.TryParse(segmentName[5..], out var marker) && marker >= 0)) throw new ArgumentException("Invalid segment; use loop, enter, exit, or exit@MARKER.");
        var columns = Integer(options, "columns", 0, 1, 64); var rows = Integer(options, "rows", 0, 1, 64);
        var frameCount = Integer(options, "frames", 0, 1, columns * rows);
        var cells = options.TryGetValue("cells", out var chosen) ? chosen.Split(',').Select(x => int.Parse(x, invariant)).ToArray() : Enumerable.Range(0, frameCount).ToArray();
        if (cells.Length != frameCount || cells.Distinct().Count() != cells.Length || cells.Any(x => x < 0 || x >= columns * rows)) throw new ArgumentException("Cells must be distinct valid indices, exactly matching --frames.");
        var head = PointValue(Required(options, "head")); var neck = PointValue(Required(options, "neck"));
        var feet = options.TryGetValue("feet", out var footText) ? footText.Split(';').Select(PointValue).ToArray() : Array.Empty<Point>();
        var anchorSpace = options.GetValueOrDefault("anchor-space", "silhouette");
        var detectLandmarks = Boolean(options, "detect-landmarks", false);
        if (anchorSpace is not ("silhouette" or "cell" or "canvas")) throw new ArgumentException("Invalid anchor space.");
        var headAngle = Number(options, "head-angle", 0, -360, 360); var neckAngle = Number(options, "neck-angle", 0, -360, 360);
        var accessoryScale = Number(options, "accessory-scale", 1, .25, 3);
        var duration = Number(options, "duration-ms", 2000, 1, 120000);
        var padding = Number(options, "padding", 16, 2, 80); var ground = Number(options, "ground", 236, 100, 254);
        var boundary = Integer(options, "boundary-margin", 1, 0, 16); var alpha = Integer(options, "alpha", 16, 1, 254);
        var minimumComponent = Integer(options, "min-component", 8, 1, 1024);
        var loop = Boolean(options, "loop", segmentName == "loop" && action is "idle" or "sit" or "sleep" or "work" or "walk" or "play" or "play-wand" or "drag" or "held");
        var annotations = options.TryGetValue("annotations", out var annotationPath) ? JsonNode.Parse(File.ReadAllText(Full(annotationPath)))?.AsObject() ?? throw new InvalidDataException("Empty annotation document.") : new JsonObject();
        var overrideMap = options.TryGetValue("frame-overrides", out var overrideMapPath) ? JsonNode.Parse(File.ReadAllText(Full(overrideMapPath)))?.AsObject() ?? throw new InvalidDataException("Empty frame-overrides mapping.") : new JsonObject();
        foreach (var pair in overrideMap) if (!int.TryParse(pair.Key, out var overrideCell) || !cells.Contains(overrideCell)) throw new InvalidDataException("An override refers to an unselected cell.");
        var source = Load(sheet);
        var columnCuts = Cuts(options, "column-cuts", columns, source.PixelWidth);
        var rowCuts = Cuts(options, "row-cuts", rows, source.PixelHeight);
        var sourceRects = options.TryGetValue("rects", out var rectPath) ? JsonNode.Parse(File.ReadAllText(Full(rectPath)))?.AsArray() ?? throw new InvalidDataException("Empty rectangle array.") : null;
        var allPixels = Pixels(source); var frames = new List<SourceFrame>();
        foreach (var cell in cells)
        {
            var column = cell % columns; var row = cell / columns;
            var rect = sourceRects is not null ? NodeRect(sourceRects[cell]!.AsArray()) : new Int32Rect(columnCuts[column], rowCuts[row], columnCuts[column + 1] - columnCuts[column], rowCuts[row + 1] - rowCuts[row]);
            if (rect.X < 0 || rect.Y < 0 || rect.X + rect.Width > source.PixelWidth || rect.Y + rect.Height > source.PixelHeight) throw new InvalidDataException("A supplied frame rectangle lies outside the source sheet.");
            var cellWidth = rect.Width; var cellHeight = rect.Height;
            var pixels = CellPixels(allPixels, source.PixelWidth, rect);
            var components = Components(pixels, cellWidth, cellHeight, alpha).Where(x => x.Pixels >= minimumComponent).ToArray();
            if (components.Length == 0) throw new InvalidDataException($"Cell {cell} has no visible silhouette.");
            foreach (var component in components)
                if (component.Bounds.X <= boundary || component.Bounds.Y <= boundary || component.Bounds.X + component.Bounds.Width >= cellWidth - boundary || component.Bounds.Y + component.Bounds.Height >= cellHeight - boundary)
                    throw new InvalidDataException($"Cell {cell} touches its cell boundary; a cat, tail or prop may be clipped. Bounds={component.Bounds}. No output was written.");
            var bounds = Union(components.Select(x => x.Bounds));
            var originalBounds = bounds; var sourceHash = Hash(pixels);
            BitmapSource cellImage = new CroppedBitmap(source, rect); cellImage.Freeze();
            string? overrideFile = null, overrideHash = null; Int32Rect? overrideBounds = null; double? overrideScale = null;
            if (overrideMap[cell.ToString(invariant)] is { } overrideValue)
            {
                overrideFile = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(Full(overrideMapPath!))!, overrideValue.GetValue<string>()));
                var corrected = Load(overrideFile); overrideHash = Hash(File.ReadAllBytes(overrideFile));
                var correctedComponents = Components(Pixels(corrected), corrected.PixelWidth, corrected.PixelHeight, alpha).Where(x => x.Pixels >= minimumComponent).ToArray();
                if (correctedComponents.Length == 0 || correctedComponents.Any(x => x.Bounds.X <= boundary || x.Bounds.Y <= boundary || x.Bounds.X + x.Bounds.Width >= corrected.PixelWidth - boundary || x.Bounds.Y + x.Bounds.Height >= corrected.PixelHeight - boundary))
                    throw new InvalidDataException($"Override for cell {cell} has an empty or clipped connected silhouette.");
                overrideBounds = Union(correctedComponents.Select(x => x.Bounds));
                // Fit the complete corrected drawing to its original cell's body height, limiting
                // width if necessary. One uniform scale keeps anatomy and proportions intact.
                overrideScale = Math.Min(originalBounds.Height / (double)overrideBounds.Value.Height, originalBounds.Width / (double)overrideBounds.Value.Width);
                var x = originalBounds.X + originalBounds.Width / 2d - (overrideBounds.Value.X + overrideBounds.Value.Width / 2d) * overrideScale.Value;
                var y = originalBounds.Y + originalBounds.Height - (overrideBounds.Value.Y + overrideBounds.Value.Height) * overrideScale.Value;
                var registered = new DrawingVisual(); RenderOptions.SetBitmapScalingMode(registered, BitmapScalingMode.HighQuality);
                using (var drawing = registered.RenderOpen()) drawing.DrawImage(corrected, new Rect(x, y, corrected.PixelWidth * overrideScale.Value, corrected.PixelHeight * overrideScale.Value));
                var effective = new RenderTargetBitmap(cellWidth, cellHeight, 96, 96, PixelFormats.Pbgra32); effective.Render(registered); effective.Freeze(); cellImage = effective;
                pixels = Pixels(effective); components = Components(pixels, cellWidth, cellHeight, alpha).Where(component => component.Pixels >= minimumComponent).ToArray();
                bounds = Union(components.Select(component => component.Bounds));
                if (components.Any(component => component.Bounds.X <= boundary || component.Bounds.Y <= boundary || component.Bounds.X + component.Bounds.Width >= cellWidth - boundary || component.Bounds.Y + component.Bounds.Height >= cellHeight - boundary)) throw new InvalidDataException($"Registered override {cell} clips its original cell.");
            }
            frames.Add(new SourceFrame(cell, rect, originalBounds, bounds, components, sourceHash, cellImage, pixels, Hash(pixels), overrideFile, overrideHash, overrideBounds, overrideScale));
        }
        if (frames.Select(x => x.EffectiveSha256).Distinct().Count() != frames.Count) throw new InvalidDataException("The selected sequence contains exact repeated source cells. No duplicated frames are accepted.");
        var overrideHashes = frames.Where(x => x.OverrideSha256 is not null).Select(x => x.OverrideSha256).ToArray();
        if (overrideHashes.Distinct().Count() != overrideHashes.Length) throw new InvalidDataException("One corrected drawing cannot be reused as multiple genuine frames.");

        // One transform for the complete action preserves relative head/body size, ground and motion.
        // Per-frame crops are never independently resized to fill the canvas.
        var union = Union(frames.Select(x => x.Bounds));
        var scale = Math.Min((256 - 2 * padding) / union.Width, (ground - padding) / union.Height);
        var offsetX = (256 - union.Width * scale) / 2 - union.X * scale;
        var offsetY = ground - (union.Y + union.Height) * scale;
        var images = new List<BitmapSource>(); var packed = new List<PackedFrame>();
        foreach (var item in frames)
        {
            var annotation = annotations[item.Cell.ToString(invariant)]?.AsObject();
            var correction = annotation?["offset"] is JsonArray move ? NodePoint(move) : new Point();
            var visual = new DrawingVisual(); RenderOptions.SetBitmapScalingMode(visual, BitmapScalingMode.HighQuality);
            using (var drawing = visual.RenderOpen()) drawing.DrawImage(item.Image, new Rect(offsetX + correction.X, offsetY + correction.Y, item.CellRect.Width * scale, item.CellRect.Height * scale));
            var image = new RenderTargetBitmap(256, 256, 96, 96, PixelFormats.Pbgra32); image.Render(visual); image.Freeze();
            var pixels = Pixels(image); var outputComponents = Components(pixels, 256, 256, alpha).Where(x => x.Pixels >= minimumComponent).ToArray();
            var bounds = Union(outputComponents.Select(x => x.Bounds));
            var groundSnap = 0d;
            if (item.OverrideFile is not null)
            {
                // A second resampling can grow a soft alpha edge by a pixel or two.
                // Translate the entire corrected drawing to the original final foot line;
                // this is registration only and applies to its landmarks as well.
                var originalFoot = (int)Math.Round((item.OriginalBounds.Y + item.OriginalBounds.Height) * scale + offsetY + correction.Y);
                groundSnap = originalFoot - (bounds.Y + bounds.Height);
                if (groundSnap != 0)
                {
                    correction = new Point(correction.X, correction.Y + groundSnap);
                    var snapped = new DrawingVisual(); RenderOptions.SetBitmapScalingMode(snapped, BitmapScalingMode.HighQuality);
                    using (var drawing = snapped.RenderOpen()) drawing.DrawImage(item.Image, new Rect(offsetX + correction.X, offsetY + correction.Y, item.CellRect.Width * scale, item.CellRect.Height * scale));
                    image = new RenderTargetBitmap(256, 256, 96, 96, PixelFormats.Pbgra32); image.Render(snapped); image.Freeze();
                    pixels = Pixels(image); outputComponents = Components(pixels, 256, 256, alpha).Where(x => x.Pixels >= minimumComponent).ToArray(); bounds = Union(outputComponents.Select(x => x.Bounds));
                }
            }
            if (bounds.X < 2 || bounds.Y < 2 || bounds.X + bounds.Width > 254 || bounds.Y + bounds.Height > 254) throw new InvalidDataException($"Registered cell {item.Cell} clips its 256px output canvas. No output was written.");
            Point Map(Point value)
            {
                Point mapped = anchorSpace switch
                {
                    "canvas" => new Point(value.X * 256, value.Y * 256),
                    "cell" => new Point(value.X * item.CellRect.Width * scale + offsetX + correction.X, value.Y * item.CellRect.Height * scale + offsetY + correction.Y),
                    _ => new Point((item.Bounds.X + value.X * item.Bounds.Width) * scale + offsetX + correction.X, (item.Bounds.Y + value.Y * item.Bounds.Height) * scale + offsetY + correction.Y)
                };
                return new Point(mapped.X / 256, mapped.Y / 256);
            }
            var landmarks = detectLandmarks ? DetectLandmarks(item.EffectivePixels, item.CellRect.Width, item.CellRect.Height, item.Bounds, head, neck) : (Head: (Point?)null, Neck: (Point?)null, NeckAngle: (double?)null);
            Point MapCellPoint(Point point) => new((point.X * scale + offsetX + correction.X) / 256, (point.Y * scale + offsetY + correction.Y) / 256);
            Point ExplicitAnchor(string name, Point fallback, Point? detected) => annotation?[name] is JsonArray value ? NodePoint(value) : detected is { } actual ? MapCellPoint(actual) : Map(fallback);
            var mappedHead = ExplicitAnchor("head", head, landmarks.Head); var mappedNeck = ExplicitAnchor("neck", neck, landmarks.Neck);
            var mappedFeet = annotation?["feet"] is JsonArray feetArray ? feetArray.Select(x => NodePoint(x!.AsArray())).ToArray() : feet.Select(Map).ToArray();
            if (!Inside(mappedHead) || !Inside(mappedNeck) || mappedFeet.Any(x => !Inside(x))) throw new InvalidDataException($"Cell {item.Cell} has an attachment anchor outside its canvas.");
            images.Add(image); packed.Add(new PackedFrame(item.Cell, item.Bounds, bounds, Hash(pixels), mappedHead, mappedNeck, mappedFeet,
                annotation?["headAngle"]?.GetValue<double>() ?? headAngle, annotation?["neckAngle"]?.GetValue<double>() ?? (options.ContainsKey("neck-angle") ? neckAngle : landmarks.NeckAngle ?? neckAngle),
                annotation?["accessoryScale"]?.GetValue<double>() ?? accessoryScale, ground - (bounds.Y + bounds.Height), item.OverrideFile, item.OverrideSha256, groundSnap));
        }
        if (packed.Select(x => x.PixelSha256).Distinct().Count() != packed.Count) throw new InvalidDataException("Registration produced exact repeated frames. No duplicated frames are accepted.");
        var stem = action + "-" + segmentName.Replace('@', '-');
        var manifestPath = options.TryGetValue("manifest", out var requestedManifest) ? Full(requestedManifest) : Path.Combine(output, "animations.json");
        var manifest = File.Exists(manifestPath) ? JsonNode.Parse(File.ReadAllText(manifestPath))!.AsObject() : new JsonObject { ["version"] = 3, ["atlases"] = new JsonArray(), ["animations"] = new JsonObject() };
        if (manifest["version"]?.GetValue<int>() != 3) throw new InvalidDataException("Append target must be a v3 manifest. Build new assets beside V1.0 assets.");
        var atlases = manifest["atlases"]!.AsArray(); var animations = manifest["animations"]!.AsObject();
        var atlasStart = atlases.Count; var pageNames = new List<string>();
        for (var page = 0; page < (images.Count + 15) / 16; page++)
        {
            var name = stem + "-page-" + page.ToString("D2", invariant) + ".png"; pageNames.Add(name);
            atlases.Add(new JsonObject { ["file"] = Path.GetRelativePath(Path.GetDirectoryName(manifestPath)!, Path.Combine(output, name)).Replace('\\', '/'), ["columns"] = 4, ["rows"] = 4, ["sourceSize"] = new JsonArray(1024, 1024) });
        }
        if (animations[action] is not JsonObject actionNode) { actionNode = new JsonObject { ["canvasSize"] = new JsonArray(256, 256), ["baseline"] = ground / 256, ["segments"] = new JsonObject() }; animations[action] = actionNode; }
        var segments = actionNode["segments"]!.AsObject(); var frameNodes = new JsonArray();
        for (var i = 0; i < packed.Count; i++)
        {
            var pose = packed[i]; var annotation = annotations[pose.Cell.ToString(invariant)]?.AsObject();
            frameNodes.Add(new JsonObject { ["atlas"] = atlasStart + i / 16, ["cell"] = i % 16, ["ms"] = duration / packed.Count,
                ["head"] = Pair(pose.Head), ["neck"] = Pair(pose.Neck), ["feet"] = new JsonArray(pose.Feet.Select(p => (JsonNode)Pair(p)).ToArray()),
                ["headAngle"] = pose.HeadAngle, ["neckAngle"] = pose.NeckAngle, ["accessoryScale"] = pose.AccessoryScale,
                ["contact"] = annotation?["contact"]?.GetValue<string>() ?? "none", ["accessoryOcclusion"] = annotation?["accessoryOcclusion"]?.DeepClone() });
        }
        var segment = new JsonObject { ["frames"] = frameNodes, ["loop"] = loop };
        if (segmentName.StartsWith("exit@", StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            if (segments["exitByMarker"] is not JsonObject variants) { variants = new JsonObject(); segments["exitByMarker"] = variants; }
            variants[segmentName[5..]] = segment;
        }
        else segments[segmentName] = segment;
        if (options.TryGetValue("exit-at", out var exitText)) actionNode["exitAt"] = new JsonArray(exitText.Split(',').Select(x => (JsonNode)JsonValue.Create(int.Parse(x, invariant))!).ToArray());
        if (action == "walk")
        {
            actionNode["strideLength"] = Number(options, "stride", 48, 1, 1024);
            if (options.TryGetValue("contact-phases", out var contactText)) actionNode["contactPhases"] = new JsonArray(contactText.Split(',').Select(x => (JsonNode)JsonValue.Create(double.Parse(x, invariant))!).ToArray());
        }
        var qa = new
        {
            sourceSheet = sheet, sourceSha256 = Hash(File.ReadAllBytes(sheet)), sourceWidth = source.PixelWidth, sourceHeight = source.PixelHeight,
            columns, rows, columnCuts, rowCuts, action, segment = segmentName, sourceCells = cells, frameCount = packed.Count, durationMilliseconds = duration,
            logicalCanvas = new[] { 256, 256 }, pageGrid = new[] { 4, 4 }, decodedPageBytes = 1024 * 1024 * 4,
            commonSourceBounds = union, uniformScale = scale, offsetX, offsetY, ground, padding, anchorSpace, detectLandmarks,
            anchorAnnotation = "Optional pink-ear base and upper blue-scarf color landmarks, falling back to explicit action-relative points. Per-cell final-canvas annotations override these. Visual review required; no skeleton fitting.",
            manualAnnotationFile = options.TryGetValue("annotations", out var annotationFile) ? Full(annotationFile) : null,
            manualAnnotationSha256 = options.TryGetValue("annotations", out annotationFile) ? Hash(File.ReadAllBytes(Full(annotationFile))) : null,
            noSynthesizedInbetweens = true, noOpacityMixing = true, noDuplicateFrames = true, allCellBoundariesClear = true,
            maximumGroundDeltaPixels = packed.Max(x => x.GroundDelta), frames = packed,
            sourceComponents = frames.Select(x => new { x.Cell, x.CellRect, x.OriginalBounds, x.Bounds, x.Components, sourcePixelSha256 = x.Sha256, effectivePixelSha256 = x.EffectiveSha256, x.OverrideFile, x.OverrideSha256, x.OverrideBounds, x.OverrideScale, originalCellSuperseded = x.OverrideFile is not null })
        };
        var jsonOptions = new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented = true };
        if (Boolean(options, "validate-only", false)) { Console.WriteLine(JsonSerializer.Serialize(qa, jsonOptions)); return 0; }
        Directory.CreateDirectory(output); Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(manifestPath)!);
        for (var page = 0; page < pageNames.Count; page++)
        {
            var visual = new DrawingVisual(); using (var drawing = visual.RenderOpen())
                for (var cell = 0; cell < 16 && page * 16 + cell < images.Count; cell++) drawing.DrawImage(images[page * 16 + cell], new Rect(cell % 4 * 256, cell / 4 * 256, 256, 256));
            var image = new RenderTargetBitmap(1024, 1024, 96, 96, PixelFormats.Pbgra32); image.Render(visual); image.Freeze(); Save(image, Path.Combine(output, pageNames[page]));
        }
        var tempManifest = manifestPath + ".pending"; File.WriteAllText(tempManifest, manifest.ToJsonString(jsonOptions)); File.Move(tempManifest, manifestPath, true);
        var qaPath = Path.Combine(output, stem + "-qa.json");
        var qaDocument = JsonSerializer.SerializeToNode(qa, jsonOptions)!.AsObject();
        qaDocument["pages"] = new JsonArray(pageNames.Select(name => (JsonNode)new JsonObject { ["file"] = name, ["sha256"] = Hash(File.ReadAllBytes(Path.Combine(output, name))) }).ToArray());
        qaDocument["manifestSha256"] = Hash(File.ReadAllBytes(manifestPath));
        File.WriteAllText(qaPath, qaDocument.ToJsonString(jsonOptions));
        Console.WriteLine(JsonSerializer.Serialize(new { manifest = manifestPath, qa = qaPath, pages = pageNames, frames = images.Count, decodedBytesPerPage = 4194304 })); return 0;
    }

    private static Dictionary<string, string> Parse(string[] args)
    {
        var result = new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        for (var i = 0; i < args.Length; i++)
        {
            if (!args[i].StartsWith("--", StringComparison.Ordinal)) throw new ArgumentException("Expected --option value.");
            var key = args[i][2..]; var value = i + 1 < args.Length && !args[i + 1].StartsWith("--", StringComparison.Ordinal) ? args[++i] : "true";
            if (!result.TryAdd(key, value)) throw new ArgumentException("Repeated option: " + key);
        }
        return result;
    }
    private static string Required(Dictionary<string, string> options, string key) => options.TryGetValue(key, out var value) ? value : throw new ArgumentException("Missing --" + key);
    private static string Full(string value) => Path.GetFullPath(value);
    private static int Integer(Dictionary<string, string> options, string key, int fallback, int min, int max)
    {
        var value = options.TryGetValue(key, out var text) ? int.Parse(text, invariant) : fallback;
        return value >= min && value <= max ? value : throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(key);
    }
    private static double Number(Dictionary<string, string> options, string key, double fallback, double min, double max)
    {
        var value = options.TryGetValue(key, out var text) ? double.Parse(text, invariant) : fallback;
        return double.IsFinite(value) && value >= min && value <= max ? value : throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(key);
    }
    private static bool Boolean(Dictionary<string, string> options, string key, bool fallback) => options.TryGetValue(key, out var text) ? bool.Parse(text) : fallback;
    private static Point PointValue(string text)
    {
        var values = text.Split(','); if (values.Length != 2) throw new ArgumentException("A point needs x,y.");
        var point = new Point(double.Parse(values[0], invariant), double.Parse(values[1], invariant));
        return Inside(point) ? point : throw new ArgumentException("Relative anchors must fit [0,1].");
    }
    private static Point NodePoint(JsonArray value)
    {
        if (value.Count != 2) throw new InvalidDataException("An annotation point needs x,y.");
        var result = new Point(value[0]!.GetValue<double>(), value[1]!.GetValue<double>());
        if (!double.IsFinite(result.X) || !double.IsFinite(result.Y)) throw new InvalidDataException("Non-finite annotation point."); return result;
    }
    private static Int32Rect NodeRect(JsonArray value)
    {
        if (value.Count != 4) throw new InvalidDataException("A frame rectangle needs x,y,width,height.");
        var rect = new Int32Rect(value[0]!.GetValue<int>(), value[1]!.GetValue<int>(), value[2]!.GetValue<int>(), value[3]!.GetValue<int>());
        if (rect.Width <= 0 || rect.Height <= 0) throw new InvalidDataException("Empty source rectangle."); return rect;
    }
    private static int[] Cuts(Dictionary<string, string> options, string name, int count, int extent)
    {
        var values = options.TryGetValue(name, out var text) ? text.Split(',').Select(x => int.Parse(x, invariant)).ToArray() : Enumerable.Range(0, count + 1).Select(x => (int)Math.Round(x * extent / (double)count)).ToArray();
        if (values.Length != count + 1 || values[0] != 0 || values[^1] != extent || values.Zip(values.Skip(1)).Any(x => x.First >= x.Second)) throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid explicit grid boundary list: " + name);
        return values;
    }
    private static bool Inside(Point point) => double.IsFinite(point.X) && double.IsFinite(point.Y) && point.X >= 0 && point.X <= 1 && point.Y >= 0 && point.Y <= 1;
    private static JsonArray Pair(Point point) => new(point.X, point.Y);
    private static BitmapSource Load(string path)
    {
        var image = new BitmapImage(); image.BeginInit(); image.CacheOption = BitmapCacheOption.OnLoad; image.CreateOptions = BitmapCreateOptions.IgnoreImageCache;
        image.UriSource = new Uri(path, UriKind.Absolute); image.EndInit(); image.Freeze();
        if (image.Format == PixelFormats.Bgra32 || image.Format == PixelFormats.Pbgra32) return image;
        var converted = new FormatConvertedBitmap(image, PixelFormats.Bgra32, null, 0); converted.Freeze(); return converted;
    }
    private static byte[] Pixels(BitmapSource image) { var result = new byte[image.PixelWidth * image.PixelHeight * 4]; image.CopyPixels(result, image.PixelWidth * 4, 0); return result; }
    private static byte[] CellPixels(byte[] sheet, int width, Int32Rect rect)
    {
        var result = new byte[rect.Width * rect.Height * 4];
        for (var row = 0; row < rect.Height; row++) Buffer.BlockCopy(sheet, ((rect.Y + row) * width + rect.X) * 4, result, row * rect.Width * 4, rect.Width * 4);
        return result;
    }
    private static Component[] Components(byte[] pixels, int width, int height, int alpha)
    {
        var visited = new bool[width * height]; var queue = new int[width * height]; var result = new List<Component>();
        for (var initial = 0; initial < visited.Length; initial++)
        {
            if (visited[initial] || pixels[initial * 4 + 3] <= alpha) continue;
            var count = 0; var read = 0; var write = 1; queue[0] = initial; visited[initial] = true;
            var minX = width; var minY = height; var maxX = 0; var maxY = 0;
            while (read < write)
            {
                var index = queue[read++]; var x = index % width; var y = index / width; count++;
                minX = Math.Min(minX, x); minY = Math.Min(minY, y); maxX = Math.Max(maxX, x); maxY = Math.Max(maxY, y);
                for (var dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) for (var dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++)
                {
                    var nx = x + dx; var ny = y + dy; if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= width || ny >= height) continue;
                    var next = ny * width + nx; if (visited[next] || pixels[next * 4 + 3] <= alpha) continue;
                    visited[next] = true; queue[write++] = next;
                }
            }
            result.Add(new Component(count, new Int32Rect(minX, minY, maxX - minX + 1, maxY - minY + 1)));
        }
        return result.ToArray();
    }
    private static Int32Rect Union(IEnumerable<Int32Rect> values)
    {
        var array = values.ToArray(); if (array.Length == 0) throw new InvalidDataException("No visible pixels.");
        var left = array.Min(x => x.X); var top = array.Min(x => x.Y);
        return new Int32Rect(left, top, array.Max(x => x.X + x.Width) - left, array.Max(x => x.Y + x.Height) - top);
    }
    private static (Point? Head, Point? Neck, double? NeckAngle) DetectLandmarks(byte[] pixels, int width, int height, Int32Rect bounds, Point headFallback, Point neckFallback)
    {
        var pink = new byte[pixels.Length];
        for (var y = bounds.Y; y < Math.Min(height, bounds.Y + bounds.Height * .48); y++)
            for (var x = bounds.X; x < bounds.X + bounds.Width; x++)
            {
                var offset = (y * width + x) * 4; var b = pixels[offset]; var g = pixels[offset + 1]; var r = pixels[offset + 2];
                if (pixels[offset + 3] > 160 && r > 175 && g > 65 && g < 220 && b > 65 && b < 220 && r > g * 1.12 && r > b * 1.10) pink[offset + 3] = 255;
            }
        var ears = Components(pink, width, height, 16).Where(x => x.Pixels >= 20 && x.Bounds.Height >= 6 && x.Bounds.Width >= 4).ToArray();
        var target = new Point(bounds.X + headFallback.X * bounds.Width, bounds.Y + headFallback.Y * bounds.Height);
        var ear = ears.OrderBy(x => Math.Pow(x.Bounds.X + x.Bounds.Width * .5 - target.X, 2) + Math.Pow(x.Bounds.Y + x.Bounds.Height - target.Y, 2)).FirstOrDefault();
        Point? head = ear is null ? null : new Point(ear.Bounds.X + ear.Bounds.Width * .5, ear.Bounds.Y + ear.Bounds.Height + 2);
        var neckX = bounds.X + neckFallback.X * bounds.Width;
        var blue = new byte[pixels.Length];
        for (var y = Math.Max(bounds.Y, (int)(bounds.Y + bounds.Height * .40)); y < Math.Min(height, bounds.Y + bounds.Height * .90); y++)
            for (var x = bounds.X; x < Math.Min(width, bounds.X + bounds.Width); x++)
            {
                var offset = (y * width + x) * 4; var b = pixels[offset]; var g = pixels[offset + 1]; var r = pixels[offset + 2];
                if (pixels[offset + 3] > 160 && r > 30 && g > 30 && b > 145 && b > r * 1.45 && b > g * 1.12) blue[offset + 3] = 255;
            }
        // The scarf forms a broad connected band. Blue eye highlights are small
        // disconnected islands and must never become a collar attachment point.
        var neckY = bounds.Y + neckFallback.Y * bounds.Height;
        var band = Components(blue, width, height, 16).Where(x => x.Pixels >= 30 && x.Bounds.Width >= bounds.Width * .14 &&
                x.Bounds.X <= neckX + bounds.Width * .12 && x.Bounds.X + x.Bounds.Width >= neckX - bounds.Width * .12)
            .OrderBy(x => Math.Abs(x.Bounds.Y - neckY) + Math.Max(0, x.Bounds.X - neckX) + Math.Max(0, neckX - x.Bounds.X - x.Bounds.Width)).FirstOrDefault();
        var firstBlue = new List<Point>();
        if (band is not null)
            for (var x = Math.Max(band.Bounds.X, (int)(neckX - bounds.Width * .12)); x <= Math.Min(band.Bounds.X + band.Bounds.Width - 1, (int)(neckX + bounds.Width * .12)); x++)
                for (var y = band.Bounds.Y; y < band.Bounds.Y + band.Bounds.Height; y++)
                    if (blue[(y * width + x) * 4 + 3] > 16) { firstBlue.Add(new Point(x, y)); break; }
        if (firstBlue.Count < 5) return (head, null, null);
        var orderedY = firstBlue.Select(point => point.Y).OrderBy(y => y).ToArray();
        var averageX = firstBlue.Average(point => point.X); var averageY = firstBlue.Average(point => point.Y);
        var denominator = firstBlue.Sum(point => Math.Pow(point.X - averageX, 2));
        var slope = denominator < .001 ? 0 : firstBlue.Sum(point => (point.X - averageX) * (point.Y - averageY)) / denominator;
        var angle = Math.Clamp(Math.Atan(slope) * 180 / Math.PI, -30, 30);
        return (head, new Point(neckX, orderedY[orderedY.Length / 2]), angle);
    }
    private static string Hash(byte[] bytes) => Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(bytes));
    private static void Save(BitmapSource source, string path) { var encoder = new PngBitmapEncoder(); encoder.Frames.Add(BitmapFrame.Create(source)); using var stream = File.Create(path); encoder.Save(stream); }

    private static int SelfTest(string directory)
    {
        Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        string Fixture(string name, bool duplicate, bool clipped)
        {
            var visual = new DrawingVisual();
            using (var drawing = visual.RenderOpen())
                for (var cell = 0; cell < 12; cell++)
                {
                    drawing.PushTransform(new TranslateTransform(cell % 4 * 64, cell / 4 * 64));
                    drawing.DrawEllipse(Brushes.Ivory, new Pen(Brushes.SlateBlue, 1), new Point(31, 31), 15, 17);
                    drawing.DrawEllipse(Brushes.Ivory, new Pen(Brushes.SlateBlue, 1), new Point(44, 40), 9, 5);
                    drawing.DrawEllipse(Brushes.SlateBlue, null, new Point(24 + (duplicate ? 0 : cell), 50), 3, 3);
                    if (clipped && cell == 0) drawing.DrawRectangle(Brushes.Ivory, null, new Rect(0, 0, 8, 8));
                    drawing.Pop();
                }
            var image = new RenderTargetBitmap(256, 192, 96, 96, PixelFormats.Pbgra32); image.Render(visual); image.Freeze();
            var path = Path.Combine(directory, name + ".png"); Save(image, path); return path;
        }
        Dictionary<string, string> Arguments(string path, string suffix) => new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
        {
            ["sheet"] = path, ["output"] = Path.Combine(directory, suffix), ["columns"] = "4", ["rows"] = "3", ["frames"] = "12",
            ["action"] = "idle", ["head"] = ".6,.2", ["neck"] = ".5,.55", ["feet"] = ".3,1;.6,1", ["loop"] = "true"
        };
        Run(Arguments(Fixture("valid", false, false), "valid-output"));
        using var report = JsonDocument.Parse(File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(directory, "valid-output", "idle-loop-qa.json")));
        var root = report.RootElement;
        if (root.GetProperty("frameCount").GetInt32() != 12 || !root.GetProperty("noDuplicateFrames").GetBoolean()) throw new InvalidDataException("A valid input lost frames or uniqueness.");
        var outputFrames = root.GetProperty("frames").EnumerateArray().ToArray();
        if (outputFrames.Select(x => x.GetProperty("PixelSha256").GetString()).Distinct().Count() != 12) throw new InvalidDataException("Packing synthesized duplicate frames.");
        var page = Load(Path.Combine(directory, "valid-output", "idle-loop-page-00.png"));
        if (page.PixelWidth != 1024 || page.PixelHeight != 1024) throw new InvalidDataException("A page exceeded its fixed 4 MiB decoded budget.");
        var rejected = new List<string>();
        foreach (var mode in new[] { "duplicate", "clipped" })
        {
            var input = Fixture(mode, mode == "duplicate", mode == "clipped");
            try { Run(Arguments(input, mode + "-output")); }
            catch (InvalidDataException error) when (error.Message.Contains(mode == "duplicate" ? "repeated" : "boundary", StringComparison.Ordinal))
            {
                if (Directory.Exists(Path.Combine(directory, mode + "-output"))) throw new InvalidDataException("A rejected input wrote partial output.");
                rejected.Add(mode); continue;
            }
            throw new InvalidDataException("An invalid input was accepted: " + mode);
        }
        var overrideVisual = new DrawingVisual();
        using (var drawing = overrideVisual.RenderOpen())
        {
            drawing.DrawEllipse(Brushes.Ivory, new Pen(Brushes.SlateBlue, 4), new Point(512, 480), 210, 270);
            drawing.DrawEllipse(Brushes.SlateBlue, null, new Point(490, 790), 45, 35);
        }
        var overrideImage = new RenderTargetBitmap(1024, 1024, 96, 96, PixelFormats.Pbgra32); overrideImage.Render(overrideVisual); overrideImage.Freeze();
        var correctedPath = Path.Combine(directory, "corrected-diagnostic.png"); Save(overrideImage, correctedPath);
        var overrideMap = Path.Combine(directory, "overrides.json"); File.WriteAllText(overrideMap, new JsonObject { ["0"] = correctedPath }.ToJsonString());
        var overrideArguments = Arguments(Path.Combine(directory, "valid.png"), "override-output"); overrideArguments["frame-overrides"] = overrideMap;
        var originalHash = Hash(File.ReadAllBytes(Path.Combine(directory, "valid.png"))); Run(overrideArguments);
        using var overrideReport = JsonDocument.Parse(File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(directory, "override-output", "idle-loop-qa.json")));
        var overridden = overrideReport.RootElement.GetProperty("frames")[0];
        if (overridden.GetProperty("OverrideSourceSha256").GetString() != Hash(File.ReadAllBytes(correctedPath)) || overridden.GetProperty("GroundDelta").GetDouble() != 0 ||
            overrideReport.RootElement.GetProperty("frames")[1].GetProperty("OverrideSourceSha256").ValueKind != JsonValueKind.Null || originalHash != Hash(File.ReadAllBytes(Path.Combine(directory, "valid.png"))))
            throw new InvalidDataException("An override lost source provenance, moved its foot line, or changed an untouched source cell.");
        var landmarkFixture = new byte[100 * 100 * 4];
        void BlueRect(int left, int top, int right, int bottom)
        {
            for (var y = top; y < bottom; y++) for (var x = left; x < right; x++)
            { var index = (y * 100 + x) * 4; landmarkFixture[index] = 230; landmarkFixture[index + 1] = 70; landmarkFixture[index + 2] = 60; landmarkFixture[index + 3] = 255; }
        }
        BlueRect(30, 42, 38, 50); BlueRect(60, 42, 68, 50); BlueRect(25, 65, 76, 71);
        var detected = DetectLandmarks(landmarkFixture, 100, 100, new Int32Rect(10, 10, 80, 80), new Point(.72, .28), new Point(.54, .68));
        if (detected.Neck?.Y != 65) throw new InvalidDataException("Blue eye highlights were mistaken for the broad scarf band.");
        var summary = new { validFrames = 12, decodedPageBytes = 4194304, uniformScale = root.GetProperty("uniformScale").GetDouble(), rejected,
            overrideRawSourceBound = true, originalSheetUnchanged = true, overrideGroundDelta = 0, blueEyeHighlightsExcludedFromNeck = true, diagnosticShapesOnly = true };
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(directory, "self-test.json"), JsonSerializer.Serialize(summary, new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented = true }));
        Console.WriteLine(JsonSerializer.Serialize(summary)); return 0;
    }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/PetAtlasPackager/README.md ---
# Whole-cat atlas packager

This WPF tool only crops, registers and pages supplied artwork. It never creates
inbetweens, reverses/repeats cells, blends images or fits a skeleton.

Build with the bundled .NET SDK, then invoke `PetAtlasPackager.dll` through that
SDK's `dotnet.exe`:

```powershell
dotnet PetAtlasPackager.dll --sheet work.png --columns 4 --rows 3 --frames 12 `
  --action work --output Assets/Pet/v3 --head .70,.20 --neck .52,.62 `
  --feet '.35,1;.65,1' --duration-ms 2000 --loop true
```

The example anchors must be reviewed against the actual drawing. By default they
are relative to each cell's visible silhouette. `--anchor-space cell` instead
uses the original cell, and `--anchor-space canvas` uses the final 256px canvas.
An optional JSON annotation file, passed with `--annotations`, keys entries by
original cell index. It can specify normalized final-canvas `head`, `neck`,
`feet`, clockwise degree `headAngle`/`neckAngle`, `accessoryScale`, `contact`, and
an explicit registration `offset` in final-canvas pixels. Annotation points are
manual metadata, not automatic estimates of anatomical landmarks.

The transform is shared by every selected cell. It fits the union of all source
silhouettes, preserving relative size and motion, and maps the common lower
bound to `--ground` (default 236px). It does not resize each pose separately or
flatten intended vertical movement. The QA JSON reports each pose's ground
delta and attachment anchors for frame-by-frame review.

`--cells 0,1,...` selects distinct source cells. `--segment enter`, `exit`, or
`exit@15` adds a real supplied segment to the v3 manifest; `loop` is the default.
`--exit-at 0,6`, `--stride 48`, and `--contact-phases 0,.5` provide walk metadata.
An existing v3 `--manifest` can be appended to; V1.0 manifests are rejected.
Source and rendered exact duplicate cells are rejected. Significant alpha
components touching a source cell edge or output canvas edge are rejected before
any output is written. Each output page contains at most 16 256px cells and
decodes to 4 MiB.

For a sheet whose actual gutters are uneven, `--column-cuts 0,384,768,1152,1536`
and `--row-cuts 0,372,695,1024` provide explicit grid boundaries. `--rects file.json`
instead accepts an array of source `[x,y,width,height]` rectangles indexed by cell.
This lets a reviewer crop complete connected cats without forcing an artificial
equal grid through a paw. `--inspect sheet.png --min-component 1000` reports the
actual connected-alpha body bounds for planning those rectangles; it writes no
artwork. A frame rectangle must still pass the normal clear-edge checks.

`--detect-landmarks true` optionally locates the pink ear nearest the supplied
head point and the upper blue scarf edge near the supplied neck point. Only a broad
connected scarf band qualifies; small blue eye islands are excluded. The scarf's
upper-edge slope supplies the collar angle, unless an explicit angle overrides it. The color
landmarks follow whole drawn frames; no skeleton is created. Missing landmarks
fall back to the explicit relative points. Manual per-frame annotations always
win. All inferred points still require visual review with the actual accessories.

`--frame-overrides overrides.json` accepts a map such as
`{"0":"repairs/f01.png","5":"repairs/f06.png"}`. Each complete corrected drawing
is uniformly fitted to its original pose's body height, limiting width when
necessary, then centered and aligned to the original ground. It never stretches
the X/Y axes separately. Color landmarks run on the corrected frame afterwards.
The original sheet is untouched. QA records the original cell, corrected source
file/SHA-256, uniform registration scale, final pixels and the fact that the old
cell was superseded. QC templates mark those original cells rejected and bind the
replacement source hash; the integration gate verifies and copies each corrected
source. An identical corrected source cannot stand in for multiple genuine frames.

`--validate-only true` performs all analysis without writing output. Run
`--self-test <artifact-directory>` to exercise unique-frame packing, page sizing,
duplicate rejection and boundary rejection using clearly synthetic QA shapes.
Packing validation does not replace a review of motion, character identity,
prop stability or the manually annotated attachment positions.

## Integration gate

Create a review template with `--qc-template <action-loop-qa.json> --output QC.json`.
Every frame starts with `anatomyApproved: false`; the tool never approves anatomy.
A reviewer must inspect each original pose, reject extra limbs/paws and other
defects, fill their identity and approve only the individual acceptable frames.
The review is bound to the original sheet, exact packed pixels, anchor annotation
QA and encoded page hashes. Repacking or changing annotations requires review again.

`--merge-base <released-animations.json> --fragments 'work/animations.json;walk/animations.json'`
`--approvals 'work-qc.json;walk-qc.json' --output <fresh-integration-directory>`
preserves the released base actions, including idle/blink/sit/sleep, and replaces
only explicitly supplied approved actions. It copies legacy and whole-cat pages
into separate relative directories and stores reviewed provenance beside the new
manifest. It rejects missing/unapproved anatomy records, changed pages/annotations,
and rejected rig sources before creating output. A fresh directory prevents
overwriting an earlier reviewed integration snapshot. This is a technical gate
for recorded review decisions; it cannot judge anatomy automatically.

`--wpf-proof-product <YourCatFileAssistant.dll> --manifest <sample-animations.json>`
`--action success --output <new-proof-directory>` launches the real product's
`PetSpriteView` and captures every supplied pose at 512px on an opaque background,
both with and without its actual vector accessories. Its contact sheets and report
include the product/manifest fingerprints. This is a static rendering proof, not
a motion-continuity approval; it never reconstructs a cat from manifest data.

param([switch]$SkipTests, [switch]$NoArchive, [switch]$SkipInstaller, [string]$Version)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$projectDirectory = Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent
. (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'release-version.ps1')
$releaseInfo = Get-CatReleaseInfo -ProjectDirectory $projectDirectory -Version $Version
& (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'audit-licenses.ps1') -PackagePath $projectDirectory -SourceBundlePath (Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\ffmpeg\source')
if (-not $NoArchive) { & (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'pack-sources.ps1') -Version $releaseInfo.Version }
& (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'build.ps1') -RunTests:(-not $SkipTests)
$dotnetExecutable = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\dotnet\sdk\dotnet.exe'
$publishDirectory = Join-Path $projectDirectory ('release\' + $releaseInfo.PackageName)
$packageStagingDirectory = Join-Path $projectDirectory ('artifacts\publish\' + [Guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
$stagingDirectory = Join-Path $packageStagingDirectory 'app'
Push-Location $projectDirectory
try {
  & $dotnetExecutable publish src\CatFileAssistant.Desktop -c Release -m:1 -r win-x64 --self-contained true -p:PublishSingleFile=false -p:PublishTrimmed=false -o $stagingDirectory
  if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Publishing failed.' }
  $workerPublishDirectory = Join-Path $stagingDirectory 'worker'
  & $dotnetExecutable publish src\CatFileAssistant.ArchiveWorker -c Release -m:1 -r win-x64 --self-contained true -p:PublishSingleFile=false -p:PublishTrimmed=false -o $workerPublishDirectory
  if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Archive worker publication failed.' }
  $archiveLicenseDirectory = Join-Path $stagingDirectory 'licenses\7zip'
  $workerSourceDirectory = Join-Path $stagingDirectory 'licenses\archive-worker\source'
  New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $archiveLicenseDirectory,$workerSourceDirectory | Out-Null
  Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\7zip\licenses\License.txt'),(Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\7zip\licenses\copying.txt'),(Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\7zip\source\7z2604-src.tar.xz'),(Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\7zip\provenance.json') -Destination $archiveLicenseDirectory -Force
  Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\7zip\bin\7z.exe') -Destination (Join-Path $stagingDirectory 'tools\7zip\bin\7z.exe') -Force
  Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath (Join-Path $projectDirectory 'src\CatFileAssistant.ArchiveWorker') -File | Where-Object { $_.Extension -in @('.cs','.csproj') } | Copy-Item -Destination $workerSourceDirectory -Force
  Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\7zip\licenses\copying.txt') -Destination (Join-Path $stagingDirectory 'licenses\archive-worker\LICENSE.txt') -Force
  Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $projectDirectory 'docs\archive-worker-source.md') -Destination (Join-Path $stagingDirectory 'licenses\archive-worker\README.md') -Force
  $workspacePrefix = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($projectDirectory).TrimEnd('\') + '\'
  foreach ($movePath in @($stagingDirectory, $publishDirectory)) {
    if (-not [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($movePath).StartsWith($workspacePrefix, [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { throw 'Publish path escaped the workspace.' }
  }
  $appHost = Join-Path $stagingDirectory 'YourCatFileAssistant.exe'
  Remove-Item -LiteralPath $appHost -Force
  Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $projectDirectory 'README.md') -Destination $stagingDirectory -Force
  Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $projectDirectory 'docs') -Destination $stagingDirectory -Recurse -Force
  $gilbertLicenseDirectory = Join-Path $stagingDirectory 'licenses\gilbert'
  New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $gilbertLicenseDirectory | Out-Null
  Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $projectDirectory 'src\CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure\ImageObfuscationSources\LICENSE'),(Join-Path $projectDirectory 'src\CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure\ImageObfuscationSources\gilbert2d.py'),(Join-Path $projectDirectory 'src\CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure\ImageObfuscationSources\provenance.json') -Destination $gilbertLicenseDirectory -Force
  foreach ($ffmpegMaterial in @('licenses','provenance.json','component-manifest.json','source-manifest.json')) {
    $ffmpegMaterialPath = Join-Path $projectDirectory ('tools\ffmpeg\' + $ffmpegMaterial)
    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ffmpegMaterialPath) { Copy-Item -LiteralPath $ffmpegMaterialPath -Destination (Join-Path $stagingDirectory 'tools\ffmpeg') -Recurse -Force }
  }
  $ffmpegPackageSource = Join-Path $stagingDirectory 'tools\ffmpeg\source'
  New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $ffmpegPackageSource | Out-Null
  Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath (Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\ffmpeg\source') -File | Copy-Item -Destination $ffmpegPackageSource -Force
  & (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'export-design-assets.ps1') -DestinationDirectory (Join-Path $stagingDirectory 'visual-assets')
  $licenseDirectory = Join-Path $stagingDirectory 'licenses\dotnet'
