  foreach ($licenseName in @('LICENSE.txt','ThirdPartyNotices.txt')) {
    $licensePath = Join-Path (Split-Path $dotnetExecutable -Parent) $licenseName
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $licensePath)) { throw "Runtime license missing: $licenseName" }
    Copy-Item -LiteralPath $licensePath -Destination $licenseDirectory -Force
  }

  $desktopRuntimePackage = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\dotnet\packages\microsoft.windowsdesktop.app.runtime.win-x64\10.0.12'
  Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $coreRuntimePackage 'LICENSE.TXT') -Destination (Join-Path $licenseDirectory 'Core-Runtime-LICENSE.txt') -Force
  Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $coreRuntimePackage 'THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.TXT') -Destination (Join-Path $licenseDirectory 'Core-Runtime-THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.txt') -Force
  Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $desktopRuntimePackage 'LICENSE') -Destination (Join-Path $licenseDirectory 'Windows-Desktop-Runtime-LICENSE.txt') -Force

    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath (Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\dotnet\licenses') -File | Copy-Item -Destination $licenseDirectory -Force
  }
  $appHostTemplate = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\dotnet\sdk\packs\Microsoft.NETCore.App.Host.win-x64\10.0.12\runtimes\win-x64\native\apphost.exe'
  $launcher = Join-Path $packageStagingDirectory '你的猫咪文件助手.exe'

  if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Clean-root apphost generation failed.' }
  Copy-Item -LiteralPath $launcher -Destination (Join-Path $packageStagingDirectory '卸载程序.exe')
  $installedFiles = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $packageStagingDirectory -Recurse -File | ForEach-Object {
    [ordered]@{ path=$_.FullName.Substring($packageStagingDirectory.Length + 1).Replace('\','/'); sha256=(Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant() }
  })
  [ordered]@{ version=$releaseInfo.Version; files=$installedFiles } | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 5 | Set-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $stagingDirectory 'installation-files.json') -Encoding utf8
  if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $publishDirectory) {
    $archiveDirectory = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'release\archive'
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $archiveDirectory | Out-Null
    $previousDirectory = Join-Path $archiveDirectory ($releaseInfo.Label + '-' + (Get-Date -Format 'yyyyMMdd-HHmmss') + '-' + [Guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N').Substring(0,6))
    if (-not [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($previousDirectory).StartsWith($workspacePrefix, [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { throw 'Archive path escaped the workspace.' }
    Move-Item -LiteralPath $publishDirectory -Destination $previousDirectory
  }
  Move-Item -LiteralPath $packageStagingDirectory -Destination $publishDirectory
  if (-not $NoArchive) {
    $archivePath = Join-Path $projectDirectory ('release\' + $releaseInfo.PackageName + '.zip')
    Compress-Archive -LiteralPath $publishDirectory -DestinationPath $archivePath -CompressionLevel Optimal -Force
    $archiveChecksum = (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $archivePath -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant() + '  ' + [IO.Path]::GetFileName($archivePath) + [Environment]::NewLine
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText($archivePath + '.sha256', $archiveChecksum, [Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))
    Get-Item -LiteralPath $archivePath | Select-Object FullName,Length
  }
  if (-not $SkipInstaller) { & (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'build-installer.ps1') -PackageDirectory $publishDirectory -Version $releaseInfo.Version }
} finally { Pop-Location }


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/release-version.ps1 ---
function Get-CatReleaseInfo {
  param([Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$ProjectDirectory, [string]$Version)
  $projectPath = Join-Path $ProjectDirectory 'src\CatFileAssistant.Desktop\CatFileAssistant.Desktop.csproj'
  [xml]$project = Get-Content -LiteralPath $projectPath -Raw
  $sourceVersion = [string]($project.Project.PropertyGroup | Where-Object { $_.Version } | Select-Object -First 1).Version
  if ($sourceVersion -notmatch '^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$') { throw "Invalid application version in project: $sourceVersion" }
  if (-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Version) -and $Version -ne $sourceVersion) { throw "Requested version $Version does not match project version $sourceVersion." }
  $label = 'V' + ($sourceVersion -replace '\.0$', '')
  [pscustomobject]@{ Version=$sourceVersion; Label=$label; PackageName=('你的猫咪文件助手-' + $label + '-Windows-x64') }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/restore-dependencies.ps1 ---
param([string]$SourceCompanion,[string]$FfmpegArchive,[string]$SevenZipArchive,[string]$ExtractorPath,[switch]$IncludeCorrespondingSources,[switch]$SkipSdk)
$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
$root=Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent
if (-not $SkipSdk) {
  $localDotnet=Join-Path $root 'tools\dotnet\sdk\dotnet.exe'
  $systemDotnet=Get-Command dotnet -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
  if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $localDotnet) -and -not $systemDotnet) { & (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'bootstrap-sdk.ps1'); if($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0){throw 'SDK restore failed'} }
}
$python=Get-Command python -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
if(-not $python){throw 'Python 3.11+ is required for pinned native dependency restoration.'}
$argsList=@('-B',(Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'restore-native-dependencies.py'))
if($SourceCompanion){$argsList+=@('--source-companion',$SourceCompanion)}
if($FfmpegArchive){$argsList+=@('--ffmpeg-archive',$FfmpegArchive)}
if($SevenZipArchive){$argsList+=@('--sevenzip-archive',$SevenZipArchive)}
if($ExtractorPath){$argsList+=@('--extractor-path',$ExtractorPath)}
if($IncludeCorrespondingSources){$argsList+='--include-corresponding-sources'}
& $python.Source @argsList
if($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0){throw 'Native dependency restoration failed. See the error above and README.'}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/restore-native-dependencies.py ---
"""Restore exact native dependencies; never executes downloaded installers."""
import argparse,hashlib,json,pathlib,shutil,subprocess,urllib.request,zipfile,os
ROOT=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent

def read(path):return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
def sha(path):
 with path.open('rb') as f:return hashlib.file_digest(f,'sha256').hexdigest()
def target(relative):
 p=(ROOT/relative).resolve()
 if not p.is_relative_to(ROOT):raise ValueError('Dependency destination escapes project')
 return p
def save_checked(stream,relative,expected):
 p=target(relative);p.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);tmp=p.with_name(p.name+'.partial');h=hashlib.sha256()
 try:
  with tmp.open('wb') as f:
   while block:=stream.read(1024*1024):h.update(block);f.write(block)
  if h.hexdigest().lower()!=expected.lower():raise ValueError('SHA-256 mismatch: '+relative)
  os.replace(tmp,p)
 finally:
  if tmp.exists():tmp.unlink()
def obtain(local,url,expected,relative):
 if local:
  p=pathlib.Path(local).resolve()
  if sha(p)!=expected.lower():raise ValueError('Archive SHA-256 mismatch')
  return p
 p=target(relative)
 if p.exists() and sha(p)==expected.lower():return p
 try:
  with urllib.request.urlopen(urllib.request.Request(url,headers={'User-Agent':'YourCatFileAssistant-dependency-restorer'}),timeout=90) as response:save_checked(response,relative,expected)
 except Exception as ex:raise RuntimeError('Pinned dependency download failed; provide its exact local archive or --source-companion. No latest substitution: '+str(ex)) from ex
 return p

def main():
 ap=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
 ap.add_argument('--source-companion');ap.add_argument('--ffmpeg-archive');ap.add_argument('--sevenzip-archive');ap.add_argument('--extractor-path');ap.add_argument('--include-corresponding-sources',action='store_true');ap.add_argument('--verify-only',action='store_true');a=ap.parse_args()
 ff=read(ROOT/'tools/ffmpeg/provenance.json');sz=read(ROOT/'tools/7zip/provenance.json')
 expected={'tools/ffmpeg/'+x['path']:x['sha256'] for x in ff['binaryFiles'] if x['distributed']}
 expected['tools/7zip/bin/7z.dll']=sz['dllSha256'];expected['tools/7zip/bin/7z.exe']=read(ROOT/'tools/7zip/cli-sha256.json')['7z.exe']
 expected['tools/7zip/source/7z2604-src.tar.xz']=sz['sourceSha256']
 if a.include_corresponding_sources:
  if not a.source_companion:ap.error('--include-corresponding-sources requires --source-companion')
  expected.update({'tools/ffmpeg/source/'+x['path']:x['sha256'] for x in read(ROOT/'tools/ffmpeg/source/distribution-manifest.json')['requiredFiles']})
  for group in ['licenseFiles','noticeFiles','sourceFiles']:
   expected.update({'tools/ffmpeg/'+x['path']:x['sha256'] for x in ff[group]})
 missing={rel:h for rel,h in expected.items() if not target(rel).is_file() or sha(target(rel))!=h.lower()}
 if a.verify_only:
  if missing:raise RuntimeError('Native dependencies missing/mismatched; run restore-dependencies.ps1: '+', '.join(missing))
 elif a.source_companion:
  with zipfile.ZipFile(pathlib.Path(a.source_companion).resolve()) as z:
   for rel,h in missing.items():
    matches=[e for e in z.infolist() if not e.is_dir() and (e.filename.replace('\\','/')==rel or e.filename.replace('\\','/').endswith('/'+rel))]
    if len(matches)!=1:raise ValueError('Expected exactly one matching file in companion: '+rel)
    with z.open(matches[0]) as stream:save_checked(stream,rel,h)
 else:
  ff_missing={p:h for p,h in missing.items() if p.startswith('tools/ffmpeg/bin/')}
  if ff_missing:
   archive=obtain(a.ffmpeg_archive,ff['binaryArchiveUrl'],ff['binaryArchiveSha256'],'tools/ffmpeg/downloads/pinned-ffmpeg.zip')
   with zipfile.ZipFile(archive) as z:
    for rel,h in ff_missing.items():
     suffix='/'+rel.removeprefix('tools/ffmpeg/')
     matches=[e for e in z.infolist() if not e.is_dir() and ('/'+e.filename.replace('\\','/')).endswith(suffix)]
     if len(matches)!=1:raise ValueError('Missing/duplicate FFmpeg member: '+rel)
     with z.open(matches[0]) as stream:save_checked(stream,rel,h)
  if any(p.startswith('tools/7zip/bin/') for p in missing):
   installer=obtain(a.sevenzip_archive,sz['binaryUrl'],sz['binarySha256'],'tools/7zip/downloads/7z2604-x64.exe')
   extractor=a.extractor_path or shutil.which('7z')
   if not extractor:raise RuntimeError('Supply --extractor-path to an existing 7z.exe, or restore from a companion ZIP')
   payload=target('tools/7zip/downloads/payload.7z');payload.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
   with installer.open('rb') as src:
    src.seek(45568)
    if src.read(6)!=bytes.fromhex('377abcaf271c'):raise ValueError('Unexpected pinned installer payload marker')
    src.seek(45568)
    with payload.open('wb') as dst:shutil.copyfileobj(src,dst)
   target('tools/7zip/bin').mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
   subprocess.run([extractor,'x',str(payload),'-o'+str(target('tools/7zip/bin')),'7z.dll','7z.exe','-y','-bso0'],check=True)
  if 'tools/7zip/source/7z2604-src.tar.xz' in missing:
   obtain(None,sz['sourceUrl'],sz['sourceSha256'],'tools/7zip/source/7z2604-src.tar.xz')
 for rel,h in expected.items():
  if not target(rel).is_file() or sha(target(rel))!=h.lower():raise ValueError('Dependency verification failed: '+rel)
 print(json.dumps({'success':True,'filesVerified':len(expected),'correspondingSourcesRestored':a.include_corresponding_sources}))
if __name__=='__main__':main()


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/stage-package-acceptance.ps1 ---
param(
  [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$PackageDirectory,
  [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$DestinationDirectory
)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$projectDirectory = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent)).TrimEnd('\')
$packageRoot = (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $PackageDirectory).Path.TrimEnd('\')
$payload = if (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $packageRoot 'app\YourCatFileAssistant.dll')) { Join-Path $packageRoot 'app' } else { $packageRoot }
$destination = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($DestinationDirectory)
if (-not $destination.StartsWith($projectDirectory + '\', [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { throw 'Acceptance staging must remain inside the workspace.' }
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $destination) {
  if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $destination -PathType Container) -or @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $destination -Force).Count -gt 0) { throw 'Acceptance staging requires a new or empty directory; it never replaces a previous runtime.' }
}
$cursor = $destination
while ($cursor.Length -ge $projectDirectory.Length) {
  $entry = Get-Item -LiteralPath $cursor -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
  if ($null -ne $entry -and ($entry.Attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -ne 0) { throw 'Acceptance staging must not traverse a reparse point.' }
  if ($cursor -eq $projectDirectory) { break }
  $cursor = Split-Path $cursor -Parent
}
. (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'release-version.ps1')
$releaseInfo = Get-CatReleaseInfo -ProjectDirectory $projectDirectory
$productVersion = [Diagnostics.FileVersionInfo]::GetVersionInfo((Join-Path $payload 'YourCatFileAssistant.dll')).ProductVersion
if (($productVersion -split '\+')[0] -ne $releaseInfo.Version) { throw 'Published package version does not match source.' }
$environmentNames = @('DOTNET_ROOT','DOTNET_ROOT_X64','DOTNET_CLI_HOME','NUGET_PACKAGES','DOTNET_CLI_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT','DOTNET_NOLOGO')
$previousEnvironment = @{}
foreach ($name in $environmentNames) { $previousEnvironment[$name] = [Environment]::GetEnvironmentVariable($name, 'Process') }
try {
  $dotnet = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\dotnet\sdk\dotnet.exe'
  $env:DOTNET_ROOT = Split-Path $dotnet -Parent
  $env:DOTNET_ROOT_X64 = $env:DOTNET_ROOT
  $env:DOTNET_CLI_HOME = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\dotnet\home'
  $env:NUGET_PACKAGES = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\dotnet\packages'
  $env:DOTNET_CLI_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT = '1'
  $env:DOTNET_NOLOGO = '1'
  & $dotnet publish (Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tests\CatFileAssistant.Acceptance') -c Release -m:1 -r win-x64 --self-contained true -p:NuGetAudit=false -o $destination
  if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Self-contained acceptance publication failed.' }
  $checks = [ordered]@{}
  $required = @('YourCatFileAssistant.dll','CatFileAssistant.Core.dll','CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure.dll')
  $required += @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath (Join-Path $payload 'shared') -Filter '*.json' -File | ForEach-Object { 'shared\' + $_.Name })
  foreach ($relative in $required) {
    $packageFile = Join-Path $payload $relative
    $runnerFile = Join-Path $destination $relative
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $runnerFile -PathType Leaf)) { throw "Acceptance runtime is missing: $relative" }
    $packageHash = (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $packageFile -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant()
    if ($packageHash -cne (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $runnerFile -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant()) { throw "Acceptance file differs from actual package: $relative" }
    $checks[$relative.Replace('\','/')] = $packageHash
  }
  # The restored release must retain the original v2 art, including all body PNGs.
  $expectedPetVersion = 2
  $petReport = Join-Path $destination 'staged-pet-assets.json'
  & (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'verify-pet-assets.ps1') -PetDirectory (Join-Path $destination 'Assets\Pet') -ReferenceDirectory (Join-Path $payload 'Assets\Pet') -ExpectedManifestVersion $expectedPetVersion -ReportPath $petReport | Out-Null
  $reportPath = Join-Path $destination 'staging-report.json'
  [ordered]@{ checkedAt=[DateTimeOffset]::Now.ToString('o'); version=$releaseInfo.Version; package=$packageRoot; payload=$payload; runner=$destination;
    productFiles=$checks; petAssetsReport=$petReport; success=$true;
    scope='The runner is self-contained and uses byte-identical product assemblies, shared JSON and the complete Pet asset tree. No visual, performance or installer tests have run yet.' } |
    ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $reportPath -Encoding utf8
  [pscustomobject]@{ runner=(Join-Path $destination 'CatFileAssistant.Acceptance.exe'); report=$reportPath; productVersion=$productVersion }
} finally {
  foreach ($name in $environmentNames) { [Environment]::SetEnvironmentVariable($name, $previousEnvironment[$name], 'Process') }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/verify-installer-guards.ps1 ---
param([string]$ReportDirectory)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$projectDirectory = Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent
if (-not $ReportDirectory) { $ReportDirectory = Join-Path $projectDirectory ('artifacts\installer-verifier-negatives\' + (Get-Date -Format 'yyyyMMdd-HHmmss') + '-' + [Guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N').Substring(0,8)) }
$ReportDirectory = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($ReportDirectory)
if (-not $ReportDirectory.StartsWith([IO.Path]::GetFullPath($projectDirectory).TrimEnd('\') + '\', [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { throw 'Guard verification output must remain inside the workspace.' }
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ReportDirectory) {
  if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ReportDirectory -PathType Container) -or @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $ReportDirectory -Force).Count -gt 0) { throw 'Guard verification requires a new or empty report directory and never replaces existing files.' }
}
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $ReportDirectory | Out-Null
. (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'release-version.ps1')
$releaseInfo = Get-CatReleaseInfo -ProjectDirectory $projectDirectory
$productionName = Join-Path $ReportDirectory ($releaseInfo.PackageName + '-Setup.exe')
$testName = Join-Path $ReportDirectory ('CatFileAssistant-V' + $releaseInfo.Version + '-Test-Setup.exe')
# These are plain text, not executable installers. No real installer is ever used by these negative checks.
foreach ($path in @($productionName, $testName)) { [IO.File]::WriteAllText($path, 'Non-executable placeholder for installer safety guard verification.', [Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false)) }
$checks = [Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
function Expect-Rejection([string]$Name, [string]$Path, [string]$ExpectedMessage) {
  $message = 'Verifier unexpectedly accepted the invalid input.'
  try { & (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'verify-installer.ps1') -TestInstallerPath $Path | Out-Null }
  catch { $message = $_.Exception.Message }
  $checks.Add([ordered]@{ name=$Name; passed=$message -match $ExpectedMessage; message=$message })
}
Expect-Rejection 'production installer name refused' $productionName 'Only the separately identified TestMode'
Expect-Rejection 'missing TestMode receipt refused' $testName 'Missing TestMode build receipt'
$receipt = [ordered]@{ schemaVersion=2; testMode=$true; appId='{F16F864F-D8DE-4508-93DC-8F9925D674B2}'; dataIsolation='installation-local'; shortcuts='installation-local'; shortcutRoot='app/installer-test-shortcuts'; installerSha256=('0' * 64) }
function Save-Receipt { $receipt | ConvertTo-Json | Set-Content -LiteralPath ($testName + '.test.json') -Encoding utf8 }
Save-Receipt
Expect-Rejection 'tampered installer hash refused' $testName 'TestMode receipt or installer hash is invalid'
$receipt.installerSha256 = (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $testName -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant()
$receipt.shortcuts = $false
Save-Receipt
Expect-Rejection 'non-isolated shortcut declaration refused' $testName 'TestMode receipt or installer hash is invalid'
$receipt.shortcuts = 'installation-local'
$receipt.shortcutRoot = 'user-desktop'
Save-Receipt
Expect-Rejection 'non-isolated shortcut path refused' $testName 'TestMode receipt or installer hash is invalid'
$receipt.shortcutRoot = 'app/installer-test-shortcuts'
$receipt.schemaVersion = 1
Save-Receipt
Expect-Rejection 'obsolete receipt schema refused' $testName 'TestMode receipt or installer hash is invalid'
$report = [ordered]@{ checkedAt=(Get-Date).ToString('o'); success=(@($checks | Where-Object { -not $_.passed }).Count -eq 0); checks=$checks; realInstallersUsed=$false; placeholdersExecutable=$false }
$report | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 5 | Set-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $ReportDirectory 'report.json') -Encoding utf8
Write-Output (Join-Path $ReportDirectory 'report.json')
if (-not $report.success) { throw 'Installer verifier negative guard checks failed.' }


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/verify-installer.ps1 ---
param([Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$TestInstallerPath)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$projectDirectory = Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent
$TestInstallerPath = (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $TestInstallerPath).Path
if ([IO.Path]::GetFileName($TestInstallerPath) -notmatch '^CatFileAssistant-V\d+\.\d+\.\d+-Test-Setup\.exe$') {
  throw 'Only the separately identified TestMode installer may be used for automated install/uninstall verification.'
}
$testMetadataPath = $TestInstallerPath + '.test.json'
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $testMetadataPath -PathType Leaf)) { throw 'Missing TestMode build receipt. Build the installer using build-installer.ps1 -TestMode.' }
$testMetadata = Get-Content -LiteralPath $testMetadataPath -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
$installerHash = (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $TestInstallerPath -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant()
if ($testMetadata.schemaVersion -ne 2 -or $testMetadata.testMode -ne $true -or $testMetadata.appId -ne '{F16F864F-D8DE-4508-93DC-8F9925D674B2}' -or $testMetadata.dataIsolation -ne 'installation-local' -or $testMetadata.shortcuts -ne 'installation-local' -or $testMetadata.shortcutRoot -ne 'app/installer-test-shortcuts' -or $testMetadata.installerSha256 -ine $installerHash) { throw 'TestMode receipt or installer hash is invalid; refusing to execute.' }
$testRegistryKey = 'HKCU:\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Uninstall\{F16F864F-D8DE-4508-93DC-8F9925D674B2}_is1'
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $testRegistryKey) { throw 'A previous isolated installer test is still registered. Uninstall that test before rerunning.' }
$reportRoot = Join-Path $projectDirectory ('artifacts\installer-verification\' + (Get-Date -Format 'yyyyMMdd-HHmmss') + '-' + [Guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N').Substring(0,8))
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $reportRoot | Out-Null
$checks = [Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
$report = [ordered]@{ checkedAt=(Get-Date).ToString('o'); installer=$TestInstallerPath; installerSha256=$installerHash; buildReceipt=$testMetadataPath; isolatedAppId=$testRegistryKey; reportDirectory=$reportRoot; success=$false; checks=$checks; limitations=@('TestMode actually creates desktop and start-menu shortcuts using the shared production Icons/Tasks entries, but redirects their folders under the test installation. Appearance on the real production desktop/start menu and OS app-list presentation require separate interactive checks.','TestMode uses installation-local application data. The bundled runtime is verified by its loaded coreclr path; the host also has a development SDK and is not a clean VM.') }
$liveProduct = $null
function Save-Report { $report | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 8 | Set-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $reportRoot 'report.json') -Encoding utf8 }
function Assert-Check([string]$Name, [bool]$Condition) {
  $checks.Add([ordered]@{name=$Name;passed=$Condition})
  Save-Report
  if (-not $Condition) { throw "Installer verification failed: $Name" }
}
function Run-InstallerProcess([string]$File, [string[]]$Arguments, [string]$LogName) {
  $process = Start-Process -FilePath $File -ArgumentList ($Arguments + ('/LOG="' + (Join-Path $reportRoot $LogName) + '"')) -WindowStyle Hidden -PassThru
  try {
    if (-not $process.WaitForExit(180000)) { throw "Installer process timed out: $LogName (PID $($process.Id))" }
    return $process.ExitCode
  } finally { $process.Dispose() }
}
function Install-Test([string]$Directory, [string]$Language, [string]$LogName) {
  $code = Run-InstallerProcess $TestInstallerPath @('/VERYSILENT','/SUPPRESSMSGBOXES','/NORESTART',('/LANG=' + $Language),('/DIR="' + $Directory + '"')) $LogName
  Assert-Check ($LogName + ': setup completed') ($code -eq 0)
  Assert-Check ($LogName + ': test isolation marker') ((Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $Directory 'app\installer-test-mode') -Raw) -eq 'isolated-installer-test')
  $seed = Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $Directory 'app\installed-language.json') -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
  $expected = if ($Language -eq 'en_US') { 'en-US' } else { 'zh-CN' }
  Assert-Check ($LogName + ': language seed') ($seed.language -eq $expected)
}
function Write-UserFile([string]$Path, [string]$Text) {
  New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path (Split-Path $Path -Parent) | Out-Null
  [IO.File]::WriteAllText($Path, $Text, [Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))
  return [pscustomobject]@{Path=$Path;Hash=(Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $Path -Algorithm SHA256).Hash}
}
function Check-UserFiles([object[]]$Files, [string]$Stage) {
  foreach ($file in $Files) {
    Assert-Check ($Stage + ': preserve ' + $file.Path.Substring($reportRoot.Length + 1)) ((Test-Path -LiteralPath $file.Path -PathType Leaf) -and (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $file.Path -Algorithm SHA256).Hash -eq $file.Hash)
  }
}
function Check-Shortcuts([string]$Directory, [string]$Stage) {
  $shortcutRoot = Join-Path $Directory 'app\installer-test-shortcuts'
  $shortcuts = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $shortcutRoot -Filter '*.lnk' -File -Recurse)
  Assert-Check ($Stage + ': exactly two isolated shortcuts, with no duplicates') ($shortcuts.Count -eq 2)
  $shell = New-Object -ComObject WScript.Shell
  try {
    foreach ($folder in @('Desktop', 'StartMenu')) {
      $path = Join-Path $shortcutRoot ($folder + '\你的猫咪文件助手.lnk')
      Assert-Check ($Stage + ': ' + $folder + ' shortcut exists') (Test-Path -LiteralPath $path -PathType Leaf)
      $shortcut = $shell.CreateShortcut($path)
      try {
        $launcher = Join-Path $Directory '你的猫咪文件助手.exe'
        Assert-Check ($Stage + ': ' + $folder + ' target is root launcher') ($shortcut.TargetPath -ieq $launcher)
        Assert-Check ($Stage + ': ' + $folder + ' working directory is installation root') ($shortcut.WorkingDirectory.TrimEnd('\') -ieq $Directory.TrimEnd('\'))
        $icon = $shortcut.IconLocation
        $iconSeparator = $icon.LastIndexOf(',')
        $validIcon = $iconSeparator -gt 0 -and $icon.Substring(0, $iconSeparator).Trim('"') -ieq $launcher -and $icon.Substring($iconSeparator + 1).Trim() -eq '0'
        Assert-Check ($Stage + ': ' + $folder + ' icon is root launcher') $validIcon
        Assert-Check ($Stage + ': ' + $folder + ' has no launch arguments') ([string]::IsNullOrEmpty($shortcut.Arguments))
      } finally { [Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::FinalReleaseComObject($shortcut) | Out-Null }
    }
  } finally { [Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::FinalReleaseComObject($shell) | Out-Null }
}
function Check-ShortcutsRemoved([string]$Directory, [string]$Stage, [bool]$ExpectEmptyDirectoryRemoval) {
  $shortcutRoot = Join-Path $Directory 'app\installer-test-shortcuts'
  $remaining = @(if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $shortcutRoot) { Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $shortcutRoot -Filter '*.lnk' -File -Recurse })
  Assert-Check ($Stage + ': all installed shortcuts removed') ($remaining.Count -eq 0)
  if ($ExpectEmptyDirectoryRemoval) { Assert-Check ($Stage + ': empty isolated shortcut directories removed') (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $shortcutRoot)) }
}
function Data-Snapshot([string]$Directory) {
  if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Directory)) { return @() }
  return @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Directory -Recurse -File | ForEach-Object { $_.FullName.Substring($Directory.Length) + ':' + (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Algorithm SHA256).Hash } | Sort-Object)
}
function Shortcut-Snapshot {
  foreach ($folder in @([Environment]::GetFolderPath('DesktopDirectory'), [Environment]::GetFolderPath('Programs'))) {
    $shortcut = Join-Path $folder '你的猫咪文件助手.lnk'
    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $shortcut -PathType Leaf) { $shortcut + ':' + (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $shortcut -Algorithm SHA256).Hash }
    else { $shortcut + ':missing' }
  }
}
function Check-InternalUninstallerRemoved([string]$Directory, [string]$Stage) {
  $uninstallDirectory = Join-Path $Directory 'app\uninstall'
  $clock = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
  do {
    $remaining = @(if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $uninstallDirectory) { Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $uninstallDirectory -File -Force })
    if ($remaining.Count -eq 0) { break }
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 200
  } while ($clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds -lt 15)
  Assert-Check ($Stage + ': internal uninstaller and its records remove themselves') ($remaining.Count -eq 0)
}
$realData = Join-Path ([Environment]::GetFolderPath('LocalApplicationData')) 'CatFileAssistant'
$beforeData = Data-Snapshot $realData
$beforeShortcuts = @(Shortcut-Snapshot)
try {
  $installA = Join-Path $reportRoot '中文 安装目录'
  Install-Test $installA 'zh_CN' 'install-zh.log'
  Check-Shortcuts $installA 'Chinese installation'
  $rootFiles = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $installA -File | Select-Object -ExpandProperty Name)
  Assert-Check 'clean root has only main and uninstall launchers' ($rootFiles.Count -eq 2 -and '你的猫咪文件助手.exe' -in $rootFiles -and '卸载程序.exe' -in $rootFiles)
  Assert-Check 'isolated uninstall registration exists' (Test-Path -LiteralPath $testRegistryKey)
  $dataA = Join-Path $installA 'installer-test-data'
  $settings = Write-UserFile (Join-Path $dataA 'settings.json') ('{"Language":"zh-CN","OutputDirectory":' + ((Join-Path $installA 'Converted Files') | ConvertTo-Json -Compress) + ',"Pet":{"Enabled":false}}')
  $petSave = Write-UserFile (Join-Path $dataA 'pet-state.json') '{}'
  $userFiles = @(
    (Write-UserFile (Join-Path $installA 'my-source.txt') 'source file in install root'),
    (Write-UserFile (Join-Path $installA 'Converted Files\result.txt') 'converted result inside install root'),
    (Write-UserFile (Join-Path $installA 'app\Converted Files\result.txt') 'converted result beside program dependencies'),
    (Write-UserFile (Join-Path $installA 'app\installer-test-shortcuts\Desktop\my-note.txt') 'user file beside a generated shortcut'),
    (Write-UserFile (Join-Path $dataA 'my-personal-file.txt') 'user file inside app data'),
    (Write-UserFile (Join-Path $reportRoot 'external outputs\result.txt') 'converted file outside installation')
  )
  Install-Test $installA 'en_US' 'upgrade-en.log'
  Check-Shortcuts $installA 'English upgrade'
  $installedA = @((Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $installA 'app\installation-files.json') -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json).files | ForEach-Object { Join-Path $installA $_.path }) + @((Join-Path $installA 'app\installation-files.json'),(Join-Path $installA 'app\installed-language.json'),(Join-Path $installA 'app\installer-test-mode'))
  Check-UserFiles @($settings,$petSave) 'upgrade preserves saved preferences and cat'
  Assert-Check 'saved language takes precedence over a different installer language' ((Get-Content -LiteralPath $settings.Path -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json).Language -eq 'zh-CN')
  Check-UserFiles $userFiles 'upgrade'
  $executable = Join-Path $installA '你的猫咪文件助手.exe'
  $liveProduct = Start-Process -FilePath $executable -WorkingDirectory $installA -WindowStyle Hidden -PassThru
  $clock = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
  while ($clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds -lt 20) {
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 200
    $liveProduct.Refresh()
    if ($liveProduct.HasExited -or $liveProduct.MainWindowHandle -ne 0) { break }
  }
  Assert-Check 'installed app opens a real window' (-not $liveProduct.HasExited -and $liveProduct.MainWindowHandle -ne 0)
  $module = $liveProduct.Modules | Where-Object ModuleName -EQ 'coreclr.dll' | Select-Object -First 1
  Assert-Check 'installed app uses bundled runtime under app folder' ($null -ne $module -and $module.FileName.StartsWith((Join-Path $installA 'app') + '\', [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase))
  $exitRequest = Start-Process -FilePath $executable -ArgumentList '--installer-exit' -WorkingDirectory $installA -WindowStyle Hidden -PassThru
  try {
    $exitRequestExited = $exitRequest.WaitForExit(70000)
    $report.exitHandshake = [ordered]@{ helperExited=$exitRequestExited; helperExitCode=$(if ($exitRequestExited) { $exitRequest.ExitCode } else { $null }) }
    Assert-Check 'installer exit handshake succeeds' ($exitRequestExited -and $exitRequest.ExitCode -eq 0)
  } finally { $exitRequest.Dispose() }
  Assert-Check 'installer exit waits for full product shutdown' ($liveProduct.WaitForExit(5000) -and $liveProduct.ExitCode -eq 0)
  $liveProduct.Dispose(); $liveProduct = $null
  [IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $dataA 'settings.json'), '{"Language":"en-US"}')
  $lock = [IO.File]::Open((Join-Path $dataA 'settings.json'), [IO.FileMode]::Open, [IO.FileAccess]::ReadWrite, [IO.FileShare]::None)
  try {
    $failed = Run-InstallerProcess (Join-Path $installA 'app\uninstall\unins000.exe') @('/VERYSILENT','/SUPPRESSMSGBOXES','/NORESTART') 'locked-data-refusal.log'
    Assert-Check 'locked app data cancels uninstall' ($failed -ne 0 -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath $executable) -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath $testRegistryKey))
  } finally { $lock.Dispose() }
  $keepSettingsHash = (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath (Join-Path $dataA 'settings.json') -Algorithm SHA256).Hash
  $keepPetHash = (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath (Join-Path $dataA 'pet-state.json') -Algorithm SHA256).Hash
  Write-UserFile (Join-Path $dataA 'logs\diagnostics.log') 'owned log' | Out-Null
  $rootUninstall = Start-Process -FilePath (Join-Path $installA '卸载程序.exe') -ArgumentList @('/VERYSILENT','/SUPPRESSMSGBOXES','/NORESTART','/KEEPSAVE=1',('/LOG="' + (Join-Path $reportRoot 'keep-save-root-entry.log') + '"')) -WindowStyle Hidden -PassThru
  try { Assert-Check 'root uninstall launcher starts' ($rootUninstall.WaitForExit(15000) -and $rootUninstall.ExitCode -eq 0) } finally { $rootUninstall.Dispose() }
  $clock.Restart()
  while (((Test-Path -LiteralPath $executable) -or (Test-Path -LiteralPath $testRegistryKey)) -and $clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds -lt 90) { Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 300 }
  Assert-Check 'root uninstall entry removes installed program' (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $executable) -and -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $testRegistryKey))
  Check-InternalUninstallerRemoved $installA 'keep-save uninstall'
  Check-ShortcutsRemoved $installA 'keep-save uninstall' $false
  $remainingA = @($installedA | Where-Object { Test-Path -LiteralPath $_ -PathType Leaf })
  Assert-Check 'keep-save uninstall removes every recorded program file' ($remainingA.Count -eq 0)
  Assert-Check 'keep-save preserves settings exactly' ((Get-FileHash -LiteralPath (Join-Path $dataA 'settings.json') -Algorithm SHA256).Hash -eq $keepSettingsHash)
  Assert-Check 'keep-save preserves cat exactly' ((Get-FileHash -LiteralPath (Join-Path $dataA 'pet-state.json') -Algorithm SHA256).Hash -eq $keepPetHash)
  Assert-Check 'keep-save still removes owned logs' (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $dataA 'logs\diagnostics.log')))
  Check-UserFiles $userFiles 'keep-save uninstall'
  $installB = Join-Path $reportRoot 'English Install Folder'
  Install-Test $installB 'en_US' 'install-en.log'
  Check-Shortcuts $installB 'English installation'
  $installedB = @((Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $installB 'app\installation-files.json') -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json).files | ForEach-Object { Join-Path $installB $_.path }) + @((Join-Path $installB 'app\installation-files.json'),(Join-Path $installB 'app\installed-language.json'),(Join-Path $installB 'app\installer-test-mode'))
  $dataB = Join-Path $installB 'installer-test-data'
  Write-UserFile (Join-Path $dataB 'settings.json') '{"Language":"en-US"}' | Out-Null
  Write-UserFile (Join-Path $dataB 'pet-state.json') '{}' | Out-Null
  Write-UserFile (Join-Path $dataB 'logs\diagnostics.log') 'owned log' | Out-Null
  Write-UserFile (Join-Path $dataB 'owned-temporary-files.json.tmp') 'interrupted receipt write' | Out-Null
  $secondUser = @((Write-UserFile (Join-Path $dataB 'converted-result.txt') 'must survive'), (Write-UserFile (Join-Path $installB 'app\output.txt') 'must survive'))
  $junction = Join-Path $installB 'linked-user-files'
  New-Item -ItemType Junction -Path $junction -Target (Join-Path $reportRoot 'external outputs') | Out-Null
  try {
    $failed = Run-InstallerProcess (Join-Path $installB 'app\uninstall\unins000.exe') @('/VERYSILENT','/SUPPRESSMSGBOXES','/NORESTART') 'reparse-refusal.log'
    Assert-Check 'reparse tree cancels uninstall before deleting anything' ($failed -ne 0 -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $installB '你的猫咪文件助手.exe')))
    Check-UserFiles $userFiles 'reparse refusal'
  } finally {
    $absoluteJunction = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($junction)
    if (-not $absoluteJunction.StartsWith($reportRoot + '\', [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase) -or ((Get-Item -LiteralPath $absoluteJunction).Attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -eq 0) { throw 'Unexpected junction cleanup target.' }
    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $absoluteJunction -Force
  }
  $removed = Run-InstallerProcess (Join-Path $installB 'app\uninstall\unins000.exe') @('/VERYSILENT','/SUPPRESSMSGBOXES','/NORESTART') 'default-clean-internal-entry.log'
  Assert-Check 'internal uninstall entry succeeds' ($removed -eq 0)
  Assert-Check 'default uninstall removes known settings cat logs and receipt files' (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $dataB 'settings.json')) -and -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $dataB 'pet-state.json')) -and -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $dataB 'logs\diagnostics.log')) -and -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $dataB 'owned-temporary-files.json.tmp')))
  Assert-Check 'default uninstall removes program and registration' (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $installB '你的猫咪文件助手.exe')) -and -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $installB 'app\YourCatFileAssistant.dll')) -and -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $testRegistryKey))
  Check-InternalUninstallerRemoved $installB 'default uninstall'
  Check-ShortcutsRemoved $installB 'default uninstall' $true
  $remainingB = @($installedB | Where-Object { Test-Path -LiteralPath $_ -PathType Leaf })
  Assert-Check 'default uninstall removes every recorded program file' ($remainingB.Count -eq 0)
  Check-UserFiles $secondUser 'default uninstall'
  Check-UserFiles $userFiles 'default uninstall other locations'
  $afterData = Data-Snapshot $realData
  Assert-Check 'real user app data is unchanged by isolated tests' (($beforeData -join "`n") -eq ($afterData -join "`n"))
  Assert-Check 'real user desktop and start-menu shortcuts are unchanged by isolated tests' (($beforeShortcuts -join "`n") -eq (@(Shortcut-Snapshot) -join "`n"))
  $report.success = $true
  Save-Report
  Write-Output (Join-Path $reportRoot 'report.json')
} catch {
  $report.error = $_.Exception.Message
  Save-Report
  throw
} finally {
  if ($liveProduct) { try { if (-not $liveProduct.HasExited) { $liveProduct.Kill(); $liveProduct.WaitForExit() } } finally { $liveProduct.Dispose() } }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/verify-package.ps1 ---
param([string]$PackageDirectory, [string]$ArchivePath, [string]$SourceArchivePath, [switch]$RunConversions, [string]$Version)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$projectDirectory = Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent
. (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'release-version.ps1')
$releaseInfo = Get-CatReleaseInfo -ProjectDirectory $projectDirectory -Version $Version
$archiveHash = $null
$verifiedArchiveFiles = $null
$reportDirectory = Join-Path $projectDirectory ('artifacts\package-verification\' + (Get-Date -Format 'yyyyMMdd-HHmmss') + '-' + [Guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N').Substring(0,6))
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $reportDirectory | Out-Null
if ($ArchivePath) {
  $ArchivePath = (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $ArchivePath).Path
  $archiveHash = (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $ArchivePath -Algorithm SHA256).Hash
  $extractedDirectory = Join-Path $reportDirectory 'extracted'
  Expand-Archive -LiteralPath $ArchivePath -DestinationPath $extractedDirectory
  $candidates = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $extractedDirectory -Directory | Where-Object { Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $_.FullName '你的猫咪文件助手.exe') })
  if ($candidates.Count -ne 1) { throw 'Archive does not contain one complete application directory.' }
  $PackageDirectory = $candidates[0].FullName
  $referenceDirectory = Join-Path $projectDirectory ('release\' + $releaseInfo.PackageName)
  if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $referenceDirectory) {
    $referenceFiles = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $referenceDirectory -Recurse -File)
    $extractedFiles = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $PackageDirectory -Recurse -File)
    if ($referenceFiles.Count -ne $extractedFiles.Count) { throw 'Archive file count differs from the published directory.' }
    foreach ($referenceFile in $referenceFiles) {
      $relativePath = $referenceFile.FullName.Substring($referenceDirectory.Length + 1)
      $extractedFile = Join-Path $PackageDirectory $relativePath
      if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $extractedFile) -or (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $referenceFile.FullName).Hash -ne (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $extractedFile).Hash) { throw "Archive file differs: $relativePath" }
    }
    $verifiedArchiveFiles = $referenceFiles.Count
  }
}
if (-not $PackageDirectory) { $PackageDirectory = Join-Path $projectDirectory ('release\' + $releaseInfo.PackageName) }
$packagePath = (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $PackageDirectory).Path.TrimEnd('\')
$installationRoot = $packagePath
$cleanLayout = Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $installationRoot 'app\YourCatFileAssistant.dll')
if ($cleanLayout) {
  $rootFileNames = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $installationRoot -File | Select-Object -ExpandProperty Name | Sort-Object)
  if ($rootFileNames.Count -ne 2 -or '你的猫咪文件助手.exe' -notin $rootFileNames -or '卸载程序.exe' -notin $rootFileNames) { throw 'V1 installation root is not clean.' }
  $packagePath = Join-Path $installationRoot 'app'
  $installationManifest = Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $packagePath 'installation-files.json') -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
  foreach ($entry in $installationManifest.files) {
    $installedPath = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Join-Path $installationRoot $entry.path))
    if (-not $installedPath.StartsWith($installationRoot + '\', [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase) -or -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $installedPath -PathType Leaf) -or (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $installedPath -Algorithm SHA256).Hash -ine $entry.sha256) { throw "Installed-file manifest mismatch: $($entry.path)" }
  }
}
$sourceBundleRoot = $null
if ($SourceArchivePath) {
  $SourceArchivePath = (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $SourceArchivePath).Path
  $sourceExtractedDirectory = Join-Path $reportDirectory 'sources'
  Expand-Archive -LiteralPath $SourceArchivePath -DestinationPath $sourceExtractedDirectory
  $sourceCandidates = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $sourceExtractedDirectory -Directory | Where-Object { Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $_.FullName 'tools\ffmpeg\source\distribution-manifest.json') })
  if ($sourceCandidates.Count -ne 1) { throw 'Source archive does not contain one complete source root.' }
  $sourceBundleRoot = Join-Path $sourceCandidates[0].FullName 'tools\ffmpeg\source'
  $applicationSourceManifestPath = Join-Path $sourceCandidates[0].FullName 'application-source-manifest.json'
  if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $applicationSourceManifestPath)) { throw 'Matching application source manifest missing.' }
  $applicationSources = Get-Content -LiteralPath $applicationSourceManifestPath -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
  if ($applicationSources.applicationVersion -ne $releaseInfo.Version) { throw "Application source version does not match $($releaseInfo.Version)." }
  $projectPrefix = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($projectDirectory).TrimEnd('\') + '\'
  $sourcePrefix = $sourceCandidates[0].FullName.TrimEnd('\') + '\'
  foreach ($sourceEntry in $applicationSources.files) {
    $deliveredSource = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Join-Path $sourceCandidates[0].FullName $sourceEntry.path))
    if (-not $deliveredSource.StartsWith($sourcePrefix, [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase) -or -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $deliveredSource -PathType Leaf) -or (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $deliveredSource -Algorithm SHA256).Hash -ine $sourceEntry.sha256) { throw "Delivered application source differs from its manifest: $($sourceEntry.path)" }
    if ($sourceEntry.path -match '^(src|shared|scripts|tests)/') {
      $referenceSource = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Join-Path $projectDirectory $sourceEntry.path))
      if (-not $referenceSource.StartsWith($projectPrefix, [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase) -or -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $referenceSource -PathType Leaf) -or (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $referenceSource -Algorithm SHA256).Hash -ine $sourceEntry.sha256) { throw "Application corresponding source differs: $($sourceEntry.path)" }
    }
  }
  & (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'audit-licenses.ps1') -PackagePath $packagePath -SourceBundlePath $sourceBundleRoot -ReportPath (Join-Path $reportDirectory 'license-audit.json')
} else {
  & (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'audit-licenses.ps1') -PackagePath $packagePath -IntegrityOnly -ReportPath (Join-Path $reportDirectory 'license-integrity-only.json')
}
$executable = Join-Path $installationRoot '你的猫咪文件助手.exe'
$engineDirectory = Join-Path $packagePath 'tools\ffmpeg\bin'
foreach ($required in @('YourCatFileAssistant.dll','YourCatFileAssistant.runtimeconfig.json','coreclr.dll','hostfxr.dll','PresentationFramework.dll','tools\ffmpeg\bin\ffmpeg.exe','tools\ffmpeg\bin\ffprobe.exe','licenses\dotnet\LICENSE.txt','licenses\dotnet\ThirdPartyNotices.txt','shared\presets.json','docs\compression.md','docs\gif.md','docs\archives.md','tools\7zip\bin\7z.dll','tools\7zip\bin\7z.exe','worker\CatFileAssistant.ArchiveWorker.exe','worker\CatFileAssistant.ArchiveWorker.runtimeconfig.json','worker\coreclr.dll','licenses\7zip\License.txt','licenses\7zip\copying.txt','licenses\7zip\7z2604-src.tar.xz','licenses\7zip\provenance.json','licenses\archive-worker\source\CatFileAssistant.ArchiveWorker.csproj','licenses\archive-worker\source\NativeInterop.cs','licenses\archive-worker\README.md','licenses\archive-worker\LICENSE.txt')) {
  if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $packagePath $required))) { throw "Package file missing: $required" }
}
$productVersion = [Diagnostics.FileVersionInfo]::GetVersionInfo((Join-Path $packagePath 'YourCatFileAssistant.dll')).ProductVersion
foreach ($recoveryFile in @('docs\password-recovery.md','shared\recovery-patterns-provenance.json','licenses\archive-worker\source\Recovery.cs')) {
  if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $packagePath $recoveryFile))) { throw "Recovery package file missing: $recoveryFile" }
}
if (($productVersion -split '\+')[0] -ne $releaseInfo.Version) { throw "Unexpected product version: $productVersion" }
foreach ($petFile in @('docs\desktop-pet.md','Assets\Pet\animations.json','Assets\Pet\generation-prompts.json','visual-assets\pet\animations.json')) {
  if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $packagePath $petFile))) { throw "Desktop pet package file missing: $petFile" }
}
# V1.0.1 deliberately restores the original drawings after new-animation cancellation.
$expectedPetVersion = 2
$petAssetsReport = Join-Path $reportDirectory 'pet-assets.json'
& (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'verify-pet-assets.ps1') -PetDirectory (Join-Path $packagePath 'Assets\Pet') -ReferenceDirectory (Join-Path $projectDirectory 'src\CatFileAssistant.Desktop\Assets\Pet') -ExportDirectory (Join-Path $packagePath 'visual-assets\pet') -ExpectedManifestVersion $expectedPetVersion -ReportPath $petAssetsReport | Out-Null
foreach ($imageFile in @('docs\image-obfuscation.md','licenses\gilbert\LICENSE','licenses\gilbert\gilbert2d.py','licenses\gilbert\provenance.json','licenses\dotnet\Core-Runtime-LICENSE.txt','licenses\dotnet\Core-Runtime-THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.txt','licenses\dotnet\Windows-Desktop-Runtime-LICENSE.txt')) {
  if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $packagePath $imageFile))) { throw "Package file missing: $imageFile" }
}
$gilbertProvenance = Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $packagePath 'licenses\gilbert\provenance.json') -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
foreach ($licenseFile in @('THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md','docs\third-party-audit.md','tools\ffmpeg\provenance.json','tools\ffmpeg\component-manifest.json','tools\ffmpeg\licenses\COPYING.GPLv3','tools\ffmpeg\source\README.md','tools\ffmpeg\source\distribution-manifest.json','licenses\dotnet\provenance.json')) {
  if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $packagePath $licenseFile))) { throw "License/source material missing: $licenseFile" }
}
foreach ($gilbertFile in $gilbertProvenance.files) {
  $gilbertPath = Join-Path $packagePath ('licenses\gilbert\' + $gilbertFile.path)
  if ((Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $gilbertPath -Algorithm SHA256).Hash -ine $gilbertFile.sha256) { throw "Gilbert original source/license hash mismatch: $($gilbertFile.path)" }
}
$archiveMetadata = Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $packagePath 'licenses\7zip\provenance.json') -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
if ((Get-FileHash -LiteralPath (Join-Path $packagePath 'tools\7zip\bin\7z.dll')).Hash -ne $archiveMetadata.dllSha256) { throw 'Packaged 7-Zip DLL checksum mismatch.' }
if ((Get-FileHash -LiteralPath (Join-Path $packagePath 'licenses\7zip\7z2604-src.tar.xz')).Hash -ne $archiveMetadata.sourceSha256) { throw 'Packaged 7-Zip source checksum mismatch.' }
foreach ($sourceFile in (Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath (Join-Path $projectDirectory 'src\CatFileAssistant.ArchiveWorker') -File | Where-Object { $_.Extension -in @('.cs','.csproj') })) {
  $packagedSource = Join-Path $packagePath ('licenses\archive-worker\source\' + $sourceFile.Name)
  if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $packagedSource) -or (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $sourceFile.FullName).Hash -ne (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $packagedSource).Hash) { throw ('Corresponding archive worker source differs: ' + $sourceFile.Name) }
}
$originalPath = $env:PATH
$originalDotnetRoot = $env:DOTNET_ROOT
$originalDotnetRootX64 = $env:DOTNET_ROOT_X64
$originalCliHome = $env:DOTNET_CLI_HOME
$originalPackages = $env:NUGET_PACKAGES
$originalTelemetry = $env:DOTNET_CLI_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT
$originalNoLogo = $env:DOTNET_NOLOGO
$ownedProcess = $null
try {
  # Test the real product apphost without discovering the local development SDK or a system engine.
  $env:PATH = "$env:SystemRoot\System32;$env:SystemRoot"
  $env:DOTNET_ROOT = Join-Path $reportDirectory 'no-installed-runtime'
  $env:DOTNET_ROOT_X64 = $env:DOTNET_ROOT
  $ownedProcess = Start-Process -FilePath $executable -ArgumentList '--verify-package' -WorkingDirectory $packagePath -WindowStyle Hidden -PassThru
  $clock = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
  while ($clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds -lt 15) {
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 200
    $ownedProcess.Refresh()
    if ($ownedProcess.HasExited) { throw "Product exited during startup: $($ownedProcess.ExitCode)" }
    if ($ownedProcess.MainWindowHandle -ne 0) { break }
  }
  if ($ownedProcess.MainWindowHandle -eq 0) { throw 'Product did not create a window.' }
  $title = $ownedProcess.MainWindowTitle
  $runtimeModule = $ownedProcess.Modules | Where-Object ModuleName -EQ 'coreclr.dll' | Select-Object -First 1
  if (-not $runtimeModule -or -not $runtimeModule.FileName.StartsWith($packagePath + '\', [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { throw 'Product did not load its bundled runtime.' }
  $runtimeFile = $runtimeModule.FileName
  $bytes = [IO.File]::ReadAllBytes($executable)
  $peOffset = [BitConverter]::ToInt32($bytes, 60)
  $subsystem = [BitConverter]::ToUInt16($bytes, $peOffset + 24 + 68)
  if ($subsystem -ne 2) { throw 'EXE is not a Windows GUI executable.' }
  if (-not $ownedProcess.CloseMainWindow()) { throw 'Product did not accept a normal idle close.' }
  if (-not $ownedProcess.WaitForExit(10000)) { throw 'Product did not exit after closing its idle window.' }
  if ($ownedProcess.ExitCode -ne 0) { throw "Product idle close failed: $($ownedProcess.ExitCode)" }
  $report = [ordered]@{
    checkedAt = (Get-Date).ToString('o'); operatingSystem = [Environment]::OSVersion.VersionString
    packageDirectory = $installationRoot; payloadDirectory = $packagePath; cleanRootLayout = $cleanLayout; executable = $executable; title = $title; productVersion = $productVersion
    extractedFromArchive = $ArchivePath
    archiveSha256 = $archiveHash; archiveFilesMatchedPublishedDirectory = $verifiedArchiveFiles
    guiSubsystem = $subsystem; runtimeLoadedFrom = $runtimeFile; idleExitCode = $ownedProcess.ExitCode
    pathExcludedSdkAndEngine = $true; dotnetRootWasMissing = $true
    exeSha256 = (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $executable -Algorithm SHA256).Hash
    archiveEngineVersion = $archiveMetadata.version
    archiveEngineAndCorrespondingSourcesVerified = $true
    sourceArchive = $SourceArchivePath
    sourceArchiveSha256 = $(if ($SourceArchivePath) { (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $SourceArchivePath -Algorithm SHA256).Hash } else { $null })
    correspondingSourceMaterialsVerified = $null -ne $sourceBundleRoot
    petAssetsReport = $petAssetsReport
    conversionsReport = $null
    conversionsAcceptancePassed = $null
    limitations = @('This host is Windows 10; this is not a Windows 11 or clean virtual machine test.','Display scaling and physical drag/drop/keyboard acceptance require their own records.')
  }
  # Preserve structural/runtime/source evidence even when a later desktop scenario fails.
  $report | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6 | Set-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $reportDirectory 'report.json') -Encoding utf8
  if ($RunConversions) {
    # A separate self-contained acceptance executable runs the same desktop assembly and published engine.
    $env:PATH = $originalPath
    $dotnetExecutable = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\dotnet\sdk\dotnet.exe'
    $env:DOTNET_ROOT = Split-Path $dotnetExecutable -Parent
    $env:DOTNET_ROOT_X64 = $env:DOTNET_ROOT
    $env:DOTNET_CLI_HOME = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\dotnet\home'
    $env:NUGET_PACKAGES = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'tools\dotnet\packages'
    $env:DOTNET_CLI_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT = '1'
    $env:DOTNET_NOLOGO = '1'
    $acceptanceDirectory = Join-Path $reportDirectory 'runner'
    # Check the exact product DLLs, shared resources and complete paged art tree.
    # The control loads art beside its assembly, regardless of the --root switch.
    & (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'stage-package-acceptance.ps1') -PackageDirectory $installationRoot -DestinationDirectory $acceptanceDirectory | Out-Null
    $env:PATH = "$env:SystemRoot\System32;$env:SystemRoot"
    $env:DOTNET_ROOT = Join-Path $reportDirectory 'no-installed-runtime'
    $env:DOTNET_ROOT_X64 = $env:DOTNET_ROOT
    $conversionArtifacts = Join-Path $reportDirectory 'conversion-results'
    $report.conversionsReport = Join-Path $conversionArtifacts 'report.json'
    & (Join-Path $acceptanceDirectory 'CatFileAssistant.Acceptance.exe') --root $packagePath --engine-directory $engineDirectory --artifacts $conversionArtifacts
    $report.conversionsAcceptancePassed = $LASTEXITCODE -eq 0
    $report | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6 | Set-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $reportDirectory 'report.json') -Encoding utf8
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Self-contained package conversion acceptance failed.' }
  }
  $report | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6 | Set-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $reportDirectory 'report.json') -Encoding utf8
  Write-Output (Join-Path $reportDirectory 'report.json')
} finally {
  try {
    if ($ownedProcess) { try { if (-not $ownedProcess.HasExited) { $ownedProcess.Kill(); $ownedProcess.WaitForExit() } } finally { $ownedProcess.Dispose() } }
  } finally {
    $env:PATH = $originalPath
    $env:DOTNET_ROOT = $originalDotnetRoot
    $env:DOTNET_ROOT_X64 = $originalDotnetRootX64
    $env:DOTNET_CLI_HOME = $originalCliHome
    $env:NUGET_PACKAGES = $originalPackages
    $env:DOTNET_CLI_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT = $originalTelemetry
    $env:DOTNET_NOLOGO = $originalNoLogo
  }
}


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/verify-pet-assets.ps1 ---
param(
  [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$PetDirectory,
  [string]$ReferenceDirectory,
  [string]$ExportDirectory,
  [int]$ExpectedManifestVersion = 0,
  [string]$ReportPath
)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
Set-StrictMode -Version Latest

function Get-AssetRoot([string]$Path) {
  $root = (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $Path).Path.TrimEnd('\', '/')
  if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $root -PathType Container)) { throw "Pet asset directory missing: $Path" }
  return $root
}
function Get-SafeAssetPath([string]$Root, [string]$Relative) {
  if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Relative) -or [IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($Relative) -or $Relative.Contains(':') -or
      @($Relative -split '[/\\]' | Where-Object { $_ -eq '..' }).Count -ne 0) { throw "Unsafe pet asset path: $Relative" }
  $prefix = $Root + [IO.Path]::DirectorySeparatorChar
  $file = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Join-Path $Root $Relative))
  if (-not $file.StartsWith($prefix, [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { throw "Pet asset escaped its directory: $Relative" }
  $cursor = $file
  while ($cursor.Length -ge $Root.Length) {
    $item = Get-Item -LiteralPath $cursor -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    if ($null -ne $item -and ($item.Attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -ne 0) { throw "Pet asset traverses a reparse point: $Relative" }
    if ($cursor -eq $Root) { break }
    $cursor = Split-Path $cursor -Parent
  }
  if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $file -PathType Leaf)) { throw "Referenced pet asset is missing: $Relative" }
  return $file
}
function Get-PngSize([string]$Path) {
  $stream = [IO.File]::OpenRead($Path)
  try {
    $header = New-Object byte[] 24
    if ($stream.Read($header, 0, 24) -ne 24 -or [BitConverter]::ToString($header, 0, 8) -ne '89-50-4E-47-0D-0A-1A-0A' -or
        [Text.Encoding]::ASCII.GetString($header, 12, 4) -cne 'IHDR') { throw "Pet page is not a PNG: $Path" }
    $width = [int]$header[16] * 16777216 + [int]$header[17] * 65536 + [int]$header[18] * 256 + [int]$header[19]
    $height = [int]$header[20] * 16777216 + [int]$header[21] * 65536 + [int]$header[22] * 256 + [int]$header[23]
    if ($width -le 0 -or $height -le 0) { throw "Invalid pet PNG dimensions: $Path" }
    return @($width, $height)
  } finally { $stream.Dispose() }
}
function Get-FrameReferences($Node) {
  if ($null -eq $Node -or $Node -is [string] -or $Node -is [ValueType]) { return }
  if ($Node -is [Array]) { foreach ($child in $Node) { Get-FrameReferences $child }; return }
  if ($null -ne $Node.PSObject.Properties['atlas'] -and $null -ne $Node.PSObject.Properties['cell']) { Write-Output $Node }
  foreach ($property in $Node.PSObject.Properties) { Get-FrameReferences $property.Value }
}
function Get-AssetInventory([string]$Root) {
  $inventory = [ordered]@{}
  foreach ($entry in (Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $Root -Recurse -Force | Sort-Object FullName)) {
    if (($entry.Attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -ne 0) { throw "Pet asset tree contains a reparse point: $($entry.FullName)" }
    if (-not $entry.PSIsContainer) {
      $relative = $entry.FullName.Substring($Root.Length + 1).Replace('\', '/')
      $null = Get-SafeAssetPath $Root $relative
      $inventory[$relative] = (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $entry.FullName -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant()
    }
  }
  return $inventory
}
function Assert-MatchingAssets([string]$Root, $Expected, [string]$Label) {
  $actual = Get-AssetInventory $Root
  if ($Expected.Count -ne $actual.Count) { throw "$Label pet asset file count differs ($($Expected.Count) versus $($actual.Count))." }
  foreach ($relative in $Expected.Keys) {
    if (-not $actual.Contains($relative) -or $actual[$relative] -cne $Expected[$relative]) { throw "$Label pet asset differs: $relative" }
  }
}

$petRoot = Get-AssetRoot $PetDirectory
$manifestPath = Get-SafeAssetPath $petRoot 'animations.json'
$manifest = Get-Content -LiteralPath $manifestPath -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
if ($ExpectedManifestVersion -ne 0 -and $manifest.version -ne $ExpectedManifestVersion) { throw "Unexpected pet manifest version: $($manifest.version)" }
if ($manifest.version -notin @(2, 3)) { throw "Unsupported pet manifest version: $($manifest.version)" }
$atlasFiles = [Collections.Generic.HashSet[string]]::new([StringComparer]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)
$pages = @()
foreach ($atlas in $manifest.atlases) {
  if (-not $atlasFiles.Add($atlas.file) -or $atlas.columns -lt 1 -or $atlas.rows -lt 1) { throw 'Pet atlas names must be unique and grids must be positive.' }
  $pagePath = Get-SafeAssetPath $petRoot $atlas.file
  $size = Get-PngSize $pagePath
  if ($null -ne $atlas.PSObject.Properties['sourceSize'] -and ($atlas.sourceSize.Count -ne 2 -or $atlas.sourceSize[0] -ne $size[0] -or $atlas.sourceSize[1] -ne $size[1])) { throw "Pet atlas dimensions differ from metadata: $($atlas.file)" }
  $pages += [pscustomobject]@{ file=$atlas.file; width=$size[0]; height=$size[1]; sha256=(Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $pagePath -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant() }
}
if ($pages.Count -eq 0) { throw 'Pet manifest does not contain any atlas pages.' }
$actions = @($manifest.animations.PSObject.Properties)
foreach ($required in @('idle','blink','groom','stretch','sleep','walk','happy','feed','play','work','success','error','drag','play-wand','feed-canned','held','sit')) {
  if ($required -notin $actions.Name) { throw "Pet action is missing: $required" }
}
$segmentedFrameCount = 0
foreach ($action in $actions) {
  $value = $action.Value
  if ($null -ne $value.PSObject.Properties['atlas']) {
    if ($value.atlas -lt 0 -or $value.atlas -ge $pages.Count -or $value.row -lt 0 -or $value.row -ge $manifest.atlases[$value.atlas].rows -or $value.frames -lt 1) { throw "Invalid legacy pet action: $($action.Name)" }
  }
  $frames = @(Get-FrameReferences $value)
  foreach ($frame in $frames) {
    if ($frame.atlas -lt 0 -or $frame.atlas -ge $pages.Count -or $frame.cell -lt 0 -or $frame.cell -ge $manifest.atlases[$frame.atlas].columns * $manifest.atlases[$frame.atlas].rows) { throw "Invalid paged pet frame in action: $($action.Name)" }
    if ($null -ne $frame.PSObject.Properties['ms'] -and $frame.ms -le 0) { throw "Nonpositive pet frame duration: $($action.Name)" }
    $segmentedFrameCount++
  }
  if ($null -eq $value.PSObject.Properties['atlas'] -and $frames.Count -eq 0) { throw "Pet action has no usable frames: $($action.Name)" }
}
$inventory = Get-AssetInventory $petRoot
if ($ReferenceDirectory) { Assert-MatchingAssets (Get-AssetRoot $ReferenceDirectory) $inventory 'Reference' }
if ($ExportDirectory) { Assert-MatchingAssets (Get-AssetRoot $ExportDirectory) $inventory 'Design export' }
$report = [ordered]@{ checkedAt=[DateTimeOffset]::Now.ToString('o'); directory=$petRoot; manifestVersion=$manifest.version;
  manifestSha256=$inventory['animations.json']; actionCount=$actions.Count; segmentedFrameCount=$segmentedFrameCount;
  pages=$pages; fileCount=$inventory.Count; files=$inventory; reference=$ReferenceDirectory; designExport=$ExportDirectory;
  pathSafety='Relative paths, no parent traversal, no reparse points';
  scope='Encoded-page existence, dimensions, indices and exact complete-tree hashes. This is not a visual anatomy or continuity approval.' }
if ($ReportPath) { $report | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 8 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $ReportPath -Encoding utf8 }
[pscustomobject]@{ manifestVersion=$manifest.version; actions=$actions.Count; pages=$pages.Count; segmentedFrames=$segmentedFrameCount; files=$inventory.Count; report=$ReportPath }


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/verify-release-preservation.ps1 ---
param(
  [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$SnapshotPath,
  [string]$ReleaseDirectory,
  [string]$ReportPath
)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$projectDirectory = Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent
if (-not $ReleaseDirectory) { $ReleaseDirectory = Join-Path $projectDirectory 'release' }
$releaseRoot = (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $ReleaseDirectory).Path.TrimEnd('\')
$snapshot = @(Get-Content -LiteralPath $SnapshotPath -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json)
if ($snapshot.Count -eq 0) { throw 'Release preservation snapshot is empty.' }
$checks = @()
foreach ($entry in $snapshot) {
  if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($entry.name) -or [IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($entry.name) -or $entry.name.Contains(':') -or @($entry.name -split '[/\\]' | Where-Object { $_ -eq '..' }).Count -ne 0) { throw 'Unsafe name in preservation snapshot.' }
  $file = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Join-Path $releaseRoot $entry.name))
  if (-not $file.StartsWith($releaseRoot + '\', [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { throw 'Preservation path escaped release directory.' }
  $exists = Test-Path -LiteralPath $file -PathType Leaf
  $actualLength = if ($exists) { (Get-Item -LiteralPath $file).Length } else { $null }
  $actualHash = if ($exists) { (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $file -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant() } else { $null }
  $checks += [pscustomobject]@{ name=$entry.name; expectedLength=$entry.length; actualLength=$actualLength; expectedSha256=$entry.sha256; actualSha256=$actualHash;
    passed=$exists -and $actualLength -eq $entry.length -and $actualHash -ieq $entry.sha256 }
}
$report = [ordered]@{ checkedAt=[DateTimeOffset]::Now.ToString('o'); snapshot=(Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $SnapshotPath).Path; releaseDirectory=$releaseRoot;
  success=@($checks | Where-Object { -not $_.passed }).Count -eq 0; checks=$checks; scope='Only the files explicitly recorded in the before snapshot; no release files were written.' }
if ($ReportPath) { $report | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 5 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $ReportPath -Encoding utf8 }
if (-not $report.success) { throw 'A previously released file is missing or changed; inspect the preservation report.' }
[pscustomobject]@{ preservedFiles=$checks.Count; report=$ReportPath }


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/verify-source-archive.py ---
"""Validate every required corresponding-source archive directly from the delivered ZIP."""
import hashlib, json, pathlib, sys, zipfile, xml.etree.ElementTree as ET

archive = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1]).resolve()
report_path = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[2]).resolve()
project_path = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / 'src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/CatFileAssistant.Desktop.csproj'
expected_version = sys.argv[3] if len(sys.argv) > 3 else ET.parse(project_path).findtext('.//Version')
with zipfile.ZipFile(archive) as package:
    names = package.namelist()
    if len(names) != len(set(names)):
        raise ValueError('Duplicate source ZIP entries')
    for name in names:
        path = pathlib.PurePosixPath(name)
        if path.is_absolute() or '..' in path.parts:
            raise ValueError('Unsafe source ZIP entry: '+name)
    manifests = [name for name in names if name.endswith('/tools/ffmpeg/source/distribution-manifest.json')]
    if len(manifests) != 1:
        raise ValueError('Expected one corresponding-source manifest')
    manifest = json.loads(package.read(manifests[0]))
    if not manifest['sourceBundleCompleteForReviewedInventory'] or manifest['distributionLicense'] != 'GPL-3.0-or-later':
        raise ValueError('Source inventory was not reviewed for distribution')
    source_prefix = manifests[0].removesuffix('distribution-manifest.json')
    verified_bytes = 0
    for entry in manifest['requiredFiles']:
        name = source_prefix+entry['path'].replace('\\','/')
        digest = hashlib.sha256()
        with package.open(name) as source:
            while chunk := source.read(1024*1024):
                verified_bytes += len(chunk)
                digest.update(chunk)
        if digest.hexdigest().lower() != entry['sha256'].lower():
            raise ValueError('Source ZIP hash mismatch: '+entry['path'])
    license_name = source_prefix.removesuffix('source/')+'licenses/COPYING.GPLv3'
    if license_name not in names:
        raise ValueError('Source ZIP does not include GPL 3 text')
    application_manifests = [name for name in names if name.endswith('/application-source-manifest.json')]
    if len(application_manifests) != 1:
        raise ValueError('Expected one matching application source manifest')
    application_manifest = json.loads(package.read(application_manifests[0]))
    if application_manifest['applicationVersion'] != expected_version:
        raise ValueError('Unexpected application source version')
    application_prefix = application_manifests[0].removesuffix('application-source-manifest.json')
    packaged_project = ET.fromstring(package.read(application_prefix+'src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/CatFileAssistant.Desktop.csproj'))
    if packaged_project.findtext('.//Version') != expected_version:
        raise ValueError('Packaged project version does not match application manifest')
    for entry in application_manifest['files']:
        digest = hashlib.sha256(package.read(application_prefix+entry['path'])).hexdigest()
        if digest.lower() != entry['sha256'].lower():
            raise ValueError('Application source ZIP hash mismatch: '+entry['path'])
    for required in ('src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/App.xaml.cs', 'src/CatFileAssistant.Desktop/Assets/Pet/animations.json',
                     'src/CatFileAssistant.Infrastructure/PetStateStore.cs', 'tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/PetIntegrationAcceptance.cs',
                     'tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/PetAnimationAcceptance.cs', 'tests/CatFileAssistant.Acceptance/PetPerformanceAcceptance.cs'):
        if application_prefix+required not in names:
            raise ValueError('Application source missing: '+required)
report = dict(archive=str(archive), success=True, requiredFilesVerified=len(manifest['requiredFiles']),
              sourceBytesVerified=verified_bytes, totalZipEntries=len(names),
              distributionLicense=manifest['distributionLicense'], note='Checks delivered source bytes and manifest; does not certify patent or legal status.')
report['applicationVersion'] = application_manifest['applicationVersion']
report['applicationFilesVerified'] = len(application_manifest['files'])
report_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
report_path.write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
print(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False))


--- ORIGINAL FILE: scripts/write-release-checksums.ps1 ---
param([string[]]$AdditionalFiles, [string]$Version)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$projectDirectory = Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent
. (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'release-version.ps1')
$releaseInfo = Get-CatReleaseInfo -ProjectDirectory $projectDirectory -Version $Version
$releaseRoot = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Join-Path $projectDirectory 'release')).TrimEnd('\')
$files = @(
  (Join-Path $releaseRoot ($releaseInfo.PackageName + '-Setup.exe')),
  (Join-Path $releaseRoot ($releaseInfo.PackageName + '.zip')),
  (Join-Path $releaseRoot ($releaseInfo.PackageName + '-Sources.zip'))
)
foreach ($additional in $AdditionalFiles) {
  $file = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($additional)
  if (-not $file.StartsWith($releaseRoot + '\', [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { throw 'Additional checksum files must remain inside release.' }
  $files += $file
}
$lines = @()
foreach ($file in $files) {
  if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $file -PathType Leaf)) { throw "Cannot finish release checksums; delivery file is missing: $file" }
  $relative = $file.Substring($releaseRoot.Length + 1).Replace('\', '/')
  $lines += (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $file -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant() + '  ' + $relative
}
$utf8 = [Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false)
for ($index = 0; $index -lt $files.Count; $index++) {
  [IO.File]::WriteAllText($files[$index] + '.sha256', $lines[$index] + [Environment]::NewLine, $utf8)
}
$combined = Join-Path $releaseRoot ('你的猫咪文件助手-' + $releaseInfo.Label + '-SHA256.txt')
[IO.File]::WriteAllText($combined, ($lines -join [Environment]::NewLine) + [Environment]::NewLine, $utf8)
Get-Item -LiteralPath $combined | Select-Object FullName,Length
